## Urban Studies

`U1` **Each building tells a story: A New York City neighborhood on the cusp of densification**  
**每栋建筑都在讲述故事：处于高密度化临界点的纽约市社区**  
*Mara Sidney, Tamara Velasquez Leiferman, Joshua McWhirter*｜2026-11-17｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261500283](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500283)

> **Abstract:** This study seeks to understand the business of densification through analysis of public policies, property records, and “the everyday” in a rapidly changing Manhattan neighborhood. Our case study of Inwood uses a new conceptual framework for urban policy analysis to bring multiple data sources together to examine four sites that show how key elements of New York City’s rezoning strategy intersects with other housing policies. Our findings speak to the challenges of maintaining affordable housing stock amid densification and the importance of including everyday experiences in policy analysis. We find that new densification policies interact with pre-existing policies to produce complex, contradictory outcomes. For example, de-densification coexists with densification. Second, although some manifestations of densification are visible, such as high-rise buildings, others are not, including tenant displacement and apartment warehousing. Finally, we find that policy implementation works as ad hoc policy making because actors seek policy changes through alternative channels after rezoning is formally adopted.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究通过分析一个快速变化的曼哈顿社区中的公共政策、房产记录和“日常生活”，试图理解高密度化的运作机制。我们对因伍德的案例研究采用一种新的城市政策分析概念框架，整合多种数据来源，考察四个地点，以展示纽约市重新区划战略的关键要素如何与其他住房政策相交织。我们的研究发现揭示了在高密度化进程中维持可负担住房存量所面临的挑战，以及将日常经验纳入政策分析的重要性。我们发现，新的高密度化政策与既有政策相互作用，产生复杂而矛盾的结果。例如，去密度化与高密度化同时存在。其次，尽管高密度化的某些表现是可见的，如高层建筑，但其他表现则不可见，包括租户迁离和公寓闲置囤积。最后，我们发现政策实施实际上作为一种临时性的政策制定在运作，因为在重新区划正式通过后，行动者会通过其他渠道寻求政策变更。


`P1` **The rubble and beyond: Expanding urbicide through affective geographies and the settler-colonial longue durée**  
**瓦砾及其之外：通过情感地理与定居者殖民的长时段拓展“城市灭绝”概念**  
*Ohoud Kamal, Yamen N. Al-Betawi*｜2026-11-17｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261503755](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503755)

> **Abstract:** Urbicide scholarship has established, in varying ways, that ‘killing a city’ reaches beyond the material. Yet the concepts used to name this ‘reach’ do not describe the same object or make the same analytical claim. The question is therefore not only whether urbicide reaches beyond the material, but what a given conceptualisation allows us to understand about what destruction reaches. This article expands urbicide scholarship by theorising affect not as an additional dimension or effect of destruction, but as a capacity enabled by material and human–nonhuman relations through which violence can be reached. The argument is developed through a situated reading of contemporary urbicidal violence in Gaza (2023–ongoing), where recurrent military operations have compounded destruction while foreclosing reconstruction between them, within a longer settler-colonial history of displacement and enclosure. Drawing on Anderson’s account of morale and affective geographies literature, the article shows how destruction reaches the material and relational conditions through which affective capacities are constituted and exercised. It further proposes storytelling and counter-mapping as situated forms of evidence for tracing the reach of this destruction: memories, attachments, spatial knowledge and relations that conventional damage assessment cannot capture. Locating these processes within the settler-colonial longue durée expands the concept of urbicide showing how its reach is shaped by historically specific conditions of enclosure, cumulative destruction and the absence of a stable aftermath.
>
> **摘要：** 城市灭绝（urbicide）研究已以不同方式确立了“杀死一座城市”的影响超越物质层面这一观点。然而，用以命名这种“触及”的概念并不描述同一对象，也不作出相同的分析主张。因此，问题不仅在于城市灭绝是否超越物质层面，还在于某一特定概念化能让我们理解破坏究竟触及了什么。本文通过将情感理论化——不是作为破坏的附加维度或效应，而是作为一种由物质关系及人类—非人类关系所促成、暴力可借以触及的能力——来拓展城市灭绝研究。论证通过对加沙当代城市灭绝暴力（2023年至今）的情境化解读展开：在那里，反复的军事行动在加剧破坏的同时也阻断了其间的重建，而这一切处于更长的定居者殖民主义驱逐与围困历史之中。借鉴安德森（Anderson）关于士气的论述以及情感地理学文献，本文展示了破坏如何触及情感能力得以构成和施展的物质与关系条件。本文进一步提出将讲故事与反制图作为追踪这种破坏所及范围的情境化证据形式：即常规损害评估无法捕捉的记忆、依恋、空间知识与关系。将这些过程置于定居者殖民的长时段之中，拓展了城市灭绝的概念，表明其所及范围如何受到围困、累积性破坏以及缺乏稳定善后这些历史特定条件的塑造。


`U2` **Wealth, homeownership, and land use regulatory changes**  
**财富、住房自有与土地利用管制变化**  
*Diego Gil, Hugo E. Silva, Kenzo Asahi*｜2026-11-11｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498609](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498609)

> **Abstract:** This paper examines patterns of land use regulatory changes in Santiago, Chile, focusing on downzoning (stricter regulations) and upzoning (more flexible regulations), and their associations with sociodemographic factors. We analyze an original georeferenced dataset covering all 34 urban municipalities of Santiago over 20 years, together with 19 semi-structured interviews with key actors. The quantitative analysis suggests that high-income neighborhoods are associated with a greater likelihood of downzoning when homeownership is comparatively low. With our block-level measure of wealth, however, this association is not statistically distinguishable from zero at the sample-average homeownership rate and becomes negative at higher rates. Therefore, in affluent neighborhoods, the significant presence of homeowners is associated with less probability of downzoning. Outside high-income neighborhoods, higher homeownership is instead associated with downzoning. Qualitative evidence points to the growing role of organized residents, with socially connected groups using participatory reforms and informal ties to municipal decision-makers to shape local regulatory debates. This positive association is absent when the analysis is restricted to large-scale zoning changes. The findings contribute to debates between the “growth machine” and “homevoter” theories by suggesting that the relevance of each framework may vary across patterns of social segmentation and local demographic structures.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察智利圣地亚哥土地利用管制变化的模式，聚焦下调分区（更严格的管制）和上调分区（更灵活的管制），以及它们与社会人口因素的关联。我们分析了一个覆盖圣地亚哥全部34个城市自治市、时间跨度20年的原创地理参照数据集，并辅以对关键行动者的19次半结构化访谈。定量分析表明，当住房自有率相对较低时，高收入社区与更高的下调分区可能性相关。然而，使用我们在街区层面的财富测度，在样本平均住房自有率下，这一关联在统计上与零无显著差异，而在更高的住房自有率下则转为负向。因此，在富裕社区，大量住房所有者的存在与较低的下调分区概率相关。而在高收入社区之外，更高的住房自有率则与下调分区相关。定性证据表明有组织居民的作用日益增强，社会关系紧密的群体利用参与式改革以及与市政决策者的非正式联系来影响地方管制讨论。当分析仅限于大规模区划变化时，这一正向关联并不存在。研究发现通过表明每种框架的适用性可能随社会分割模式和地方人口结构而异，为“增长机器”理论与“房主选民”（homevoter）理论之间的争论作出了贡献。


`E1` **Planning for circular urban metabolism: Intertwining skills and spatial transformation for manufacturing transition in Padua and Venice, Italy**  
**面向循环城市代谢的规划：意大利帕多瓦与威尼斯制造业转型中技能与空间转型的交织**  
*Claudia Faraone, Francesca Gambarotto, Luca Nicoletto, Giulio Pedrini*｜2026-11-11｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261499471](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261499471)

> **Abstract:** The shift from a linear to a circular economic structure needs a new urban metabolism that minimizes waste and the use of raw materials and energy. However, a city’s potential for circularity is contingent upon its manufacturing path-dependency, both from an economic and spatial perspective. The research looks at two Italian industrial areas—Padua and Venice—and proceeds to investigate the recent dynamics of their manufacturing spatial distribution at the “transect” level scale. Using a mixed-method approach that combines morphological-spatial analysis, urban planning assessment, and quantitative labor and waste data, the study investigates how manufacturing industries contribute to circular transitions and how planning policies can support or hinder this process. Findings show that technological manufacturing, supported by technical skills and green jobs, drives circular performance through waste prevention and recycling. Industrial diversification and firm relatedness further enable symbiotic material flows. However, both cities reveal a persistent misalignment between emerging circular dynamics and existing zoning frameworks. While Padua has begun adopting mixed-use zoning, planning strategies in both areas remain fragmented and reactive. The paper calls for more integrated, circularity-oriented spatial planning that mobilizes skills and proximity as key territorial resources.
>
> **摘要：** 从线性经济结构向循环经济结构的转变需要一种新的城市代谢，以最大限度地减少废弃物以及原材料和能源的使用。然而，一座城市的循环潜力取决于其制造业在经济和空间两方面的路径依赖。本研究考察意大利的两个工业地区——帕多瓦和威尼斯——并在“样带”（transect）尺度上探究其制造业空间分布的近期动态。研究采用混合方法，结合形态—空间分析、城市规划评估以及劳动力和废弃物的定量数据，探讨制造业如何促进循环转型，以及规划政策如何支持或阻碍这一进程。研究发现，在技术技能和绿色就业支撑下的技术型制造业，通过废弃物预防和回收利用推动循环绩效。产业多样化和企业关联性进一步促成了共生性物质流动。然而，两座城市都显示出新兴循环动态与现行分区框架之间持续存在错位。虽然帕多瓦已开始采用混合用途分区，但两地的规划策略仍然碎片化且被动应对。本文呼吁开展更具整合性、以循环为导向的空间规划，将技能和邻近性作为关键的地域资源加以调动。


`S3` **Activity spaces, community resource brokers, and real-time stress and affect: Evidence from the Boston Activity Space and Health Study**  
**活动空间、社区资源中介与实时压力和情绪：来自波士顿活动空间与健康研究的证据**  
*Gregory Sharp, Emily Walton*｜2026-11-11｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261500260](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500260)

> **Abstract:** While we know that the places where we live matter for our health and well-being, other spaces in the city we visit during the day—like places where we work, worship, exercise, and buy groceries—also matter. Little work, however, has explored how daily experiences with these routine community institutions and organizations shape our emotional responses. This study uses data from the Boston Activity Space and Health Study (BASHS), a smartphone-based data collection of adults’ GPS locations and ecological momentary assessments, to capture in-the-moment responses to their daily activity space environments for 7 days. We use random effects models to estimate associations between resource broker access and real-time accounts of stress and affect, and whether these relationships are conditional on the utilization of the resource brokers. Our findings suggest that beyond having regular access to community resource brokers, utilizing them on a regular basis is impactful for alleviating momentary emotional and physiological distress. By detailing the unique role of resource brokers, the current study makes substantive and methodological contributions to our understanding of how activity spaces matter for health and well-being.
>
> **摘要：** 我们知道，居住地点对健康和福祉至关重要，但我们白天在城市中造访的其他空间——如工作、礼拜、锻炼和购买食品杂货的场所——同样重要。然而，鲜有研究探讨与这些日常社区机构和组织的日常经历如何塑造我们的情绪反应。本研究利用波士顿活动空间与健康研究（BASHS）的数据——这是一项基于智能手机、收集成年人GPS位置和生态瞬时评估的数据——捕捉其在7天内对日常活动空间环境的即时反应。我们使用随机效应模型估计资源中介可及性与压力和情绪的实时报告之间的关联，以及这些关系是否取决于对资源中介的利用。研究结果表明，除了能够经常接触社区资源中介之外，定期利用这些中介对缓解即时的情绪与生理困扰具有重要作用。通过详细阐明资源中介的独特作用，本研究对理解活动空间如何影响健康和福祉作出了实质性和方法论上的贡献。


`U2` **Limits on growth: Governing urban futures in Beijing**  
**增长的限度：北京的城市未来治理**  
*Yiqiu Liu*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261501380](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261501380)

> **Abstract:** This paper conceptualises limits on growth in Beijing, whose 2016–2035 master plan constructs a highly selective national capital for the central state by removing functions and populations deemed non-essential. Drawing on archival analysis of planning documents since 1949, extensive expert and ethnographic interviews, and multi-sited fieldwork conducted between 2017 and 2023, it develops a Lefebvrian spatio-temporal framework with three relational dimensions for analysing urban future-making. Temporal entanglements show how a historically sedimented anti-metropolis disposition is reactivated amid contemporary state recentralisation. Performative governance examines how the diagnosis of ‘big city disease’ reframes demographic and functional pressures as urban pathology, legitimising relocation and greening as therapeutic intervention. Lived futurity reveals how officials, villagers and migrants negotiate these measures through procrastination, speculation and retreat. The article theorises limits on growth as an emergent mode of Chinese statecraft in which extra-economic priorities selectively guide the production of the urban future. Situating Beijing within a wider, politically heterogeneous turn that increasingly treats demographic and territorial growth as a crisis, it reconceives urban future-making as a politics of foreclosure: not only who profits from growth, but who is permitted to remain when growth is limited by design.
>
> **摘要：** 本文对北京的“增长限度”进行概念化。北京2016–2035年城市总体规划通过疏解被视为非必要的功能与人口，为中央国家建构一个高度选择性的国家首都。基于对1949年以来规划文件的档案分析、大量专家访谈与民族志访谈，以及2017年至2023年间开展的多地点田野调查，本文构建了一个包含三个关系维度的列斐伏尔式时空框架，用以分析城市未来的塑造。时间纠缠揭示了一种历史沉积下来的反大都市倾向如何在当代国家再集权中被重新激活。表演性治理考察了“大城市病”的诊断如何将人口与功能压力重构为城市病理，从而使疏解搬迁与绿化作为治疗性干预获得正当性。生活中的未来性揭示了官员、村民和流动人口如何通过拖延、投机和退却来应对这些措施。本文将增长限度理论化为中国治国术的一种新兴模式，在其中超经济优先事项选择性地引导城市未来的生产。通过将北京置于一个更广泛的、在政治上具有异质性的转向之中——这一转向日益将人口与地域增长视为危机——本文将城市未来的塑造重新理解为一种排除的政治：问题不仅在于谁从增长中获利，更在于当增长被刻意限制时谁被允许留下。


`S1` **The gendered right to the city: Mediated access, urban agriculture and women’s empowerment in Kumasi**  
**性别化的城市权利：中介化获取、城市农业与库马西的女性赋权**  
*Mary Duah, John Boulard Forkuor, David Forkuor, Eric Agyemang et al.*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261503405](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503405)

> **Abstract:** Urban space is a vital resource for city dwellers. While literature examines the use of formal public spaces by urban residents for commerce and social activities, scholarly attention to informal agricultural spaces remains limited. This study uses a synthesis of Kabeer’s empowerment framework and Lefebvre’s right to the city to analyse women’s access to and use of urban space for agriculture in Ghana as a transformative space where gender, body and empowerment intersect. Our findings reveal that male networks serve as gatekeepers through whom women access urban space for urban agriculture. While women’s access to this social capital is empowering, it indicates that women’s right to the city and to spatial justice is mediated, achieved through relational networks and experienced as a relational right. Our findings further reveal that women appropriate these urban spaces in ways that enhance their general well-being, bodily sovereignty and promote intergenerational rights to the city for their children. By demonstrating how women’s spatial access enables agency, bodily sovereignty and the pursuit of intergenerational rights, we argue for a more nuanced understanding of empowerment and rights within informal urban contexts in the Global South.
>
> **摘要：** 城市空间是城市居民的重要资源。尽管已有文献考察城市居民对正式公共空间的商业与社交使用，但学界对非正规农业空间的关注仍然有限。本研究综合Kabeer的赋权框架与列斐伏尔的城市权利理论，分析加纳女性为农业目的而获取和使用城市空间的情况，将其视为性别、身体与赋权交汇的变革性空间。研究发现，男性网络充当把关者，女性须经由他们获取用于城市农业的城市空间。尽管女性获得这种社会资本具有赋权作用，但这表明女性的城市权利与空间正义是中介化的，是通过关系网络实现的，并作为一种关系性权利被体验。研究还发现，女性以增进自身整体福祉、身体主权并促进其子女的代际城市权利的方式占用这些城市空间。通过展示女性的空间获取如何促成能动性、身体主权以及对代际权利的追求，我们主张对全球南方非正规城市情境中的赋权与权利作出更细致的理解。


`S2` **The simultaneous slow death and persistent vitality of social infrastructure in elite neighbourhoods**  
**精英街区社会基础设施的同时缓慢消亡与持久活力**  
*Sharda Rozena, Rowland Atkinson*｜2026-11-04｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261499481](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261499481)

> **Abstract:** This article examines the vitality of social life at street level over time in the patrician elite area of Kensington, London. We consider how luxury amenities, prime homes and high-end stores intersect with the durable presence of an everyday, working city comprising diverse social infrastructures: budget hotels, rent-controlled housing and interstitial social spaces of cafes, parks and street benches. These layers of uses and users co-exist in complex time–space configurations despite the historical and contemporary designation of the neighbourhood as an elite space. Using a range of data sources, we show how the neighbourhood contains distinct features that enable the limited and partial social engagement and hypermobility of wealthy bodies with the social life of the area. However, we also find a diverse social history, culture and complex grounded experiences at street level involving a wide range of residents and other users. These observations demonstrate how, even in the most elite of urban neighbourhoods, spaces remain socially complex and diverse, despite ongoing tensions.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察伦敦肯辛顿这一贵族精英地区街道层面社会生活的历时活力。我们考虑了奢华设施、顶级住宅和高端商店如何与一个由多样化社会基础设施构成的日常工作城市的持久存在相交织：这些社会基础设施包括经济型酒店、租金管制住房，以及咖啡馆、公园和街边长椅等间隙性社会空间。尽管该街区在历史上和当代都被定位为精英空间，这些不同层次的用途和使用者仍以复杂的时空构型共存。利用多种数据来源，我们展示了该街区具有一些独特特征，使富裕群体得以与当地社会生活进行有限而局部的接触，并保持高度流动性。然而，我们也发现了街道层面多样化的社会历史、文化和复杂的在地经验，涉及广泛的居民和其他使用者。这些观察表明，即使在最精英的城市街区，空间依然在社会层面复杂而多元，尽管紧张关系持续存在。


`M1` **Understanding activity spaces: Contemporary issues and challenges and future research directions**  
**理解活动空间：当代议题、挑战与未来研究方向**  
*Renee Zahnow, Jonathan Corcoran, Rebecca Wickes*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261492885](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261492885)

> **Abstract:** Activity spaces are a subset of locations where people spend their everyday lives. Interest in activity spaces spans the social sciences, geography, health sciences and transportation research. In recent years, the spread of smartphones and other tracking devices has made it far easier to record daily movements. This has sparked an increase in activity space research and an opportunity to consider how the concept has been conceptually and empirically defined over time and across disciplinary fields. In this scoping review, we analyse 372 studies of activity spaces published between 1972 and 2024. Despite conceptual consistency, we find that studies tend to focus, empirically, on one of four dimensions of activity spaces: access, nodes, paths and exposure. Drawing on our review, we suggest greater methodological harmonisation may better support replication and comparative studies and incremental research discovery. We propose that to achieve this, a more nuanced theoretical framework is required that defines each of the dimensions of activity space, how they are related to each other and incorporates aspects of daily activity in the new millennia (e.g. online, hybrid and multilayered time–space locations).
>
> **摘要：** 活动空间是人们度过日常生活的地点的一个子集。对活动空间的关注横跨社会科学、地理学、健康科学和交通研究。近年来，智能手机及其他追踪设备的普及使记录日常移动变得容易得多。这引发了活动空间研究的增长，也为思考该概念如何随时间推移并在不同学科领域中被概念性和经验性地界定提供了契机。在这篇范围综述中，我们分析了1972年至2024年间发表的372项活动空间研究。尽管概念上具有一致性，我们发现这些研究在经验层面往往聚焦于活动空间四个维度中的一个：可达性、节点、路径和暴露。基于综述，我们建议更大程度的方法统一可能更有助于支持重复研究、比较研究以及渐进式的研究发现。我们提出，为实现这一目标，需要一个更为细致的理论框架，界定活动空间的各个维度及其相互关系，并纳入新千年日常活动的各个方面（如线上的、混合的以及多层次的时空位置）。


`S3` **Bridging critical knowledge and action gaps in urban health: A call for transdisciplinary approaches in urban studies in the era of global population aging and climate change**  
**弥合城市健康领域关键的知识与行动鸿沟：在全球人口老龄化与气候变化时代呼吁城市研究采取跨学科方法**  
*Vitor Pessoa Colombo, Elis Mina Seraya Borde, Mirko S. Winkler, Aline Dayrell Ferreira Sales et al.*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498090](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498090)

> **Abstract:** Urbanization and population aging are two “mega-trends” posing significant challenges to global health, particularly under climate change. Despite extensive evidence on the links between the built environment and health, urban planning scholars and practitioners still miss valuable opportunities to contribute to public health and urban health equity—which often remain marginal or only implicit topics in urban planning discourses and practices. Against this background, we organized the transdisciplinary workshop “Global Health Challenges Related to Demographic & Climate Changes: Implications for Urban and Territorial Planning” (Basel, 2024), convening scholars and practitioners to co-develop a research agenda addressing knowledge-to-action gaps in urban health equity and climate resilience. Building on the workshop’s discussions, this commentary highlights current challenges in reconnecting urban planning and public health while presenting opportunities to bridge this gap. We argue for an “ultra-local” approach that acknowledges the chronic lack of granular data necessary to address spatial justice and urban health inequities, emphasizing the importance of evidence- and place-based planning. To illustrate this approach, we present two recent experiences: the BH-Viva project in Brazil, which assessed impact of slum-upgrading projects on urban health equity, and the Saint-Louis Master Plan in Senegal, a pioneering effort integrating climate resilience into urban policy and planning. Ultimately, we present what we identified as priority research paths in urban studies, having transdisciplinarity as a precondition for urban planners to effectively tackle pressing global health challenges.
>
> **摘要：** 城市化与人口老龄化是对全球健康构成重大挑战的两大“超级趋势”，在气候变化背景下尤为如此。尽管关于建成环境与健康之间联系的证据十分丰富，城市规划学者和从业者仍错失了为公共健康和城市健康公平作出贡献的宝贵机会——这些议题在城市规划的话语与实践中往往仍处于边缘或仅是隐含的。在此背景下，我们组织了跨学科研讨会“与人口和气候变化相关的全球健康挑战：对城市与国土规划的启示”（巴塞尔，2024年），召集学者与从业者共同制定研究议程，以应对城市健康公平与气候韧性方面的知识—行动鸿沟。基于研讨会的讨论，本评论强调了重新连接城市规划与公共健康所面临的当前挑战，并提出弥合这一鸿沟的机遇。我们主张采取一种“超地方”方法，承认应对空间正义和城市健康不平等所需的精细数据长期匮乏，并强调基于证据和基于地方的规划的重要性。为阐明这一方法，我们介绍了两个近期案例：巴西的BH-Viva项目，该项目评估了贫民窟改造项目对城市健康公平的影响；以及塞内加尔圣路易总体规划，这是将气候韧性纳入城市政策与规划的开创性尝试。最后，我们提出了城市研究中的优先研究方向，并将跨学科性视为城市规划者有效应对紧迫全球健康挑战的前提条件。


`U2` **Internationalization of progressive municipalism—Barcelona international relations strategies**  
**进步市政主义的国际化——巴塞罗那的国际关系战略**  
*Agnieszka Szpak*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498132](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498132)

> **Abstract:** This article examines how the International Relations Master Plan 2020–2023 and the International Strategy 2025–2029 translate progressive municipalism into external action. Through qualitative content analysis and comparison, it identifies three mechanisms of internationalization: norm projection, networked amplification, and multilevel resource mobilization. Both Strategies promote a rights-based agenda centered on social justice, housing, democratic participation, digital rights, and ecological transition. However, the movement-oriented municipalism of the Colau administration has evolved under Collboni into a more institutionalized and partnership-based model. This shift demonstrates both the continuity and moderation of Barcelona’s progressive orientation. The article also highlights the limits of municipal internationalization: although the Strategies support agenda-setting, policy circulation, coalition-building, and access to resources, they neither confer formal international authority on the city nor guarantee implementation. Barcelona, therefore, illustrates both the potential and the structural constraints of cities seeking to influence state-centered international governance.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察《2020–2023年国际关系总体规划》与《2025–2029年国际战略》如何将进步市政主义转化为对外行动。通过质性内容分析与比较，本文识别出国际化的三种机制：规范投射、网络化放大和多层级资源动员。两份战略均推动以社会正义、住房、民主参与、数字权利和生态转型为核心的权利导向议程。然而，科劳执政时期以运动为导向的市政主义，在科尔博尼任内演变为一种更加制度化、以伙伴关系为基础的模式。这一转变既体现了巴塞罗那进步取向的延续性，也体现了其温和化。本文还强调了市政国际化的局限：尽管这些战略支持议程设置、政策流通、联盟构建和资源获取，但它们既未赋予城市正式的国际权威，也不能保证实施。因此，巴塞罗那既展示了城市试图影响以国家为中心的国际治理的潜力，也揭示了其所面临的结构性约束。


`U1` **Build for whom? Contested state entrepreneurialism and the governance of affordable rental housing in Shanghai, China**  
**为谁而建？上海保障性租赁住房治理中的争议性国家企业主义**  
*Qianyu Lu, Yang Song*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498685](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498685)

> **Abstract:** This paper critically interrogates state entrepreneurialism through an empirical analysis of China’s recent Affordable Rental Housing (ARH) programme. Prevailing accounts of social housing under neoliberalism tend to frame it as a residual, market-enabling instrument of welfare retrenchment. Such perspectives, however, inadequately capture contexts in which expanded provision is intertwined with intensified and reconfigured forms of state intervention. Drawing on 92 qualitative interviews in Shanghai, this paper shows how ARH operates as a strategically selective mechanism embedded within broader projects of labour governance and territorial development. While it functions as a spatial fix to absorb oversupplied housing stock amid the crisis tendencies of land-centred accumulation, its implementation reveals a fragmented and contested process of state entrepreneurialism. Intra-state frictions, bureaucratic negotiations and divergent local growth rationalities reproduce uneven and contingent outcomes, rather than a coherent developmental logic. Access to ARH is selectively structured to privilege skilled migrants, demonstrating how state housing provision is mobilised to sort labour and align welfare responsibilities with labour value extraction and urban competitiveness. Meanwhile, hierarchical state-market arrangements reproduce regulatory asymmetries and uneven access. This paper argues that social housing in contemporary China constitutes a contested terrain of accumulation and legitimation, complicating linear narratives of neoliberal convergence and advancing a more critical understanding of state-led housing provision.
>
> **摘要：** 本文通过对中国近期保障性租赁住房（ARH）计划的实证分析，批判性地审视国家企业主义。关于新自由主义下社会住房的主流论述，往往将其视为福利紧缩中一种残余性的、服务于市场的工具。然而，这些视角难以充分解释供给扩张与强化和重构的国家干预形式相互交织的情境。基于在上海开展的92次定性访谈，本文展示了保障性租赁住房如何作为一种嵌入更广泛的劳动力治理和地域发展计划中的战略选择性机制而运作。尽管它在以土地为中心的积累所面临的危机趋势下，作为一种空间修复来吸纳过剩的住房存量，但其实施过程揭示出一种碎片化且充满争议的国家企业主义进程。国家内部的摩擦、官僚协商以及分化的地方增长逻辑，再生产出不均衡且偶然的结果，而非一种连贯的发展逻辑。保障性租赁住房的准入被有选择地设计为优先照顾技能型移民，表明国家住房供给如何被动员来对劳动力进行分选，并使福利责任与劳动价值榨取和城市竞争力相一致。同时，等级化的国家—市场安排再生产出监管不对称和不均衡的准入。本文认为，当代中国的社会住房构成了一个积累与合法化相互争夺的领域，这使关于新自由主义趋同的线性叙事复杂化，并推进了对国家主导住房供给的更具批判性的理解。


`U3` **Interpreting mobility reform through care: Ambivalent authority in Taiwan**  
**以关怀解读移动性改革：台湾的矛盾性权威**  
*Francis Chia Hui Lin*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498097](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498097)

> **Abstract:** This article examines how human-centred mobility reform can reproduce, rather than displace, entrenched hierarchies of automobility. Focusing on Taiwan, it analyses a regulatory archive of national traffic legislation and Taipei municipal instruments through which recent safety reforms have been articulated in the language of care, participation, and protection for vulnerable road users. The article argues that these reforms do not simply replace vehicle-centred urban order with pedestrian-oriented governance. Instead, they overlay existing mobility hierarchies with increasingly dense classifications, conditional provisions, and delegated discretion. Drawing on Homi Bhabha’s concept of ambivalence, the paper conceptualises this condition as ambivalent authority: a mode of postcolonial governance in which authority must appear protective and non-coercive while preserving inherited infrastructures of motorised priority. Through analysis of human-oriented road design, pedestrian-priority legislation, scooter and non-motorised classifications, and municipal mechanisms for temporary road use, the paper shows how care becomes a territorial technology of rule. Its contribution is threefold: it links postcolonial theory to urban mobility governance, clarifies how automobility persists through reform rather than only through resistance to reform, and reframes regulatory complexity as a political mechanism through which contradictory urban priorities are managed. The article concludes that mobility reform in Taiwan governs not by resolving the tension between care and hierarchy, but by continually recalibrating it.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察以人为本的移动性改革如何再生产而非取代根深蒂固的汽车移动等级秩序。本文聚焦台湾，分析了由全国交通立法和台北市政工具构成的法规档案，近期的安全改革正是通过这些法规，以关怀、参与和保护弱势道路使用者的话语加以表述的。本文认为，这些改革并非简单地以行人导向的治理取代以车辆为中心的城市秩序，而是在既有移动等级之上叠加了日益繁密的分类、附条件规定和委托裁量权。借鉴霍米·巴巴（Homi Bhabha）的“矛盾性”（ambivalence）概念，本文将这一状况概念化为“矛盾性权威”：一种后殖民治理模式，其中权威必须显得具有保护性且非强制性，同时又要维护所继承的机动车优先基础设施。通过对人本道路设计、行人优先立法、机车与非机动车分类以及道路临时使用的市政机制的分析，本文展示了关怀如何成为一种领域性的统治技术。本文的贡献有三：将后殖民理论与城市移动性治理相联系；阐明汽车移动性如何通过改革本身而非仅通过对改革的抵制得以延续；并将法规复杂性重新界定为管理相互矛盾的城市优先事项的政治机制。文章最后指出，台湾的移动性改革并非通过化解关怀与等级之间的张力来进行治理，而是通过不断地重新校准这一张力。


`D1` **From Frankenstein to Gulliver urbanism: Reframing AI and scale in urban theory**  
**从弗兰肯斯坦城市主义到格列佛城市主义：在城市理论中重构人工智能与尺度**  
*Farshad Shariatpour*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261473549](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261473549)

> **Abstract:** Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in urban governance, infrastructure and design, yet urban theory still lacks a clear language for analysing the scale asymmetries through which these systems operate. This article develops Gulliver Urbanism as a conceptual framework for understanding how AI reshapes urban power when it processes information, acts, and coordinates across cognitive, temporal and spatial horizons that exceed ordinary human capacities and institutional rhythms. Moving beyond Frankenstein Urbanism’s emphasis on rebellion and loss of control, we argue that the central problem of contemporary AI urbanism is scale asymmetry within co-constituted human and machine assemblages. The framework comprises six dimensions: cognitive scale, temporal scale, spatial scale, governance asymmetry, autonomy and agency and human response. It synthesises scholarship on AI urbanism, urban political economy, infrastructural urbanism, posthumanism, law and scale, supported by AI governance frameworks and vignettes on Hangzhou City Brain, robotaxis and generative design. The article conceptualises the urban as a constitutive condition of AI scale asymmetry, where algorithmic systems encounter infrastructure, land, mobility, public services, municipal authority, social difference and collective life. Across these domains, the key challenge is how to govern systems whose capacities outpace human oversight while remaining embedded in corporate, legal and political arrangements. We argue that effective responses require mixed-intelligence governance and temporal dignity: arrangements that subject algorithmic operations to democratic oversight, contestation and human-paced deliberation. Gulliver Urbanism explains how AI reconstitutes urban life by reorganising infrastructures, municipal authority, spatial relations, design imaginaries, public services and everyday citizenship.
>
> **摘要：** 人工智能（AI）正日益嵌入城市治理、基础设施和设计之中，但城市理论仍缺乏一套清晰的语言来分析这些系统运作所依赖的尺度不对称。本文提出“格列佛城市主义”作为一个概念框架，用以理解当人工智能在超越普通人类能力和制度节奏的认知、时间和空间视域中处理信息、采取行动并进行协调时，如何重塑城市权力。超越“弗兰肯斯坦城市主义”对反叛与失控的强调，我们认为当代人工智能城市主义的核心问题在于共同构成的人—机集合体内部的尺度不对称。该框架包括六个维度：认知尺度、时间尺度、空间尺度、治理不对称、自主性与能动性以及人类回应。它综合了关于人工智能城市主义、城市政治经济学、基础设施城市主义、后人类主义、法律与尺度的研究，并辅以人工智能治理框架以及关于杭州城市大脑、无人驾驶出租车和生成式设计的案例片段。本文将城市概念化为人工智能尺度不对称的构成性条件，在此算法系统与基础设施、土地、交通、公共服务、市政权力、社会差异和集体生活相遭遇。在这些领域中，关键挑战在于如何治理那些能力超越人类监督、却仍嵌入企业、法律和政治安排之中的系统。我们认为，有效的回应需要“混合智能治理”和“时间尊严”：即让算法运作接受民主监督、争议以及以人类节奏进行审议的制度安排。格列佛城市主义解释了人工智能如何通过重组基础设施、市政权力、空间关系、设计想象、公共服务和日常公民身份来重构城市生活。


`U2` **Beyond the perimeter walls: Negotiated porosity and the governance of megaproject edges in Metro Manila**  
**越过围墙：大马尼拉巨型项目边缘的协商性渗透与治理**  
*Justin D. Stern*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498686](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498686)

> **Abstract:** This article examines Bonifacio Global City (BGC), an urban megaproject in Metro Manila, from the perspective of two adjacent barangays, or local government units: Guadalupe Nuevo and South Cembo. Drawing on fieldwork conducted between 2017 and 2024, it asks how these barangays manage the demands of a megaproject planned and marketed as self-contained, and how interdependence at the enclave edge becomes a basis for contesting the terms of urban porosity. Like many central business districts, BGC does not house many of the workers its economy depends on; it relies in part on adjacent barangays that provide the lower-cost housing, everyday services and mobility connections many workers require. The closure and later negotiated reopening of the J.P. Rizal butas, a long-standing informal opening in BGC’s perimeter wall, made this interdependence politically visible. From this case, the paper develops the concept of negotiated porosity. The term describes how the dependencies that sustain an enclave can become spatially concentrated at an interruptible point on its edge, allowing local government actors to contest the status and terms of access across the boundary. The article contributes to work on enclave urbanism, porous enclaves and the governance of urban megaprojects in the Philippines and beyond.
>
> **摘要：** 本文从两个毗邻的描笼涯（即地方政府单位）——瓜达卢佩努埃沃和南森波——的视角，考察大马尼拉的城市巨型项目博尼法西奥环球城（BGC）。基于2017年至2024年间开展的田野调查，本文追问这些描笼涯如何应对一个被规划和营销为自给自足的巨型项目所提出的需求，以及飞地边缘的相互依赖如何成为争夺城市渗透性条件的基础。与许多中央商务区一样，BGC并未容纳其经济所依赖的众多工人；它部分依赖于毗邻的描笼涯来提供许多工人所需的低成本住房、日常服务和交通联系。BGC围墙上一个长期存在的非正式开口——J.P. Rizal“butas”——的关闭及其后经协商的重新开放，使这种相互依赖在政治上变得可见。基于这一案例，本文提出“协商性渗透”概念。该术语描述维系飞地的各种依赖关系如何在其边缘一个可被中断的点上发生空间集中，从而使地方政府行动者能够就跨越边界的通行地位与条件展开争夺。本文为飞地城市主义、可渗透飞地以及菲律宾及其他地区城市巨型项目治理的研究作出贡献。


`U2` **Cultural buffering in entrepreneurial city diplomacy: Wuxi’s economic engagement abroad**  
**企业家型城市外交中的文化缓冲：无锡的海外经济交往**  
*Yichao Du*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261500009](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500009)

> **Abstract:** Why do investment-oriented municipal delegations begin with culture? This article develops cultural buffering to explain how municipal actors use cultural forms and assets when development-oriented diplomacy brings together organisations with different authority, timetables and commitments. The analysis draws on multi-sited ethnography of 5 Wuxi delegation circuits across Japan, the United Kingdom and continental Europe, followed by tracing of 21 initiatives to July 2026. It identifies two recurrent problems: municipal schedules can outrun partner learning and due diligence, while demands for visible outputs can exceed commitment and post-event ownership. Municipal actors respond to these temporal and accountability mismatches through naming, sequencing, coalition broadening and managed ambiguity. These moves can change who participates, what becomes discussable, which organisations enter the relationship and how provisional alignment is recorded. Subsequent project implementation depends on authority, resources, delivery capacity and an organisation willing to own the next step. Cultural buffering therefore identifies the encounter-level work through which an unsettled proposal becomes discussable and revisitable before its implementation is determined.
>
> **摘要：** 为什么以投资为导向的市政代表团要以文化开场？本文提出“文化缓冲”概念，用以解释当以发展为导向的外交将权限、时间表和承诺各不相同的组织聚集在一起时，市政行动者如何运用文化形式和文化资产。分析基于对无锡5个代表团出访行程（涉及日本、英国和欧洲大陆）的多点民族志研究，以及对21项倡议截至2026年7月的后续追踪。研究识别出两个反复出现的问题：市政日程可能超前于合作方的了解与尽职调查，而对可见成果的要求可能超出实际承诺和活动后的责任归属。市政行动者通过命名、排序、扩大联盟和有管理的模糊性来应对这些时间和问责上的错配。这些举措可以改变谁参与、什么变得可以讨论、哪些组织进入合作关系以及临时性共识如何被记录。后续项目实施取决于权限、资源、交付能力以及是否有组织愿意承担下一步工作。因此，文化缓冲揭示了在实施尚未确定之前，一个未定的提议如何在会面层面上变得可讨论、可再议的工作过程。


`T1` **Narrating ontologies of ruination in the disciplinary blur: Ruins as temporal gardens, trees as legal witnesses and birds as world-makers**  
**在学科模糊地带叙述废墟化的本体论：作为时间花园的废墟、作为法律见证者的树木与作为世界建构者的鸟类**  
*Mariya Hamada*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261500245](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500245)

> **Abstract:** This commentary investigates the disciplinary blur that challenges the dual ontology of ruination as being both an urban/architectural transformation and an ecological phenomenon. It defies categorizations of objective knowledge and comes to terms with multispecies and interdisciplinary approaches, both historiographically and ethnographically, readjusting to the multiple temporalities of the nonhuman world-making projects that the human-made ruin becomes part of. Drawing on the urban ruin of 19 th -century Manchester (Cottonopolis), England, and the architectural ruins of contemporary Mogador, Morocco, it argues that the temporalities of the nonhuman, intersected respectively with periodicities of industrial contamination and human abandonment, transform the singular obsolete narrative of ruins into a plural transdisciplinary debate questioning the unyielding becoming of urban space. The text’s historiographical and ethnographical micronarratives, woven together through storytelling, theorize the transformative temporalities of urban nature and their capacity to provide the anthropogenic ruin with a collaborative ecological agency. This, the commentary argues, adds botanical and ornithological ontologies to urban and architectural taxonomies of ruination. By positioning oneself in the disciplinary blur, the commentary ideates on how architectural and urban studies can accommodate unfamiliar rhythms of nonhuman-constructed botanical and ornithological ruins.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论探讨学科模糊地带，这一地带挑战了废墟化的双重本体论——废墟化既是一种城市/建筑转变，也是一种生态现象。它拒斥客观知识的分类，接纳多物种与跨学科的路径，无论在史学还是民族志层面，均重新调适以契合人造废墟所融入的非人类世界建构计划的多重时间性。本文以英国19世纪曼彻斯特（“棉都”）的城市废墟和当代摩洛哥摩加多尔的建筑废墟为例，认为非人类的时间性分别与工业污染和人类弃置的周期性相交织，将关于废墟的单一陈旧叙事转化为多元的跨学科讨论，从而质疑城市空间不屈不挠的生成过程。文中通过讲故事编织在一起的史学与民族志微观叙事，理论化了城市自然的变革性时间性及其赋予人为废墟协作性生态能动性的能力。本评论认为，这为城市与建筑的废墟化分类学增添了植物学和鸟类学的本体论。通过将自身置于学科模糊地带，本评论构想建筑与城市研究如何容纳由非人类建构的植物与鸟类废墟的陌生节律。


`R3` **Selective responsibility: Environmental, social and governance frameworks and the containment of environmental and social harm in urban real estate**  
**选择性责任：环境、社会和治理框架与城市房地产中环境与社会危害的遏制**  
*Frances Brill*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261492637](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261492637)

> **Abstract:** As environmental, social and governance (ESG) frameworks become increasingly institutionalised within real-estate investment, questions of corporate responsibility for urban social and environmental challenges become more evident. Drawing on the case of Zurich and applying Young’s Social Connection Model of responsibility, this paper examines how ESG shapes the ways in which real-estate investors identify, articulate and act upon their responsibilities towards urban environments and communities. I argue that rather than constituting a meaningful framework for addressing structural housing inequality and environmental harm, ESG operates primarily as a risk-management tool that translatesc heterogeneous societal concerns into discrete, auditable categories. Environmental metrics dominate due to their compatibility with existing valuation practices, while social dimensions stagnate and governance is reduced to documentation. The result is a form of selective, depoliticised responsibility that acknowledges corporate implication in urban processes while simultaneously containing and neutralising its scope. Nevertheless, ESG’s ambiguity creates organisational openings through which individual actors can advance more ambitious environmental and social agendas. The paper concludes that ESG enables incremental progress while leaving the structural drivers of housing inequality and environmental harm fundamentally unchallenged.
>
> **摘要：** 随着环境、社会和治理（ESG）框架在房地产投资中日益制度化，企业对城市社会与环境挑战所负责任的问题愈发凸显。本文以苏黎世为案例，运用杨（Young）的责任社会联系模型，考察ESG如何塑造房地产投资者识别、阐述并践行其对城市环境与社区责任的方式。我认为，ESG并未构成应对结构性住房不平等和环境危害的有意义框架，而主要作为一种风险管理工具，将异质性的社会关切转化为离散的、可审计的类别。环境指标因其与现有估值实践的兼容性而占据主导地位，社会维度则停滞不前，治理被简化为文件记录。其结果是一种选择性的、去政治化的责任形式：它承认企业牵涉于城市过程之中，同时又遏制和消解了这一责任的范围。尽管如此，ESG的模糊性创造了组织上的缺口，使个体行动者能够借此推进更具雄心的环境与社会议程。本文的结论是，ESG促成了渐进式进展，但住房不平等与环境危害的结构性驱动因素在根本上并未受到挑战。


`S3` **Assessment of the capacity of active ageing policies in Türkiye to adapt to age-friendly cities**  
**土耳其积极老龄化政策适应老年友好城市的能力评估**  
*Muhammet Keskin*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261497637](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261497637)

> **Abstract:** This study evaluates the alignment and historical development of active ageing policies in Türkiye within the framework of the World Health Organization’s (WHO) Age-Friendly Cities model. This qualitative study employs document analysis to examine the Specialised Commission Reports on ageing and active ageing prepared for the 10th, 11th and 12th Development Plans, as well as the National Action Plan on the Rights of Older Persons (2023–2025). These documents were analysed using WHO’s eight core domains, with a focus on policy characteristics, institutional responsibilities, and monitoring and evaluation mechanisms. The findings reveal a paradigm shift from a paternalistic, service-oriented approach toward an autonomous, rights- and technology-driven framework. Although substantial strategic progress is evident in healthcare and social policy, its integration with spatial planning and urban design remains limited, leaving transformation in physical domains (transportation and outdoor spaces) largely confined to policy discourse. While strategic vision has become progressively aligned with implementation instruments, the absence of binding local standards and impact-based monitoring remains a fundamental challenge. These limitations can be associated with Türkiye’s post-2011 institutional transformation and the evolving dynamics of its macro-planning functions. This administrative context has strengthened central institutional capacity while creating new coordination challenges for urban implementation and the operational flexibility of local authorities. Consequently, achieving genuinely age-friendly cities requires a holistic governance model that strengthens cross-sectoral coordination, supports local implementation through common standards, and is underpinned by impact-oriented monitoring.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究在世界卫生组织（WHO）老年友好城市模型的框架下，评估土耳其积极老龄化政策的一致性及其历史发展。这项定性研究采用文献分析法，考察为第10、11和12个发展计划编制的关于老龄化和积极老龄化的专门委员会报告，以及《老年人权利国家行动计划（2023—2025）》。研究依据WHO的八个核心领域对这些文件进行分析，重点关注政策特征、机构责任以及监测与评估机制。研究发现揭示了一种范式转变：从家长式、以服务为导向的方法转向自主的、由权利和技术驱动的框架。尽管在医疗卫生和社会政策方面取得了显著的战略进展，但其与空间规划和城市设计的整合仍然有限，物理领域（交通和户外空间）的转型在很大程度上仍停留于政策话语。尽管战略愿景已逐步与实施工具相衔接，但缺乏具有约束力的地方标准和基于影响的监测仍是一项根本挑战。这些局限可与土耳其2011年后的制度转型及其宏观规划职能的演变动态相关联。这一行政背景强化了中央机构能力，同时也给城市层面的实施以及地方当局的运作灵活性带来了新的协调挑战。因此，要实现真正的老年友好城市，需要一种整体性治理模式：加强跨部门协调，通过共同标准支持地方实施，并以影响导向的监测为支撑。


`U3` **Urban transport governance in Accra: Integrative approaches and hybrid modalities of reform**  
**阿克拉的城市交通治理：整合性方法与改革的混合模式**  
*Devanne Brookins*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261464899](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261464899)

> **Abstract:** Urban transport reforms, while recognized as central to economic transformation and environmental sustainability, have been difficult to realize across Global South cities. Engaging this challenge, the article explores the socio-institutional dynamics of urban transport reform through the lens of integrative approaches to governance in Accra. It argues that reform processes must be understood not merely as technical or efficiency-driven interventions but as moments of institutional disruption and political contestation that reveal the embedded interests, capacities, and power asymmetries among urban stakeholders. Focusing on hybrid governance modalities and their intersection with existing governing coalitions, the article highlights how such configurations reshape the role of the state and reconfigure relationships with non-state actors. Employing a political settlements framework, the analysis demonstrates how entrenched paratransit actors navigate and adapt to new institutional arrangements to maintain their influence, often to the detriment of transport reforms such as bus rapid transit. The case underscores the importance of historically informed and context-sensitive approaches to research and suggests the potential for comparative typology of integrative reforms to enhance understanding of institutional outcomes and inform future interventions. This article contributes to the growing literature on the governance of urban transport, offering a nuanced perspective of integrative approaches by revealing how integrative approaches, layered institutional innovations, and extant governing coalitions shape the uneven trajectories of urban transport reform.
>
> **摘要：** 城市交通改革虽被公认为经济转型与环境可持续性的核心，但在全球南方城市中一直难以实现。针对这一挑战，本文通过整合性治理方法的视角，探讨阿克拉城市交通改革的社会—制度动态。本文认为，改革过程不应仅被理解为技术性或效率驱动的干预，而应被视为制度扰动与政治争夺的时刻，从中可以揭示城市利益相关者之间根深蒂固的利益、能力和权力不对称。本文聚焦于混合治理模式及其与现有执政联盟的交汇，阐明这些配置如何重塑国家的角色并重构其与非国家行动者的关系。运用政治解决框架，分析表明根深蒂固的辅助公交行动者如何在新的制度安排中周旋和适应以维持其影响力，而这往往不利于快速公交系统等交通改革。该案例凸显了具有历史意识和情境敏感性的研究方法的重要性，并提出建立整合性改革比较类型学的可能性，以增进对制度结果的理解并为未来干预提供参考。本文为日益增长的城市交通治理文献作出贡献，通过揭示整合性方法、层叠式制度创新与现有执政联盟如何塑造城市交通改革的不均衡轨迹，为整合性方法提供了细致的视角。


`S3` **Aspirations and expectations of participation in age-friendly cities: Perspectives from across four European case studies**  
**老年友好城市中参与的愿景与期望：来自四个欧洲案例研究的视角**  
*Patty Doran, Tine Buffel, Linda Naughton*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261493750](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261493750)

> **Abstract:** The Age-friendly Cities and Communities framework is widely used by policymakers seeking to create environments that support well-being and inclusion for older people as they age in place. However, there is limited empirical insight into how participation is understood and interpreted within age-friendly programmes. To address this gap, this paper examines how policymakers and delivery partners understand and approach older people’s participation through a multi-case study of four long-established European age-friendly cities: Brno, Oslo, Brussels and Bilbao. The case studies drew on semi-structured interviews ( n = 42) and observational fieldwork in each city, supported by in-country experts. The analysis identified four key themes: individualised participation linked to active and healthy ageing; civic and democratic engagement in decision-making; everyday participation in social and community life; and concerns around inclusion and exclusion. Findings show that participation is understood in multiple and sometimes competing ways, combining aspirations for inclusion and well-being with expectations that older people remain active and contribute to their communities. This reveals a tension between participation as an opportunity that older people can take up on their own terms if they wish to do so, and participation as an expectation associated with active ageing. The paper shows how such expectations can shift attention away from unequal opportunities to participate and the barriers faced by marginalised groups. The paper argues that, without critical attention to this tension, age-friendly participation risks reproducing rather than addressing inequalities.
>
> **摘要：** “老年友好城市和社区”框架被政策制定者广泛使用，旨在为老年人就地养老创造支持福祉与包容的环境。然而，关于参与在老年友好项目中如何被理解和诠释，实证洞见十分有限。为填补这一空白，本文通过对四个历史悠久的欧洲老年友好城市——布尔诺、奥斯陆、布鲁塞尔和毕尔巴鄂——的多案例研究，考察政策制定者和实施伙伴如何理解和推进老年人的参与。案例研究借助半结构化访谈（n = 42）以及在各城市开展的观察性田野调查，并得到当地专家的支持。分析识别出四个关键主题：与积极健康老龄化相关的个体化参与；决策中的公民与民主参与；社会与社区生活中的日常参与；以及围绕包容与排斥的关切。研究发现，参与以多种有时相互竞争的方式被理解，既包含对包容与福祉的愿景，也包含对老年人保持活跃并为社区作出贡献的期望。这揭示出一种张力：一方面，参与是老年人可以按自己的意愿选择接受的机会；另一方面，参与是与积极老龄化相关联的期望。本文表明，这种期望可能将注意力从参与机会的不平等以及边缘群体面临的障碍上转移开。本文认为，若不对这一张力进行批判性审视，老年友好的参与可能会再生产而非消解不平等。


`U1` **Denser housing, more commodified? Comparing the relationship between densification and the production of (de-)commodified housing in Milan, Oslo and Vienna**  
**住房更密，商品化更甚？米兰、奥斯陆与维也纳城市加密与（去）商品化住房生产关系的比较**  
*Michael Friesenecker, Marco Peverini, Rebecca Cavicchia*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261494806](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261494806)

> **Abstract:** Urban densification is often linked to sustainability benefits and sometimes advocated as a strategy to improve housing provision and affordability. This claim, however, rests on the assumption that increasing supply translates into improved housing affordability and social outcomes, while overlooking the fact that densification is often realised through market-led development processes that tend to reproduce commodified forms of housing provision. While decommodification cannot be equated with affordability, it is a relevant dimension of long-term inclusiveness of housing systems. We assume that the relationship between densification and the (de)commodification of housing is not inherent to increased density or housing supply but mediated by institutional and policy arrangements. This article explores how densification relates to the provision of (de)commodified housing through a comparative analysis of Milan, Oslo and Vienna, European cities with divergent housing systems and policy capacities. We combine quantitative analysis of tenure composition in densification areas with a qualitative assessment of capacities in land, zoning and housing policy. We show that housing produced through densification is predominantly commodified, but to varying degrees. In Oslo, private-sector-led land development and limited housing-policy capacity result in predominantly market-led densification; Milan combines greater planning capacity with limited subsidies and market-oriented land development while Vienna combines land provision and zoning with subsidies for limited-profit housing. Our comparative findings highlight that the degree of (de)commodification depends on local capacities to shape and coordinate housing and land policies towards either decommodified or market-oriented provision, demonstrating that similar densification strategies can produce different housing outcomes across institutional contexts.
>
> **摘要：** 城市加密常被与可持续性收益联系在一起，有时也被倡导为改善住房供给和可负担性的策略。然而，这一主张建立在增加供给即可转化为住房可负担性和社会成效改善的假设之上，却忽视了城市加密往往通过市场主导的开发过程实现，而这些过程倾向于再生产商品化的住房供给形式。尽管去商品化不能等同于可负担性，但它是住房体系长期包容性的一个相关维度。我们假定，城市加密与住房（去）商品化之间的关系并非密度增加或住房供给本身所固有，而是由制度与政策安排所中介。本文通过对米兰、奥斯陆和维也纳这三个住房体系与政策能力各异的欧洲城市进行比较分析，探讨城市加密如何与（去）商品化住房的供给相关联。我们将对加密区域住房权属构成的定量分析与对土地、区划和住房政策能力的定性评估相结合。研究表明，通过城市加密生产的住房以商品化为主，但程度各异。在奥斯陆，私营部门主导的土地开发和有限的住房政策能力导致以市场主导为主的加密；米兰将较强的规划能力与有限的补贴及市场导向的土地开发相结合；而维也纳则将土地供给和区划与对有限盈利住房的补贴相结合。比较研究结果突显，（去）商品化的程度取决于地方塑造和协调住房与土地政策、使之朝向去商品化或市场导向供给的能力，表明相似的城市加密策略在不同制度背景下可能产生不同的住房结果。


`U2` **Governance as obstacle: The reproduction of urban path dependencies in Kuwait**  
**作为障碍的治理：科威特城市路径依赖的再生产**  
*Nuno F. da Cruz, Dhari S. Alrasheed, Muneerah Alrabe, Abdullah al-Khonaini*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261497864](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261497864)

> **Abstract:** This article investigates how urban governance in Kuwait reproduces unsustainable spatial and socio-economic outcomes. We distinguish two dynamics: outcomes the state deliberately sustains, above all the residential segregation of Kuwaitis and non-Kuwaitis, and outcomes it professes to pursue in its own plans yet persistently fails to deliver, such as curbing sprawl and car dependence or clearing chronic housing shortfalls. Situating governance within a rentier political economy, we treat it not as a neutral mechanism but as a state-spatial strategy through which oil rents are territorialised, with sharply different consequences for citizens and non-citizens. Drawing on interview-based network analysis, we show that authority is highly centralised yet fragmented in delivery and dominated by technocratic agencies, while actors focused on social or mobility concerns remain peripheral. While the government appears to acknowledge the need for reform, Kuwait’s governance regime actively obstructs the city’s ability to shift course, sustaining a path-dependent urban model through institutional exclusion, inertia, and repeated disruption of planning. The article contributes to debates on state capacity, rentier political economy, and urban governance in the Gulf, grounded in both quantitative and qualitative analysis.
>
> **摘要：** 本文探讨科威特的城市治理如何再生产不可持续的空间与社会经济结果。我们区分了两种动态：一是国家刻意维持的结果，首先是科威特人与非科威特人的居住隔离；二是国家在自身规划中声称要追求却始终未能实现的结果，如遏制城市蔓延和汽车依赖、解决长期住房短缺问题。我们将治理置于食利型政治经济之中，不把它视为一种中立机制，而视为一种国家空间战略，石油租金借此得以地域化，并对公民和非公民产生截然不同的后果。基于以访谈为基础的网络分析，我们表明，权力高度集中但在执行上碎片化，且由技术官僚机构主导，而关注社会或交通问题的行动者则处于边缘地位。尽管政府似乎承认改革的必要性，科威特的治理体制却通过制度性排斥、惰性以及对规划的反复干扰，积极阻碍城市改变方向，维持着一种路径依赖的城市模式。本文基于定量与定性分析，为关于国家能力、食利型政治经济以及海湾地区城市治理的讨论作出了贡献。


`S3` **Beyond ‘staying put’? Ageing in place through a relocation-based housing project in Singapore**  
**超越“原地不动”？通过新加坡一个基于迁居的住房项目实现原居安老**  
*Shengxiao (Alex) Li, Shin Bin Tan*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498093](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498093)

> **Abstract:** Ageing in place (AIP) has become a central framework for planning housing and infrastructure in ageing societies. The AIP literature is dominated by the perspective of ‘staying put’. As such, there is limited evidence on whether relocation can provide a viable pathway for older people to age in place. Accordingly, this study examines Kampung Admiralty (KA) – a government-led vertical mixed-use development in Singapore that integrates senior housing, grocery shopping, healthcare, childcare, and an active ageing centre (AAC) under a single roof – to assess its potential as a strategy for promoting healthy ageing. From interviews with residents in KA and communities nearby, we find that relocating older adults to smaller, affordable housing units meets the needs of older people who want to downsize their residences. The AAC serves as a focal point for developing new social networks among both relocated residents and older adults living nearby. The ‘everything under one roof’ model is well received, especially given residents’ declining health and the challenges that Singapore’s tropical climate poses. Although KA demonstrates success in relocating people and integrating functions for older people, it has limitations, including insufficient provision for live-in helpers and constrained access to specialised healthcare. This case offers important theoretical and policy insights for AIP.
>
> **摘要：** 原居安老已成为老龄化社会中住房与基础设施规划的核心框架。原居安老研究文献以“原地不动”的视角为主导，因此，关于迁居能否为老年人提供实现原居安老的可行途径，相关证据十分有限。据此，本研究考察了新加坡海军部村庄——一个由政府主导的垂直混合用途开发项目，将老年住房、食品购物、医疗保健、托儿服务和活跃乐龄中心整合于同一屋檐下——以评估其作为促进健康老龄化策略的潜力。通过对海军部村庄及周边社区居民的访谈，我们发现，将老年人迁至面积更小、价格可负担的住房单元，满足了希望缩小居所的老年人的需求。活跃乐龄中心成为迁入居民与周边老年人建立新社会网络的焦点。“一站式”模式广受欢迎，尤其是考虑到居民健康状况的下降以及新加坡热带气候带来的挑战。尽管海军部村庄在迁居安置和为老年人整合功能方面取得了成功，但它仍存在局限，包括对住家帮佣的配套不足以及专科医疗服务可及性受限。该案例为原居安老提供了重要的理论与政策启示。


`E1` **The project-ification of urban natures**  
**城市自然的项目化**  
*Charrlotte Adelina*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498096](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498096)

> **Abstract:** Project times are instrumental in co-producing temporalities of urban natures. To unpack how the figure of the project alters understandings of and governs relations with plant life, I point to frameworks through which urban ecologies come into being and are subsequently cared for. To illustrate these linkages, I draw on site visits to urban greening interventions in Hyderabad, Telangana (India) created as part of a state-led afforestation programme. In addition to a spatial ordering of plants for conservation, afforestation practices take shape through a ‘timing’ of vegetal life, in terms of projected and project gains. Taking the project-ification of plant life seriously, I argue that calls to recognise plural, divergent or non-linear temporalities would still need to contend with deeply entrenched, pervasive frameworks of project management in the governance of urban natures. Eco-managerial time practices and ways of relating to plants are, therefore, crucial sites of intervention for vegetal geography. Lastly, I question the uptake of the infrastructural lens, which interrogates how urban greening fails to perform its promised services without a critical look at the practices of doing, composing or contracting infrastructures, arguing instead for a study of ‘nature as project’.
>
> **摘要：** 项目时间在共同生产城市自然的时间性方面发挥着关键作用。为剖析“项目”这一形象如何改变人们对植物生命的理解并治理与植物生命的关系，我指出了城市生态得以形成并随后受到照护的若干框架。为阐明这些关联，我借助对印度特伦甘纳邦海得拉巴城市绿化干预项目的实地考察，这些项目是作为一项国家主导的植树造林计划的一部分而建立的。除了为保护目的而对植物进行空间排序之外，造林实践还通过对植物生命的“计时”——即预期收益与项目收益——而成形。认真对待植物生命的项目化，我认为，承认多元、分歧或非线性时间性的呼吁，仍需应对城市自然治理中根深蒂固、无处不在的项目管理框架。因此，生态管理主义的时间实践以及与植物相处的方式，是植物地理学的关键干预场域。最后，我质疑对基础设施视角的采纳——该视角追问城市绿化为何未能提供其承诺的服务，却未对实施、构成或承包基础设施的实践进行批判性审视——转而主张研究“作为项目的自然”。


`S3` **Two-wheelers and bicycles: Comparing mobility, meaningful activities and well-being among urban-dwelling older adults in Bengaluru and the Northern Netherlands**  
**摩托车与自行车：班加罗尔与荷兰北部城市老年人的移动性、有意义活动与福祉比较**  
*Louise Meijering, Sobin George, Thomas Aaron Lowe, Anne Mombarg et al.*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261498124](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498124)

> **Abstract:** Mobility, or everyday movement, is an integral part of ageing well. Situated in theoretical frameworks around the capability to be mobile, meaningful activities and subjective well-being, this article zooms in on the role of health and the urban context in everyday movement. This article aims to contrast and compare how urban-dwelling older adults in different health conditions use their capability to be mobile to engage in meaningful activities that contribute to their experienced well-being in the Bengaluru Metropolitan Area and the Northern Netherlands. We draw on a qualitative study, combining activity diaries and in-depth interviews, collected among 18 older adults in the Northern Netherlands and 22 in Bengaluru. The findings show that in both contexts, engaging in independent mobility can contribute to engagement in meaningful activities and well-being, with a particularly important role for two-wheelers and bicycles. Specifically, we found that older adults can stay well, even when their health declines; that they are able to maintain their mobility in creative ways, in light of both individual and environmental challenges; and that they adapt both their capability to be mobile and to engage in meaningful activities. In conclusion, age-friendly environments should support the capability to move independently in later life.
>
> **摘要：** 移动性，即日常移动，是健康老龄化不可或缺的一部分。本文立足于围绕移动能力、有意义活动和主观福祉的理论框架，聚焦健康状况与城市环境在日常移动中的作用。本文旨在对比和比较班加罗尔都市区与荷兰北部不同健康状况的城市老年人如何运用其移动能力参与有意义活动，进而促进其体验到的福祉。我们基于一项结合活动日志与深度访谈的质性研究，研究对象包括荷兰北部的18位老年人和班加罗尔的22位老年人。研究发现，在两种情境中，独立移动都有助于参与有意义活动并提升福祉，其中摩托车和自行车发挥着尤为重要的作用。具体而言，我们发现：即使健康状况下降，老年人仍能保持良好状态；面对个体与环境方面的挑战，他们能够以富有创造性的方式维持移动性；并且他们会调整自身的移动能力以及参与有意义活动的能力。总之，老年友好型环境应支持晚年独立移动的能力。


`S2` **Social isolation in motion: How socioeconomic segregation in everyday mobility varies by neighborhood racial-ethnic composition in U.S. cities**  
**流动中的社会隔离：美国城市日常移动中的社会经济隔离如何随街区种族—族裔构成而变化**  
*Brian L. Levy*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261493239](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261493239)

> **Abstract:** Recent scholarship finds that socioeconomic (dis)advantage in urban neighborhoods’ routine mobility networks—the neighborhoods their residents visit and receive visits from—predicts their vitality. Mobility-based (dis)advantage is correlated with, but conceptually distinct from, the socioeconomic status of a neighborhood’s residents. Thus, residential and mobility-based disadvantage can lead to distinct or compounding inequalities. This paper builds on Wilson’s social isolation framework to analyze how mobility-based disadvantage is unequally distributed by neighborhood racial-ethnic composition in the United States’ 100 most populous cities and their commuting zones. Majority-Black and majority-Hispanic neighborhoods experience heightened levels of mobility-based disadvantage, which deepens well-known disparities with majority-white neighborhoods based on residents’ socioeconomic statuses. Generally, mobility disadvantage compounds inequalities based on residential disadvantage, but for some Black and Hispanic neighborhoods with relatively low residential disadvantage, mobility disadvantage is a distinct form of inequality. The extent of these inequalities varies by urban form, with white spatial clustering and residential segregation by income as salient moderators. Isolation in structural neighborhood networks emerges as a clear feature of racial-ethnic inequality, though that one varies between cities.
>
> **摘要：** 近期研究发现，城市街区日常移动网络——即其居民前往访问以及接待来访者的街区——中的社会经济（劣）优势能够预测街区活力。基于移动的（劣）优势与街区居民的社会经济地位相关，但在概念上有所区别。因此，居住劣势与移动劣势可能导致各自独立或相互叠加的不平等。本文以威尔逊的社会隔离框架为基础，分析美国人口最多的100个城市及其通勤区中，基于移动的劣势如何按街区种族—族裔构成不平等地分布。以黑人为主和以西班牙裔为主的街区经历着更高程度的移动劣势，这加深了基于居民社会经济地位的、与以白人为主街区之间众所周知的差距。总体而言，移动劣势叠加了基于居住劣势的不平等；但对于一些居住劣势相对较低的黑人和西班牙裔街区而言，移动劣势是一种独特的不平等形式。这些不平等的程度因城市形态而异，白人空间集聚和按收入划分的居住隔离是显著的调节因素。结构性街区网络中的隔离显现为种族—族裔不平等的一个明显特征，尽管其程度因城市而异。


`S3` **Ageing, identity and place: Extending intersectional perspectives in age-friendly urbanism through the lived experiences of mid-older adults with intellectual disabilities and LGBT+ identities**  
**老龄化、身份与地方：通过患有智力障碍和具有LGBT+身份的中老年人的生活经验拓展老年友好城市主义中的交叉性视角**  
*Mei Lan Fang, Judith Sixsmith, Richard Vytniorgu, Joe Tai et al.*｜2026-10-21｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261492433](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261492433)

> **Abstract:** The World Health Organization’s Age-Friendly Cities and Communities (AFCC) agenda promotes environments that support independence and participation in later life, yet affords limited attention to the relational and intersectional dimensions of belonging. Drawing on IncludeAge, a multisite study across Great Britain, this study explores how community inclusion and exclusion are experienced and negotiated by two groups often marginalised within age-friendly research and policy: mid-older adults (aged 40+) with intellectual disabilities (ID) and mid-older LGBT+ people. In doing so, it extends age-friendly urbanism through the intersectional place perspective (IPP). Guided by community-based participatory research principles, life-course interviews were conducted with 77 participants with ID and 76 LGBT+ participants, across urban, suburban and rural contexts. Data were analysed using reflexive thematic and narrative co-analysis informed by the IPP. Three themes were developed through collaborative analysis: (1) everyday spaces, micro-interactions and the making of belonging; (2) agency, resistance and claiming space; and (3) digital spaces as sites of connection, exposure and mistrust. Across both groups, belonging was negotiated relationally and unevenly across physical, social, service and digital domains. By foregrounding intersectionality, place and life-course trajectories, the study advances a more reflexive understanding of age-friendly urbanism. It extends the IPP within urban ageing debates, illuminates cumulative disadvantage and resilience, demonstrates the value of co-produced knowledge and proposes a conceptual theory of change to support a more intersectionally responsive AFCC agenda.
>
> **摘要：** 世界卫生组织的“老年友好城市与社区”（AFCC）议程倡导营造支持晚年独立与参与的环境，但对归属感的关系性和交叉性维度关注有限。本研究依托覆盖英国多地的IncludeAge项目，探讨在老年友好研究和政策中常被边缘化的两个群体——患有智力障碍（ID）的中老年人（40岁及以上）和中老年LGBT+人群——如何体验并协商社区的包容与排斥。由此，本研究通过交叉性地方视角（IPP）拓展了老年友好城市主义。在社区参与式研究原则的指导下，研究对城市、郊区和乡村环境中的77名智力障碍参与者和76名LGBT+参与者进行了生命历程访谈。数据采用以IPP为指导的反思性主题分析与叙事共同分析进行分析。通过协作分析形成了三个主题：（1）日常空间、微观互动与归属感的营造；（2）能动性、抵抗与空间主张；（3）作为联结、暴露与不信任场所的数字空间。在两个群体中，归属感都是在物理、社会、服务和数字领域中以关系性且不均衡的方式协商而成的。通过突出交叉性、地方和生命历程轨迹，本研究推进了对老年友好城市主义更具反思性的理解。它在城市老龄化讨论中拓展了IPP，揭示了累积性劣势与韧性，展示了共同生产知识的价值，并提出了一个概念性变革理论，以支持一个对交叉性更具回应性的AFCC议程。


`S1` **Werewolf to Rudolph: Urban disciplining in public space and bubble acculturation of migrants**  
**从狼人到鲁道夫：公共空间中的城市规训与移民的泡泡式文化适应**  
*Chen Qu*｜2026-10-20｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261492146](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261492146)

> **Abstract:** Most research on migrant acculturation focuses on transnational, multi-ethnic contexts. This article examines internal rural-to-urban migrants in China, exploring how urban public space shapes their place-based acculturation. It rethinks acculturation through the lens of everyday disciplinary power, introducing place-based urban disciplining and the concept of bubble acculturation that challenges quasi-internal colonialism manifested as urban supremacy. Based on ethnographic research in a megacity, the study reveals a slow, fluid process of acculturation, where migrants’ initial sense of familiarity gradually becomes normality through interactions with the urban environment. Public space plays a dual role: it facilitates acculturation whilst also projecting dominant urban values that discipline migrants culturally. These findings challenge static or decontextualised models of acculturation by highlighting the spatial and cultural power embedded in urban life. The article argues for a critical cosmopolitanism that resists cultural hierarchies and recognises the agency of internal migrants in addressing dynamic urban transitions.
>
> **摘要：** 大多数关于移民文化适应的研究聚焦于跨国、多族群的情境。本文考察中国从农村到城市的国内移民，探讨城市公共空间如何塑造其基于地方的文化适应。本文透过日常规训权力的视角重新思考文化适应，提出基于地方的城市规训以及“泡泡式文化适应”概念，以挑战以城市至上为表现形式的准内部殖民主义。基于在一座超大城市开展的民族志研究，本研究揭示了一个缓慢而流动的文化适应过程，在此过程中，移民最初的熟悉感通过与城市环境的互动逐渐变为常态。公共空间发挥着双重作用：它促进文化适应，同时也投射出在文化上规训移民的主导性城市价值观。这些发现通过突出城市生活中所嵌入的空间与文化权力，挑战了静态或去情境化的文化适应模型。本文主张一种抵制文化等级、承认国内移民在应对动态城市转型中能动性的批判性世界主义。


`U2` **Governing street vendors, assembling differentiated Chinese statehood: The case of Guanting Street, Hefei**  
**治理街头摊贩，组装差异化的中国国家性：以合肥官亭街为例**  
*Hantao Sun, Neema Kudva*｜2026-10-20｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261493286](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261493286)

> **Abstract:** This paper investigates the everyday production of the local state through street vending governance in an informal food market on Guanting Street, Hefei, China. By studying how three state and quasi-state institutions jointly govern street vendors on Guanting Street, we argue that existing literature, which explains street vending governance through state fragmentation or ambivalence, does not sufficiently capture how dispersed and fragmented institutions relate to one another in practice. We observe and analyze the mechanisms in use and conclude by advancing the idea of differentiated statehood to explain governance of street vendors on Guanting Street. The analysis further demonstrates a key political effect of differentiated statehood: the production of a state that effectively manages the street yet forecloses collective negotiation. While rooted in a contemporary Chinese city and within debates on Chinese governance, the paper contributes more broadly to debates on governance of urban informality and everyday state formation.
>
> **摘要：** 本文通过中国合肥官亭街一个非正规食品市场中的街头摊贩治理，考察地方国家的日常生产。通过研究三个国家及准国家机构如何共同治理官亭街的街头摊贩，我们认为，现有以国家碎片化或矛盾性来解释街头摊贩治理的文献，未能充分把握分散且碎片化的机构在实践中如何相互关联。我们观察并分析了所使用的机制，最终提出“差异化国家性”的概念来解释官亭街的街头摊贩治理。分析进一步揭示了差异化国家性的一个关键政治效应：它生产出一个能够有效管理街道却排除集体协商的国家。尽管本文立足于当代中国城市及有关中国治理的讨论，但它更广泛地为城市非正规性治理和日常国家形成的相关讨论作出了贡献。


`U1` **Resisting gentrification: Density of belonging in strangled kampung of BSD City, Indonesia**  
**抵抗士绅化：印度尼西亚BSD城中被扼困村落的归属密度**  
*Eka Permanasari, Gita Rama Mahardhika, Muhammad Dimas Prasetya Witono Putra, Melia Dinata*｜2026-10-17｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/00420980261493775](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261493775)

> **Abstract:** As Southeast Asia urbanizes, urban expansion repeatedly displaces low-income communities, changes land use, and erodes cultural heritage. In Indonesia, the enclosure of low-income kampung settlements within master-planned private cities was not an organic market outcome but a deliberate product of the Suharto government’s location permit system. This article explores the contradictory pathways of densification through the lens of strangled kampungs, small, irregular settlements trapped within the expansive growth of exclusive, highly regulated private cities. Drawing on historical urban morphological mapping, semi-structured interviews with kampung residents, and field observation across eight strangled kampungs in BSD City, the study analyses four cross-cutting dimensions of this resilience: deliberate cartographic erasure by developers; border-making through enclosing walls, restricted access routes, and engineered flooding; religio-cultural anchoring through mosques, ancestral tombs, and active pilgrimage sites; and adaptive economic practices within the private city itself. The article introduces density of belonging where the concentration of shared memory, emotional attachment, cultural meaning, and religious practice embedded in place function as a critical and underexplored dimension of urban resilience.
>
> **摘要：** 随着东南亚的城市化，城市扩张不断驱逐低收入社区、改变土地利用并侵蚀文化遗产。在印度尼西亚，低收入甘榜（kampung）聚落被圈围于总体规划的私人城市之中，并非自然的市场结果，而是苏哈托政府区位许可制度的刻意产物。本文通过“被扼困的甘榜”——即困于排他性、高度管制的私人城市扩张之中的小型不规则聚落——这一视角，探讨致密化的矛盾路径。基于历史城市形态制图、对甘榜居民的半结构化访谈以及在BSD城八个被扼困甘榜的实地观察，本研究分析了这种韧性的四个交叉维度：开发商刻意的地图抹除；通过围墙、受限通行路线和人为制造的洪涝进行的边界营造；通过清真寺、祖先墓地和活跃的朝圣地实现的宗教—文化锚定；以及在私人城市内部的适应性经济实践。本文提出“归属密度”概念，即嵌入地方的共同记忆、情感依恋、文化意义和宗教实践的集聚，是城市韧性中一个关键却未被充分探讨的维度。
