## Environment and Planning A

*Environment and Planning A: Economy and Space*

`R3` **Safe bonds, risky debt? Covered bonds and the financialisation of housing in Sweden**  
**安全的债券，有风险的债务？——瑞典的担保债券与住房金融化**  
*Viktor Skyrman, Timothy Blackwell, Juan Barredo, Luis Buendía*｜2026-11-12｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/0308518x261499187](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261499187)

> **Abstract:** Contributing to the literatures on housing financialisation and financial geography, this article examines the growth and role of covered bonds in fuelling housing financialisation in Sweden. Lauded as safer and more efficient alternatives to residential mortgage-backed securities, covered mortgage bonds – on-balance-sheet debt securities issued by banks to fund mortgages – provide investors with robust protection through dual recourse to both the issuing bank and the segregated ‘cover pool’ of mortgage assets. While the structure of these instruments provides a derisking function for bondholders, they simultaneously reconfigure the architecture of housing finance in permissive mortgage regimes, such as Sweden’s, by intensifying the supply of mortgage credit in highly leveraged urban centres and concentrating credit risk on the balance sheets of domestic banks. Drawing on the historiography of regulatory change, policy debates and interviews with stakeholders, the article investigates how, despite resistance from state authorities, Swedish finance lobbyists were able to successfully frame covered bond legislation as essential to maintaining international financial competitiveness. The Swedish case illustrates how the interplay between the structural power of domestic financial elites and European financial integration, mediated through national policy settings, reshapes housing and finance systems, deepening systemic interdependencies between the financial system, households and the state, while reconfiguring the geographies of financial risk.
>
> **摘要：** 本文对住房金融化和金融地理学文献作出贡献，考察担保债券在瑞典助推住房金融化过程中的增长及其作用。担保抵押债券——由银行发行、用于为抵押贷款融资的表内债务证券——被誉为比住房抵押贷款支持证券更安全、更高效的替代品，它通过对发行银行和隔离的抵押资产“担保池”的双重追索权，为投资者提供强有力的保护。尽管这些工具的结构为债券持有人提供了去风险功能，但它们同时在瑞典这类宽松的抵押贷款体制中重构了住房金融架构：在高杠杆的城市中心强化抵押信贷供给，并将信用风险集中于国内银行的资产负债表上。本文借助监管变革的历史编纂、政策辩论以及对利益相关者的访谈，探讨在国家当局的抵制下，瑞典金融游说者如何成功地将担保债券立法塑造为维持国际金融竞争力所必需之举。瑞典案例表明，国内金融精英的结构性权力与欧洲金融一体化之间的相互作用，经由国家政策环境的中介，重塑了住房与金融体系，加深了金融体系、家庭与国家之间的系统性相互依赖，同时重构了金融风险的地理格局。


`R3` **Financialization and Local Statecraft: Introduction to the book review forum**  
**金融化与地方治国术：书评论坛导言**  
*Desiree Fields*｜2026-11-11｜Vol. 58(8)｜[doi:10.1177/0308518x261483258](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261483258)

> **Abstract:** This piece introduces a book review forum for Financialization and Local Statecraft by Andy Pike.
>
> **摘要：** 本文为安迪·派克所著《金融化与地方治国术》一书的书评论坛撰写导言。


`R2` **Licensing liberal imperialism: The ‘rogue recruiter’ and the racial logic of low-wage labor supply chains**  
**为自由帝国主义颁发许可：“流氓招募者”与低薪劳动力供应链的种族逻辑**  
*Michelle Buckley, Paula Chakravartty*｜2026-11-05｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/0308518x261489455](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261489455)

> **Abstract:** Within much contemporary migration policy, labor recruiters are often understood as unscrupulous and state-evading actors embedded in informal networks of brokerage who profit from the demand for cheap labor abroad. To historians and other scholars of the British imperial indenture system, however, private labor recruiters of the late 19th century are understood as firmly situated within the imperial state’s apparatus of plantation labor recruitment and management. This paper seeks to bring analyses of scholars of indenture recruitment into closer conversation with critical migration scholarship on the contemporary regulation of labor recruiters in India. Drawing insights from these literatures together with archival data primarily on the 19th century Allahabad-Trinidad and indenture migration corridor which illuminates the structure and operation of indenture recruitment networks in the region that is present-day Uttar Pradesh, we examine the colonial repertoires of recruiter regulation that form the foundations of subcontracted recruitment networks in the region today. In particular, we foreground how two mechanisms of control and regulation—pyramid subcontracting and licensing—were vital in establishing a class of informalized, commission-based recruiters at the lowest tiers of the network. Drawing upon Denise Ferreira da Silva’s notion of the racial dialectic, we highlight the enduring use of licensing and subcontracting as a “colonial racial arsenal” which has always failed to effectively regulate recruiters and their networks. We foreground how, both then and now, these tools have instead served to delineate first-mile recruiters as racial rule-breaking subjects in an otherwise orderly and fair migration regime.
>
> **摘要：** 在当代许多移民政策中，劳务招募者常被理解为嵌入非正式中介网络、肆无忌惮且规避国家监管的行动者，他们从海外对廉价劳动力的需求中牟利。然而，对于研究英国殖民契约劳工制度的历史学家及其他学者而言，19世纪末的私人劳务招募者被认为牢牢处于帝国国家的种植园劳工招募与管理机构之中。本文试图使契约劳工招募研究者的分析与关于印度当代劳务招募者监管的批判性移民研究进行更紧密的对话。我们综合这些文献的洞见，并主要利用关于19世纪阿拉哈巴德—特立尼达契约移民走廊的档案资料——这些资料揭示了今北方邦地区契约劳工招募网络的结构与运作——考察构成该地区当今分包式招募网络基础的殖民时期招募者监管手段。我们特别突出了两种控制与监管机制——金字塔式分包和许可制度——如何在网络最底层确立了一个非正式化、以佣金为基础的招募者阶层。借鉴丹尼丝·费雷拉·达席尔瓦（Denise Ferreira da Silva）的“种族辩证法”概念，我们强调许可与分包作为一种“殖民种族武器库”被持续使用，而它始终未能有效监管招募者及其网络。我们揭示，无论过去还是现在，这些工具反而都将“第一英里”招募者界定为在一个本应有序、公平的移民体制中违反规则的种族化主体。


`D1` **When peripheral cities go viral: Platform interception, digital participation and the reconfiguration of city branding**  
**当边缘城市走红网络：平台拦截、数字参与与城市品牌的重构**  
*Xiaoyue Deng, Shenjing He*｜2026-10-29｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/0308518x261491600](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261491600)

> **Abstract:** The rise of ‘internet-celebrity cities’ ( wanghong ) has become a new emblem of platform urbanism. Yet existing platform studies offer limited insight into this phenomenon as a product of digitally enabled city branding, an oversight that obscures the new dynamics that digital platforms introduce into entrepreneurial governance. To address this gap, this paper proposes a physical–virtual complementary framework for examining how platforms’ operations and local practices coalesce to (re)shape the image of cities. The framework is illustrated through a case study of Rongjiang , a peripheral Chinese city distinguished by its dramatic internet virality. In Rongjiang ’s digital branding endeavour, digital platforms reconfigured the composition, roles, and power dynamics of the stakeholders involved in city branding, extended the co-creation of city branding by enabling massive and unsupervised participation, and consolidated their own unbalanced control over selective online visibility. These findings highlight a paradox of digital empowerment in city branding: platforms enabled more extensive and decentralised branding participation while introducing and reinforcing a new, algorithm-dominated pattern of participation that redefined local agency in city branding. This research extends platform-urbanism scholarship beyond its predominantly economic and cultural discourses to engage with city branding as an integral part of the urban entrepreneurialism literature. The findings advance understanding of urban entrepreneurialism with particular attention to transforming local practices around plural participation in a platform-mediated urban age.
>
> **摘要：** “网红城市”的兴起已成为平台城市主义的新标志。然而，现有平台研究对这一作为数字化城市品牌塑造产物的现象着墨有限，这一疏漏遮蔽了数字平台为企业主义治理带来的新动态。为弥补这一不足，本文提出一个物理—虚拟互补框架，用以考察平台运作与地方实践如何结合起来（重新）塑造城市形象。该框架通过对榕江这一以戏剧性网络走红而著称的中国边缘城市的案例研究加以阐释。在榕江的数字品牌塑造过程中，数字平台重构了参与城市品牌塑造的利益相关者的构成、角色与权力关系，通过促成大规模且不受监督的参与扩展了城市品牌的共同创造，并巩固了其自身对选择性线上可见度的不均衡控制。这些发现揭示了城市品牌塑造中的数字赋权悖论：平台促成了更广泛、更去中心化的品牌参与，同时又引入并强化了一种由算法主导的新参与模式，重新定义了城市品牌塑造中的地方能动性。本研究将平台城市主义研究拓展至其以经济和文化话语为主的范畴之外，将城市品牌塑造作为城市企业主义文献的有机组成部分加以讨论。研究发现推进了对城市企业主义的理解，尤其关注平台中介的城市时代中围绕多元参与而转变的地方实践。


## Environment and Planning B

*Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science*

`M1` **AGORA cities: The hidden infrastructure shaping urban fragility**  
**AGORA城市：塑造城市脆弱性的隐性基础设施**  
*Eleonora Andreotti, Munazza Usmani, Alice Barsanti, Maurizio Napolitano*｜2026-11-17｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261509701](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261509701)

> **Abstract:** Urban services are unevenly distributed across the city, yet it is unclear when their joint presence signals fragility and when it instead outlines a neighbourhood’s vocation. For Bologna and Florence, we integrate Foursquare and OpenStreetMap points of interest with Sentinel-2-derived green indicators based on the 3–30–300 rule, cluster urban areas accordingly, and benchmark the clusters against the demographic, social, economic, and composite fragility indices produced by the municipal statistical offices, which are exogenous to the analysis. In both cities, we identify a subset of infrastructures, termed AGORA (Arts, Green environments, and Open Routes for Access and mobility), whose joint provision is associated with lower fragility. The AGORA Alignment score (AA), based on the rank correlation between AGORA endowment and composite fragility, indicates full alignment in Florence (AA = 1.00) and strong but incomplete alignment in Bologna (AA = 0.80). The remaining service categories instead show wide and heterogeneous distributions: they differentiate areas without tracking fragility and can be interpreted as expressions of urban vocations. The least fragile cluster, the only one with full AGORA endowment, hosts just 3.3% of residents in Bologna and 6.3% in Florence. The framework offers a procedure transferable to other contexts and identifies the expansion of AGORA infrastructures as a candidate lever for reducing fragility without altering existing vocations.
>
> **摘要：** 城市服务在城市中分布不均，但尚不清楚它们的共同存在何时意味着脆弱性，何时反而勾勒出一个街区的功能定位。针对博洛尼亚和佛罗伦萨，我们将Foursquare和OpenStreetMap兴趣点数据与基于“3–30–300”规则、由Sentinel-2卫星影像得出的绿化指标相结合，据此对城市区域进行聚类，并以市政统计部门编制的人口、社会、经济及综合脆弱性指数（这些指数独立于本分析之外）作为基准对聚类结果进行对照。在两座城市中，我们都识别出一组基础设施，称之为AGORA（艺术、绿色环境以及通达与移动的开放路线），其共同供给与较低的脆弱性相关。基于AGORA禀赋与综合脆弱性之间秩相关的AGORA一致性得分（AA）表明，佛罗伦萨完全一致（AA = 1.00），博洛尼亚则高度但不完全一致（AA = 0.80）。其余服务类别则呈现出广泛且异质的分布：它们能区分不同区域但并不追踪脆弱性，可被解读为城市功能定位的体现。脆弱性最低的聚类——也是唯一拥有完整AGORA禀赋的聚类——在博洛尼亚仅容纳3.3%的居民，在佛罗伦萨为6.3%。该框架提供了一套可迁移至其他情境的程序，并指出扩展AGORA基础设施可作为在不改变现有功能定位的前提下降低脆弱性的潜在杠杆。


`U1` **When does post-demolition vacancy become persistent, and how much vacant land remains? Nationwide evidence from South Korea**  
**拆除后的空置何时变得持久，又有多少空置土地留存？来自韩国全国的证据**  
*Sunjae Lee, Minju Shin, Bumjoon Kang*｜2026-11-14｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261507758](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261507758)

> **Abstract:** Vacant-land inventories show where parcels remain vacant, but not how long they have remained without built reuse after demolition. Using nationwide administrative histories of 435,702 demolished parcels in South Korea (2005–2024), we link demolition dates to subsequent occupancy approvals and estimate Kaplan–Meier still-vacant curves. We identify when these curves enter a sustained period of slow decline, yielding two measures: stabilization time (ST), when the slowdown begins, and stabilization survival (SS), the share remaining without registered built reuse at that point. Under the main three-year persistence criterion, the pooled national curve reaches this phase 1055 days after demolition, with an estimated still-vacant share of 56.4%. Among parcels without observed registered built reuse at the end of 2024, 29% had elapsed durations shorter than this benchmark. Across 1148 neighborhoods, similar ST values can correspond to different SS values, and vice versa. Neighborhood and municipal conditions are more systematically associated with SS than with ST. These findings show that the timing of slowing reuse and the amount of vacancy remaining capture different aspects of post-demolition change.
>
> **摘要：** 空置土地清单显示了哪些地块仍处于空置状态，却无法显示这些地块在拆除后有多长时间未获建设再利用。本文利用韩国全国435,702个已拆除地块（2005—2024年）的行政历史记录，将拆除日期与随后的使用许可相关联，并估计卡普兰—迈耶（Kaplan–Meier）仍空置曲线。我们识别这些曲线何时进入持续缓慢下降的阶段，由此得出两个指标：稳定化时间（ST），即放缓开始的时间点；以及稳定化存续率（SS），即该时间点仍未获登记建设再利用的地块比例。在三年持续性的主要判定标准下，全国合并曲线在拆除后1055天进入这一阶段，估计仍空置比例为56.4%。在截至2024年底仍未观察到登记建设再利用的地块中，有29%的已历时长短于这一基准。在1148个社区中，相近的ST值可能对应不同的SS值，反之亦然。社区和市级条件与SS的关联比与ST的关联更具系统性。这些发现表明，再利用放缓的时间点与剩余空置的数量反映了拆除后变化的不同方面。


`U2` **Predicting economies of scale in Brazilian municipal expenditure: Panel econometrics and interpretable machine learning**  
**预测巴西市政支出的规模经济：面板计量经济学与可解释机器学习**  
*Khaled Toffaha, Luan V. Bernardelli, Michael A. Kortt, Albert Wijeweera et al.*｜2026-11-07｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261502609](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502609)

> **Abstract:** Municipal size is a persistent question in urban planning and public finance because small jurisdictions may face high per capita costs, whereas larger jurisdictions may face congestion and administrative complexity. This study compares panel econometric and interpretable machine-learning models for real municipal operational expenditure per capita in Brazil. The analysis uses 49,228 municipality-year observations for 5,568 municipalities from 2013 to 2021 and evaluates prediction through municipality-holdout, temporal-holdout, and state-block designs. Histogram Gradient Boosting performs best for unseen municipalities and held-out states, while a municipality fixed-effects-style predictor performs best for later-year prediction of municipalities already observed in the panel. The results indicate strong economies of scale among small municipalities followed by a broad low-cost range rather than one universal optimum. Controlled econometric and spatial-error models imply minima near 143,000 to 176,000 residents, while a reduced-form pooled OLS model excluding grants implies about 232,000 residents. The strongest boosting models show no clear interior upturn within the evaluated grid, with predicted expenditure remaining within 10% of the minimum above approximately 48,000 to 53,000 residents. Grouped SHAP results rank population first, followed by grants and local economic capacity. Residual Moran tests show that unexplained expenditure differences remain spatially clustered. The findings support spatially informed expenditure benchmarking and caution against consolidation policies based on one fixed population threshold.
>
> **摘要：** 市政规模是城市规划与公共财政中一个长期存在的问题，因为小型辖区可能面临较高的人均成本，而较大的辖区则可能面临拥挤和行政复杂性。本研究比较了面板计量经济模型与可解释机器学习模型对巴西实际人均市政运营支出的预测。分析使用了2013—2021年5,568个市镇的49,228个市镇—年份观测值，并通过市镇留出、时间留出和州区块三种设计评估预测效果。直方图梯度提升模型对未见市镇和留出州的预测表现最佳，而市镇固定效应式预测器对面板中已观测市镇的后续年份预测表现最佳。结果表明，小型市镇存在显著的规模经济，随后是一个宽泛的低成本区间，而非单一的普遍最优点。控制变量的计量经济模型和空间误差模型意味着最低点在约143,000至176,000名居民附近，而排除转移支付的简化式混合OLS模型则意味着约232,000名居民。表现最强的提升模型在评估网格内未显示明显的内部回升，人口超过约48,000至53,000人后，预测支出保持在最小值的10%以内。分组SHAP结果显示人口排名第一，其次是转移支付和地方经济能力。残差Moran检验表明，未解释的支出差异仍呈空间集聚。研究结果支持基于空间信息的支出基准设定，并告诫不应依据单一固定人口门槛制定合并政策。


`M1` **Quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception: An MC Dropout approach**  
**量化城市安全感知深度学习模型的不确定性：一种MC Dropout方法**  
*Vicente Larraín, Billy Peralta, Hans Lobel*｜2026-11-05｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261504070](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261504070)

> **Abstract:** Deep learning models have revolutionized the automated analysis of urban perception. However, these models typically provide point predictions without associated uncertainty estimates, limiting their application in critical urban planning and policy decisions. This research explores the implementation of Monte Carlo Dropout (MC Dropout) as a method for quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception. Using ResNet-based convolutional neural networks, we evaluated this approach on both global urban datasets (PlacePulse 2.0) and a specific case study of Santiago, Chile, focusing specifically on the safety dimension of urban perception. Our findings reveal that MC Dropout yields per-image uncertainty estimates without requiring significant architectural modifications, alongside a modest improvement in predictive accuracy (from 66.81% to 67.64%). We show that these estimates vary systematically with urban characteristics, with distinct patterns observed between different urban districts, and that the MC Dropout spread is systematically narrower than the discrepancy between the stochastic and deterministic predictions. We are explicit about what the study does not establish: none of our analyses tests the uncertainty estimates against predictive error, so we do not claim that they are calibrated or that they rank predictions by reliability, and we specify in detail the risk–coverage protocol and pairwise proper scoring rule that such a validation would require. The potential implications for urban analytics—triage of cases for human verification, mapping the spatial distribution of predictive dispersion within cities, and improved interpretability of model decision-making—are presented as prospective applications conditional on that validation. The methodological framework established here can be adopted and extended for future studies and practical applications in urban environments.
>
> **摘要：** 深度学习模型彻底改变了城市感知的自动化分析。然而，这些模型通常只提供点预测而缺乏相应的不确定性估计，限制了其在关键城市规划与政策决策中的应用。本研究探讨将蒙特卡洛丢弃法（MC Dropout）作为量化城市安全感知深度学习模型不确定性的方法。利用基于ResNet的卷积神经网络，我们在全球城市数据集（PlacePulse 2.0）以及智利圣地亚哥的具体案例中对该方法进行了评估，重点关注城市感知中的安全维度。研究发现，MC Dropout无需对模型架构做重大修改即可得到逐张图像的不确定性估计，同时预测准确率略有提升（从66.81%提高到67.64%）。我们表明，这些估计值随城市特征系统性变化，不同城区之间呈现出不同的模式，且MC Dropout的离散范围系统性地窄于随机预测与确定性预测之间的差异。我们明确说明本研究未能证实的内容：我们的分析均未将不确定性估计与预测误差进行检验，因此我们并不声称这些估计是经过校准的，也不声称它们能够按可靠性对预测进行排序；我们详细阐述了此类验证所需的风险—覆盖协议和成对适当评分规则。对城市分析的潜在意义——对需要人工核查的案例进行分流、绘制城市内部预测离散度的空间分布图，以及提升模型决策的可解释性——均作为以该验证为前提的潜在应用予以呈现。本文建立的方法框架可在未来研究和城市环境的实际应用中被采用和拓展。


`M1` **Urban form typologies in Seoul since 1960: A framework for block-level classification and their changing prevalence over time**  
**1960年以来首尔的城市形态类型：街区尺度分类框架及其流行程度的历时变化**  
*Junhyeong Shim, Bumjoon Kang*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261502967](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502967)

> **Abstract:** This study proposes a framework for classifying urban form typologies at the block level and examining changes in their prevalence in Seoul since 1960. The study identifies major typologies aligned with urban planning trends and analyzes their morphological characteristics. Key questions addressed include: how many distinct urban form types can be identified in Seoul; what unique features define each type; and how changes in the prevalence of these types over time reflect broader urban development trends. The analytical procedure consisted of three steps. First, block units were defined based on infrastructure boundaries such as arterial roads. Second, eight indicators were established to capture the morphological features of blocks, parcels, streets, and buildings—the fundamental components of urban structure. Third, Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) clustering was applied to classify blocks with similar numerical indicators into five distinct types. The result effectively captures how many morphological features aligned with Seoul’s urban planning objectives by era. Compared with previous typologies, the study integrates multiple scales of urban form and examines changes in typology prevalence across different periods, providing a basis for interpreting the historical process of urbanization.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究提出了一个在街区尺度上对城市形态类型进行分类的框架，并考察1960年以来首尔各类型流行程度的变化。研究识别出与城市规划趋势相一致的主要类型，并分析其形态特征。研究探讨的关键问题包括：首尔可以识别出多少种不同的城市形态类型；每种类型有何独特特征；以及这些类型流行程度随时间的变化如何反映更广泛的城市发展趋势。分析过程包括三个步骤。首先，以主干道等基础设施边界为依据界定街区单元。其次，建立八项指标以刻画街区、地块、街道和建筑——城市结构的基本组成部分——的形态特征。第三，应用高斯混合模型（GMM）聚类，将数值指标相似的街区划分为五种不同类型。结果有效地揭示了不同时代的诸多形态特征如何与首尔的城市规划目标相契合。与以往的类型学相比，本研究整合了城市形态的多个尺度，并考察了不同时期类型流行程度的变化，为解读城市化的历史进程提供了基础。


`D1` **Generative AI for urban planning: Designing streetscapes from street-view imagery using diffusion models**  
**面向城市规划的生成式人工智能：利用扩散模型基于街景图像设计街道景观**  
*Yuzhou Chen, Yuebing Liang, Lingqian Hu, Kailai Sun et al.*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261500883](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500883)

> **Abstract:** Street-view imagery (SVI) is widely used to quantify key indicators of urban environment, such as greenery, sky, or road view indices. However, relatively few studies have examined controlled SVI synthesis that combines explicit target proportions of trees, sky, buildings, and roads with road-layout masks, while also testing conflicts between textual and spatial controls. To address this gap, we propose a generative multimodal AI framework that synthesizes alternative streetscapes conditioned on targeted visual metrics, enabling direct visual exploration of urban scenarios. We first construct a multimodal dataset that aligns SVIs with textual descriptions, segmentation maps, road masks, and quantitative metrics of visual elements in Chicago and Orlando. Using this dataset, we demonstrate that diffusion models can produce realistic and semantically consistent streetscape imagery while responding to both textual and imagery controls. Our quantitative evaluations show that incorporating visual controls can improve semantic consistency, reducing the LPIPS index by approximately 6% while maintaining global visual realism. In addition, overall semantic consistency increases by 23.7% in Orlando and 46.4% in Chicago, as measured by the mIoU index, with class-wise gains exceeding 100% improvement for building view indices. Streetscape generation shows directional and interpretable responses to both visual and textual prompts, and when textual and visual controls conflict, imagery controls tend to dominate, indicating a clear control hierarchy and the importance of further developing visual controls for urban scene generation. Overall, this work establishes an important benchmark for streetscape generation using SVIs and diffusion models, and illustrates how generative AI can support controllable and exploratory urban scenario visualization.
>
> **摘要：** 街景图像（SVI）被广泛用于量化城市环境的关键指标，如绿视率、天空可视率或道路可视率。然而，较少研究探讨将树木、天空、建筑和道路的明确目标比例与道路布局掩膜相结合的受控街景图像合成，并检验文本控制与空间控制之间的冲突。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了一个生成式多模态人工智能框架，以目标视觉指标为条件合成替代性街道景观，从而实现对城市情景的直接可视化探索。我们首先构建了一个多模态数据集，将芝加哥和奥兰多的街景图像与文本描述、语义分割图、道路掩膜以及视觉要素的定量指标进行对齐。利用该数据集，我们证明扩散模型能够生成逼真且语义一致的街景图像，并能同时响应文本和图像控制。定量评估表明，引入视觉控制可以提升语义一致性，使LPIPS指数降低约6%，同时保持整体视觉真实感。此外，以mIoU指数衡量，奥兰多和芝加哥的整体语义一致性分别提高了23.7%和46.4%，其中建筑可视率的类别增益超过100%。街景生成对视觉和文本提示均表现出有方向性且可解释的响应；当文本控制与视觉控制发生冲突时，图像控制往往占主导地位，这表明存在明确的控制层级，也说明进一步发展用于城市场景生成的视觉控制的重要性。总体而言，本研究为利用街景图像和扩散模型进行街景生成建立了重要基准，并展示了生成式人工智能如何支持可控的、探索性的城市情景可视化。


`U3` **What defines the linear city? Examining accessibility premises with novel flow-based linearity indices**  
**何为线性城市？基于新型流量线性指数检验可达性前提**  
*Yongki Kim*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261500494](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500494)

> **Abstract:** This study re-examines the widely held assumption that the linear city model enhances both efficiency and equity of accessibility. From a space syntax perspective, we introduce two flow-based linearity indices: the Morphological Linearity Index, which captures how strongly path-implied trips align along a single axis, and the Structural Linearity Index, which captures how strongly paths converge on shared street segments. We then relate these indices to accessibility measures that are likewise derived from network configuration alone. Applying these indices to 60 South Korean cities, we find that corridor-centered structures alone do not guarantee efficient or equitable accessibility. Networks with higher morphological linearity show lower mean detour rates yet no corresponding reduction in average trip distance, a directness-distance trade-off that can offset net efficiency gains. Higher structural linearity is associated with both greater detour rates and longer trip distances, suggesting that corridor-centered designs can undermine overall accessibility. By translating the “linear city” into measurable indices, this study provides an operational basis for evidence-based road network planning and assessment.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究重新检视一个被广泛接受的假设，即线性城市模式能够同时提升可达性的效率与公平。基于空间句法视角，我们提出两个基于流量的线性指数：形态线性指数，用于衡量路径所隐含的出行沿单一轴线排列的程度；结构线性指数，用于衡量路径在共享街段上汇聚的程度。随后，我们将这些指数与同样仅从网络构型中推导出的可达性指标相关联。将这些指数应用于韩国60个城市后，我们发现仅凭以走廊为中心的结构并不能保证高效或公平的可达性。形态线性较高的网络平均绕行率较低，但平均出行距离并未相应缩短，这种直达性与距离之间的权衡可能抵消净效率收益。较高的结构线性则与更高的绕行率和更长的出行距离相关，表明以走廊为中心的设计可能削弱整体可达性。通过将“线性城市”转化为可测度的指数，本研究为基于证据的道路网络规划与评估提供了可操作的基础。


`M1` **Joining models together**  
**将模型联结起来**  
*Michael Batty*｜2026-10-30｜Vol. 53(9)｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261502365](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502365)

`M1` **Assessing the association between street view–derived perceived liveliness and street thefts**  
**评估基于街景感知的街道活力与街头盗窃之间的关联**  
*Hanlin Zhou, Lin Liu, Minxuan Lan, Devin Yongzhao Wu et al.*｜2026-10-29｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261502943](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502943)

> **Abstract:** Crime geographers are attentive to how the spatial characteristics of the environment affect criminal behaviors. Recently, scholars have found that aside from physical and social attributes, the perception of the environment, such as safety perception, is also associated with crime occurrence. However, perceived liveliness, a visual perceptual proxy for potential routine activity settings that are closely linked to routine activity theory and crime pattern theory, is rarely explored in prior research. This study examines the association between perceived liveliness and street thefts in the City of Cincinnati, the United States. The liveliness perception scores are derived from street view imagery using a support vector machine model trained on the MIT Place Pulse V2 crowdsourcing image survey. Negative binomial regression models are used to test the relationships between perceived liveliness and street theft, with social and physical environmental attributes controlled. Results show that perceived liveliness significantly improves model performance and is positively associated with street theft. This study provides the first evidence that perceived liveliness helps explain crimes. The positive relationship between perceived liveliness of the built environment and crime adds a new dimension to the routine activity theory and the crime pattern theory. It also offers a scalable and cost-effective measure that can assist urban planners and law enforcement in identifying potential theft hotspots using readily available street view imagery.
>
> **摘要：** 犯罪地理学者关注环境的空间特征如何影响犯罪行为。近来，学者们发现，除物理和社会属性外，对环境的感知（如安全感知）也与犯罪发生相关。然而，感知活力作为潜在日常活动场景的一种视觉感知代理变量，与日常活动理论和犯罪模式理论密切相关，却在以往研究中鲜有探讨。本研究考察美国辛辛那提市感知活力与街头盗窃之间的关联。活力感知得分利用基于MIT Place Pulse V2众包图像调查训练的支持向量机模型从街景图像中提取。在控制社会和物理环境属性的条件下，采用负二项回归模型检验感知活力与街头盗窃之间的关系。结果表明，感知活力显著提升了模型表现，并与街头盗窃呈正相关。本研究首次提供了感知活力有助于解释犯罪的证据。建成环境的感知活力与犯罪之间的正向关系为日常活动理论和犯罪模式理论增添了新的维度。它还提供了一种可扩展且具成本效益的测度方法，可借助易于获取的街景图像帮助城市规划者和执法部门识别潜在的盗窃热点。


`D1` **Exploratory geoplay in metaverse fieldwork: Avatar movement as learning data for geographic understanding**  
**元宇宙野外考察中的探索性地理游戏：作为地理理解学习数据的化身移动**  
*Kotaro Iizuka, Hiroyuki Yamauchi, Takuro Ogura*｜2026-10-26｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261501338](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261501338)

> **Abstract:** Geogames are increasingly recognized as playful systems based on real-world geographic data and spatial reasoning, yet open-ended geographic play remains underexplored. This study examines immersive virtual fieldwork as exploratory geoplay, where participants engage with geographic environments through autonomous observation, movement, and interaction rather than scores, competition, or fixed missions. We developed a multiplayer metaverse environment based on a life-scale 3D reconstruction of the Byobugaura Sea Cliff in Japan. Sixty-two participants explored it in two rounds: one unguided and one supported by light interpretive prompts. Avatar positions were recorded using the YAIBA behavior-logging system and analyzed through space-time clustering, PCA, trajectory-grid aggregation, and cluster-duration analysis. During unguided exploration, participants clustered around geomorphologically significant areas, while interpretive supports shifted visits toward breakwater and terrace zones. Cluster durations increased in the smaller sub-instance but remained stable in the larger main instance. Questionnaire responses reflected positive experiences and perceived behavioral changes. By framing avatar movement as learning data, while recognizing that movement does not directly measure knowledge acquisition, this study proposes exploratory geoplay as a model for studying locational attention and geographic inquiry within immersive environments.
>
> **摘要：** 地理游戏日益被视为基于真实世界地理数据和空间推理的游戏化系统，但开放式的地理游戏仍未得到充分探讨。本研究将沉浸式虚拟野外考察视为探索性地理游戏，参与者通过自主观察、移动和互动而非分数、竞争或固定任务来接触地理环境。我们基于日本屏风浦海崖的真实尺度三维重建，开发了一个多人元宇宙环境。62名参与者分两轮进行了探索：一轮无引导，另一轮辅以轻度的解说提示。参与者的化身位置通过YAIBA行为记录系统进行记录，并采用时空聚类、主成分分析、轨迹网格聚合和聚类持续时间分析等方法进行分析。在无引导探索中，参与者聚集在具有重要地貌意义的区域；而解说支持则使访问转向防波堤和阶地区域。聚类持续时间在较小的子实例中有所增加，但在较大的主实例中保持稳定。问卷反馈反映出积极的体验和自我感知的行为变化。通过将化身移动视为学习数据，同时承认移动并不能直接衡量知识获取，本研究提出探索性地理游戏作为研究沉浸式环境中位置注意力与地理探究的一种模式。


`M1` **A national dataset of downtown business district boundaries for pre-automobile U.S. cities and towns**  
**美国前汽车时代城镇市中心商业区边界的全国数据集**  
*Andrew J. Van Leuven*｜2026-10-26｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261500874](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500874)

> **Abstract:** The downtown business district is a fundamental unit of analysis in urban revitalization research, yet no nationally consistent, publicly available dataset of downtown boundaries exists for small and mid-sized U.S. cities and towns. This paper introduces an open data product containing 6,551 downtown polygons for pre-automobile-era places spanning 47 contiguous states. The dataset is constructed from a fully reproducible, open-source R workflow: a national universe of historically significant places is assembled using historical census records, contemporary population data, and metropolitan classification criteria; points-of-interest data from the Overture Maps Foundation are collected within each place boundary; and a kernel density estimation algorithm identifies the highest-concentration commercial cluster as the downtown core. The resulting boundaries are validated against Main Street program districts, which the polygons cover at high rates in the two states examined. The polygons are deposited at Harvard Dataverse under a CC0 1.0 license, with all R code available on GitHub. The dataset supports research on downtown revitalization, Main Street commercial activity, and the economic geography of small and mid-sized American cities.
>
> **摘要：** 市中心商业区是城市复兴研究中的基本分析单元，但对于美国中小城市和城镇，目前尚不存在全国统一且公开可用的市中心边界数据集。本文介绍了一个开放数据产品，其中包含覆盖美国本土47个州的前汽车时代地点的6,551个市中心多边形。该数据集通过完全可复现的开源R工作流构建：利用历史人口普查记录、当代人口数据和大都市区分类标准，汇编出具有历史意义地点的全国总体；在每个地点边界内收集来自Overture Maps基金会的兴趣点数据；并采用核密度估计算法将集中度最高的商业集群识别为市中心核心。所得边界经与“主街”（Main Street）计划街区进行验证，在所考察的两个州中，这些多边形对计划街区的覆盖率很高。多边形数据以CC0 1.0许可存放于哈佛Dataverse，全部R代码均可在GitHub上获取。该数据集可支持有关市中心复兴、主街商业活动以及美国中小城市经济地理的研究。


`M1` **Optimising resource management in data-scarce environments: A modelling approach for Greater Cairo**  
**数据稀缺环境下的资源管理优化：大开罗的建模方法**  
*Eman Abou Ziyan, Richard Kingston, Joe Ravetz*｜2026-10-22｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261500938](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500938)

> **Abstract:** Pressing environmental challenges present significant obstacles to cities’ sustainable development and underscore the need for effective resource management. However, resource management studies often overlook or oversimplify the spatial and social dimensions of resource use, limiting their ability to target interventions to specific population groups and integrate them into urban planning. Addressing this requires disaggregated data that capture spatial variation and interdependencies between socio-economic factors and resource consumption. Yet such data are often scarce in Global South contexts, where data limitations frequently hinder meaningful resource management research. This research develops a hybrid multi-scale analysis framework that integrates Spatial Microsimulation, Multi-Scale Geographically Weighted Regression (MGWR), and Geodemographic Classification to help researchers address data limitations and generate policy-relevant insights. The framework offers three contributions: methodological, empirical, and policy translation. The research provides the first systematic, household-level analysis of food-water-energy consumption patterns across Greater Cairo. Spatial microsimulation creates robust synthetic household datasets by integrating multiple surveys, while MGWR reveals spatially varying drivers of consumption. The geodemographic classification delivers the first empirically grounded typology of Greater Cairo households based on demographic, socio-economic, and consumption characteristics. Lastly, the applied probability-based allocation of clusters to neighbourhoods offers an indicative tool for exploring differentiated tariff structures and area-specific demand management, validated through expert workshops. By addressing Greater Cairo’s data limitations, the framework offers a replicable approach for other cities facing similar constraints, supporting the transition to evidence-based, spatially differentiated resource management in the Global South.
>
> **摘要：** 紧迫的环境挑战给城市可持续发展带来了重大障碍，凸显了有效资源管理的必要性。然而，资源管理研究常常忽视或过度简化资源使用的空间与社会维度，限制了其将干预措施精准对准特定人群并纳入城市规划的能力。解决这一问题需要能够捕捉空间差异以及社会经济因素与资源消费之间相互依赖关系的分解数据。然而，此类数据在全球南方往往十分稀缺，数据局限常常阻碍有意义的资源管理研究。本研究构建了一个混合多尺度分析框架，整合空间微观模拟、多尺度地理加权回归（MGWR）和地理人口分类，以帮助研究者应对数据局限并生成与政策相关的见解。该框架提供了三方面贡献：方法论、实证和政策转化。本研究首次对大开罗地区的食物—水—能源消费模式进行了系统的家庭层面分析。空间微观模拟通过整合多项调查构建了稳健的合成家庭数据集，而MGWR则揭示了消费驱动因素的空间差异。地理人口分类首次基于人口、社会经济和消费特征，为大开罗家庭提供了有实证依据的类型划分。最后，所应用的基于概率的聚类—街区分配方法，为探索差异化的收费结构和分区需求管理提供了一种指示性工具，并通过专家研讨会进行了验证。通过应对大开罗的数据局限，该框架为面临类似约束的其他城市提供了可复制的方法，支持全球南方向基于证据、空间差异化的资源管理转型。


`U3` **Carbon emission and health impacts of remote working: A case study of the Helsinki capital region**  
**远程办公的碳排放与健康影响：以赫尔辛基首都地区为例**  
*Alonso Espinosa Mireles de Villafranca, Petr Anashin, Bhagya Nagakamkanamge, Phil Symonds et al.*｜2026-10-21｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23998083261500928](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500928)

> **Abstract:** Remote working can reduce commuting-related carbon emissions but may also lower population physical activity levels. This study examines these trade-offs in the Helsinki Capital Region by modelling changes in commuting and worktime travel under three scenarios: no remote work, current levels, and full potential uptake. We combine demographic data, the Finnish National Travel Survey, public transport schedules, and official transport demand models to estimate impacts on CO 2 emissions and active travel. Commuting volumes are taken from demographic data containing home and workplace information of workers, and worktime trips are modelled using an agent-based synthetic population model. Under the full remote work scenario, commuting-related emissions decrease by approximately 72.9 tonnes per day, a 16.7% reduction compared to current levels. However, rebound trips during working hours offset 71.3% of these potential savings, even after accounting for reduced workplace-based trips. Health impacts were assessed using the WHO Health Economic Assessment Tool. Compared to a baseline with no remote work, current remote working levels are associated with approximately 42 additional premature deaths annually due to reduced physical activity, with a further 31 deaths under full remote work conditions. Conversely, if commuting time were repurposed for walking or cycling, up to 71 premature deaths could be avoided under current conditions and 137 under full remote working. The findings highlight the interrelationship between environmental and health dimensions of remote work. While remote working involves clear trade-offs, integrated policy approaches that combine emission reduction strategies and promotion of physical activity while limiting rebound travel are needed to achieve substantial co-benefits.
>
> **摘要：** 远程办公可以减少与通勤相关的碳排放，但也可能降低人口的身体活动水平。本研究在赫尔辛基首都地区考察这些权衡，对三种情景下通勤和工作时间出行的变化进行建模：无远程办公、当前水平和全部潜力实现。我们结合人口数据、芬兰全国出行调查、公共交通时刻表以及官方交通需求模型，估算其对二氧化碳排放和主动出行的影响。通勤量取自包含劳动者住所与工作地点信息的人口数据，工作时间出行则采用基于主体的合成人口模型进行模拟。在完全远程办公情景下，通勤相关排放每天减少约72.9吨，较当前水平下降16.7%。然而，即使考虑到基于工作地点的出行减少，工作时间内的反弹出行仍抵消了这些潜在减排量的71.3%。健康影响采用世界卫生组织健康经济评估工具（HEAT）进行评估。与无远程办公的基准相比，当前远程办公水平因身体活动减少每年约导致42例额外过早死亡，在完全远程办公条件下还将再增加31例。相反，如果将通勤时间转用于步行或骑行，在当前条件下最多可避免71例过早死亡，在完全远程办公条件下可避免137例。研究结果凸显了远程办公的环境维度与健康维度之间的相互关系。尽管远程办公涉及明显的权衡，但仍需采取将减排策略与促进身体活动相结合、同时限制反弹出行的综合政策路径，以实现可观的协同效益。


## Environment and Planning C

*Environment and Planning C: Politics and Space*

`P1` **A climate of countering extremism? Exploring the enrolment of climate activism into UK counter-extremism policy discourses**  
**反极端主义的气候？探究气候行动主义被纳入英国反极端主义政策话语的过程**  
*Andrew Telford*｜2026-11-14｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261507567](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261507567)

> **Abstract:** Climate activists are increasingly branded as ‘extremists’, ‘terrorists’, and ‘zealots’ by political actors hostile to decarbonisation, including the enrolment of climate activism into preventing/countering violent extremism (P/CVE) policies. Drawing inspiration from political geographic theorisations of territory ( Elden, 2010 ) and security ( Amoore, 2013 ), I conceptualise (counter-)extremism as a political technology which partially functions through the construction of spectrums (calculative discursive tools which take the form of an ‘array’ of possible positions). The paper draws on a discourse-historical analysis (DHA) and semi-structured interviews with activists based in the UK. I contend that two spectrums are constructed, each of which works according to a spatial logic: the first is an ideological spectrum, which positions climate activists as part of an extreme ‘far left’; and second is a spectrum of political practices which proximises physical violence to climate activists. I conclude with reflections on the implications of counter-extremism for climate activism, particularly in the context of the increasing repression of climate (and other) activists worldwide.
>
> **摘要：** 气候活动人士日益被敌视脱碳的政治行动者贴上“极端分子”“恐怖分子”和“狂热分子”的标签，其中包括将气候行动主义纳入预防/打击暴力极端主义（P/CVE）政策。受政治地理学对领土（Elden, 2010）和安全（Amoore, 2013）理论化的启发，我将（反）极端主义概念化为一种政治技术，它部分地通过构建“谱系”（以一系列可能立场“阵列”形式出现的计算性话语工具）来运作。本文基于话语—历史分析（DHA）以及对英国活动人士的半结构化访谈。我认为，存在两种被构建的谱系，各自按照一种空间逻辑运作：第一种是意识形态谱系，将气候活动人士定位为极端“极左”的一部分；第二种是政治实践谱系，将身体暴力与气候活动人士拉近。最后，我反思了反极端主义对气候行动主义的影响，尤其是在全球范围内对气候（及其他）活动人士的压制日益加剧的背景下。


`U2` **Branding cities or citizens? Virtue-based branding as a technology of subjectification in Louisville**  
**塑造城市品牌还是塑造市民？路易斯维尔以美德为基础的品牌塑造作为一种主体化技术**  
*Ekramul Islam*｜2026-11-12｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261499130](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261499130)

> **Abstract:** City branding is increasingly understood as shaping not only how cities are seen but who their residents become. This paper contributes to the growing scholarship that conceptualizes place branding as a technology of subjectification, arguing that when a city is branded with a human attribute, it is in effect its citizens who are branded. Drawing on a qualitative case study of Compassionate Louisville, the research traces how compassion was institutionalized as a governing discourse following the city’s formal adoption of the brand identity. The study reveals how the brand narrative operates simultaneously as a moral ideal, a performative expectation, and a political resource used to legitimize policy and manage social contradictions. By framing compassion as an apolitical, universal virtue, the brand establishes a disciplinary framework that encourages self-regulation and moral conformity while depoliticizing systemic social suffering. The paper contributes to debates within political geography and urban studies by demonstrating how virtue-based branding acts as an affective technology of power, reshaping urban subjectivities.
>
> **摘要：** 城市品牌塑造日益被理解为不仅影响城市如何被看待，也影响其居民成为什么样的人。本文为将地方品牌塑造概念化为主体化技术的日益增长的研究作出贡献，认为当一座城市被冠以某种人类特质的品牌时，实际上被打上品牌烙印的是其市民。基于对“慈悲路易斯维尔”（Compassionate Louisville）的定性案例研究，本研究追溯了该市正式采纳这一品牌身份后，慈悲如何被制度化为一种治理话语。研究揭示了品牌叙事如何同时作为一种道德理想、一种表演性期待以及一种用于使政策合法化和管理社会矛盾的政治资源而运作。通过将慈悲框定为一种非政治的普世美德，该品牌建立起一套规训框架，鼓励自我规制与道德服从，同时使系统性社会苦难去政治化。本文通过展示以美德为基础的品牌塑造如何作为一种情感性权力技术重塑城市主体性，为政治地理学和城市研究中的相关讨论作出贡献。


`S1` **On the intersection of Black geographies and policy mobilities: Tracing circuits of anti-racism in Newcastle upon Tyne**  
**论黑人地理学与政策流动性的交叉：追踪泰恩河畔纽卡斯尔的反种族主义回路**  
*Astrid Wood*｜2026-11-12｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261502934](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261502934)

> **Abstract:** This paper reflects theoretically on the intersection of Black geographies and policy mobilities by tracing the historical and contemporary movements of Black activists, intellectuals and anti-racist struggles in Newcastle upon Tyne. While Black geographies foregrounds Black spatial imaginaries, resistance and epistemologies rooted in racial capitalism and coloniality, it has insufficiently interrogated how racialized policy formations and their circulations function transjurisdictionally. Likewise, policy mobilities, which charts the movement and mutation of urban policy models across contexts, offers an opportunity to expose the racialized structures underpinning these movements. This paper develops a conceptual approach that examines how race and power circulate through circuits of racialized spatial practices, Black actors and political thought and anti-racist struggles. The paper traces how Black individuals, ideas and infrastructures – routed through historical visits by Martin Luther King Jr., Frederick Douglass and Muhammad Ali as well as through contemporary cultural programs like Freedom City and protest movements like Black Lives Matter – are mobilized within and beyond the urban. In so doing, it advances a global, relational understanding of Black spatial politics that provokes a rethinking of urban theory’s Euro-American biases.
>
> **摘要：** 本文通过追踪泰恩河畔纽卡斯尔黑人活动家、知识分子和反种族主义斗争的历史与当代流动，从理论上反思黑人地理学与政策流动性的交叉。虽然黑人地理学突出了植根于种族资本主义和殖民性的黑人空间想象、抵抗与认识论，但它对种族化政策形态及其流通如何跨辖区运作的追问尚显不足。同样，描绘城市政策模式跨情境流动与变异的政策流动性研究，也为揭示支撑这些流动的种族化结构提供了契机。本文发展了一种概念路径，考察种族与权力如何通过种族化空间实践、黑人行动者与政治思想以及反种族主义斗争的回路而流通。文章追踪了黑人个体、思想和基础设施——经由马丁·路德·金、弗雷德里克·道格拉斯和穆罕默德·阿里的历史性访问，以及“自由之城”（Freedom City）等当代文化项目和“黑人的命也是命”等抗议运动——如何在城市内外被动员起来。由此，本文推进了对黑人空间政治的全球性、关系性理解，促使人们重新思考城市理论中的欧美中心偏见。


`P1` **Saving lives or freeing territory? Sacred values and tragic trade-offs in wartime Ukraine**  
**拯救生命还是收复领土？战时乌克兰的神圣价值与悲剧性权衡**  
*Gerard Toal, Karina Korostelina, John O’Loughlin*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261499153](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261499153)

> **Abstract:** President Zelensky has asserted: “We do not trade our lands and our people.” Both territorial integrity and human lives are considered ‘sacred values’ in wartime Ukraine, values beyond cost-benefit and exchange calculations. Yet, the human costs of continuing war to retake territory are so great in Ukraine that these sacred values are forced into a tragic trade-off. Using a survey of frontline Ukrainians from 2022 made up of residents and displaced persons equally (n = 1800), this paper shows that how the ‘lives versus territory’ dilemma divided respondents. We examined the degree to which wartime hatred of the Russian leadership and material insecurity explain people’s choices, and the role that personal stress plays as a mediator for these attitudes. The results reveal that respondents with higher levels of stress were more likely to prioritize saving lives over freeing territory. Displaced persons were also more likely to prioritize lives. The respondent’s material insecurity had modest impacts. For those most stressed during wartime, lives come before land.
>
> **摘要：** 泽连斯基总统曾宣称：“我们不拿我们的土地和人民做交易。”在战时乌克兰，领土完整与人的生命都被视为“神圣价值”，即超越成本—收益和交换计算的价值。然而，为收复领土而继续战争所付出的人员代价在乌克兰如此巨大，以致这些神圣价值被迫陷入悲剧性的权衡。本文利用2022年对乌克兰前线地区民众开展的一项调查（n = 1800，居民与流离失所者各占一半），展示了“生命与领土”困境如何使受访者产生分歧。我们考察了战时对俄罗斯领导层的仇恨和物质不安全在多大程度上解释了人们的选择，以及个人压力在这些态度中所起的中介作用。结果显示，压力水平较高的受访者更倾向于将拯救生命置于收复领土之上。流离失所者也更倾向于优先考虑生命。受访者的物质不安全的影响较为有限。对于战时承受压力最大的人而言，生命先于土地。


`E2` **The material infrastructure of privatization, dispossession and resistance at the Zambian land frontier**  
**赞比亚土地边疆私有化、剥夺与抵抗的物质基础设施**  
*Linus Rosén*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261502933](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261502933)

> **Abstract:** The privatization of communal lands continues to be a central tenet of capitalist and settler-colonial appropriations with devastating implications for the dispossessed and land denied. This article traces how dispossession and resistance become bound up in the material infrastructure of privatization – fences, concrete, rebar and survey lines – and the colonial histories sedimented within them. In dialogue with legal-geographical work on property and materiality, I show how such artefacts become the nodes through which law’s abstraction gives way to struggle; once law is materialized in objects, these become susceptible to alteration and enrolment in new lives of legal association that can subvert – and creatively invert – the power trafficked through property. The analysis draws from ethnographic fieldwork in Mulonga village, central Zambia, where a privatization scheme evolved into a localized struggle between creative cultivators who reworked the things of property to resist displacement, and a bureaucracy that struggled to make them perform the political work assigned to them. Abstract property, I conclude, must be read alongside the contingency and resistant reworkings of its material articulation, which creates new legal surfaces upon which people can act, redirect legal signs, and unsettle translations through which private property achieves exclusionary effect.
>
> **摘要：** 公共土地的私有化仍是资本主义和定居者殖民主义占有的核心原则，对被剥夺者和被拒绝获得土地的人造成了毁灭性影响。本文追溯了剥夺与抵抗如何与私有化的物质基础设施——围栏、混凝土、钢筋和测量线——以及沉积于其中的殖民历史相互交织。在与关于财产与物质性的法律地理学研究对话中，我展示了这些人造物如何成为法律的抽象性让位于斗争的节点：一旦法律被物化于物体之中，这些物体便容易被改变，并被纳入新的法律关联生活，从而颠覆乃至创造性地反转通过财产所传递的权力。分析基于在赞比亚中部穆隆加村的民族志田野调查，在那里，一项私有化计划演变为一场地方性斗争：一方是富有创造力的耕作者，他们改造财产之物以抵抗迁离；另一方是官僚机构，它们难以让这些物体发挥被赋予的政治作用。我的结论是，必须将抽象财产与其物质表达的偶然性和抵抗性改造一并解读，这种改造创造出新的法律界面，人们可以在其上行动、改变法律符号的指向，并扰乱私有财产借以实现排他效果的转译过程。


`T1` **Walter Benjamin on natural history and place**  
**瓦尔特·本雅明论自然史与地方**  
*Jordan Daniels*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261494364](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261494364)

> **Abstract:** Taking his radio broadcast on the Mississippi flood of 1927 as a point of departure, this article argues that Walter Benjamin has much to contribute to current debates concerning space, nature, and place. Benjamin’s episode on the devastating flood in the American South was his final transmission for Berlin Radio’s Youth Hour in 1932, and it concludes a series of episodes on disasters that are both natural and social at once. This article situates the radio broadcast in relation to two catastrophic ways of seeing: first, in Benjamin’s work on the German mourning play , where the spatializing function of allegory transposes the temporal events of history into a spatial image, and second, in the weakly messianic vision of history as “one single catastrophe.” Drawing on these reorienting modes of vision, I argue that Benjamin’s curation of ancient and recent catastrophes across the world prompts his young listeners to recognize interlinked moments of danger spanning both historical time and global space.
>
> **摘要：** 本文以瓦尔特·本雅明关于1927年密西西比洪水的广播节目为出发点，认为本雅明对当前关于空间、自然与地方的讨论有诸多贡献。本雅明这期关于美国南方毁灭性洪水的节目是他1932年在柏林电台“青年时段”的最后一次播出，也为一系列关于兼具自然性与社会性的灾难的节目画上了句号。本文将这一广播节目置于两种“灾难性的观看方式”之中加以考察：其一是本雅明关于德意志悲苦剧的著作，其中寓言的空间化功能将历史的时间性事件转置为空间图像；其二是将历史视为“唯一一场灾难”的微弱弥赛亚式视野。借助这些重新定向的观看模式，我认为，本雅明对世界各地古代与近期灾难的编排，促使他的年轻听众认识到跨越历史时间与全球空间、相互关联的危险时刻。


`E1` **Producing spatial justice: Scalar governance and territorial differentiation in coastal China’s nuclear development**  
**生产空间正义：中国沿海核电开发中的尺度治理与地域差异化**  
*Xiuyun Yang, Kewei Huang, Qiuping Chen*｜2026-10-28｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261500382](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261500382)

> **Abstract:** Nuclear infrastructure development highlights an important empirical case in environmental governance: nuclear facilities concentrate environmental and social burdens locally, yet many projects proceed without sustained opposition. In the context of China, this paper argues that such outcomes are produced through county-level scalar governance strategies. Focusing on the Zhangzhou Nuclear Power Project, the study reconstructs how local authorities designed four concentric governance zones (20 km, 10 km, 2 km, 200 m) and calibrated distance-based interventions—compensation and relocation, tiered emergency planning and monitoring, graded communication and participation, and differentiated infrastructure and welfare investments—so that governance intensity corresponded to spatial exposure. Drawing on archival materials, policy documents, and semi-structured interviews with officials and project actors, corroborated by administrative records and implementation milestones, the analysis shows that scalar governance pursued distributive, procedural, and recognition objectives across zones. Importantly, the absence of organized resistance is interpreted not as conflict resolution but as the managed incorporation of grievances into institutional channels within a constrained political environment. By reframing nuclear legitimacy as the outcome of territorially differentiated state action, the paper contributes to debates on territory, scale, and environmental governance and positioning spatial justice as a conditional mechanism for managing uneven infrastructural risk.
>
> **摘要：** 核基础设施开发是环境治理中的一个重要经验案例：核设施将环境与社会负担集中于地方，但许多项目在推进过程中并未遭遇持续的反对。本文以中国为背景，认为这种结果是通过县级尺度治理策略生产出来的。本研究聚焦漳州核电项目，重构了地方政府如何设计四个同心治理圈层（20公里、10公里、2公里、200米），并校准基于距离的干预措施——补偿与搬迁、分级应急规划与监测、分层沟通与参与，以及差异化的基础设施与福利投资——使治理强度与空间暴露程度相对应。基于档案材料、政策文件以及对官员和项目相关行动者的半结构化访谈，并辅以行政记录和实施节点加以印证，分析表明，尺度治理在各圈层中追求分配性、程序性和承认性目标。重要的是，有组织抵抗的缺席并不被解读为冲突的化解，而是在受限的政治环境中将不满有管理地纳入制度渠道。通过将核电合法性重新界定为地域差异化国家行动的结果，本文对有关领土、尺度与环境治理的讨论有所贡献，并将空间正义定位为管理不均衡基础设施风险的一种有条件机制。


`E1` **Contested spatialities of renewable energy expansion—Introduction to the theme issue**  
**可再生能源扩张的争议空间性——专题导言**  
*Thilo Wiertz, Benjamin Schuetze, Rosa Lehmann, Alke Jenss et al.*｜2026-10-21｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261497568](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261497568)

> **Abstract:** Renewable energy increasingly complements fossil fuels in meeting rising global energy demand. Yet its rollout is neither even nor free of conflict. This theme issue foregrounds the spatialities and scales of renewable energy infrastructures and examines the deeply contested nature of energy transition efforts. Drawing on case studies from Morocco, Jordan, Namibia, Mexico, Brazil, Peru and Ecuador, the contributions offer insights into recent regional developments and highlight transregional and cross-scalar interrelations. They connect place-based research on contested energy projects in political ecology, geography and anthropology with nation-focused analyses of energy transition policy, and approach scale as an unfolding field of contestation. In doing so, they challenge notions of a homogeneous “local” in analyses of energy conflict and move beyond methodological nationalism while still recognizing the significance of the national scale. Together, the theme issue demonstrates the value of relational approaches for disentangling and understanding the contested geographies of contemporary renewable energy expansion.
>
> **摘要：** 可再生能源在满足日益增长的全球能源需求方面日益成为化石燃料的补充。然而，其推广既不均衡，也并非没有冲突。本专题突出可再生能源基础设施的空间性与尺度，考察能源转型努力深刻的争议性质。各篇文章借助来自摩洛哥、约旦、纳米比亚、墨西哥、巴西、秘鲁和厄瓜多尔的案例研究，提供了对近期区域发展的洞见，并突出跨区域和跨尺度的相互关联。它们将政治生态学、地理学和人类学中关于争议性能源项目的地方性研究，与聚焦国家的能源转型政策分析相联系，并将尺度视为一个不断展开的争夺场域。由此，它们挑战了能源冲突分析中同质化“地方”的观念，在承认国家尺度重要性的同时超越了方法论民族主义。总体而言，本专题展示了关系性方法在梳理和理解当代可再生能源扩张的争议地理方面的价值。


`U2` **Institutional closure in development governance: The repositioning of local voice**  
**发展治理中的制度性封闭：地方声音的重新定位**  
*József Golovics, Márton Katona*｜2026-10-18｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/23996544261500369](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261500369)

> **Abstract:** Large-scale industrial projects linked to the green transition can proceed through accelerated and procedurally insulated authorization while formal opportunities for participation or contestation remain available. This article develops the concept of institutional closure to denote configurations in which competence allocation, authorization design, and procedural sequencing leave locally accessible intervention weakly aligned with the arenas and stages at which consequential development choices remain open. Using a structured qualitative comparison of six battery-related projects across five Hungarian municipalities, the analysis reconstructs authorization arrangements and traces local voice across political, participatory, administrative, and legal arenas. The comparison identifies patterned but non-deterministic variation among three predominant trajectories: juridified voice, in which legal and administrative arenas gain relative importance; procedural exhaustion, in which repeated engagement loses timely leverage over core authorization; and managed engagement, in which involvement remains concentrated on implementation or mitigation. Across the cases, local voice persisted without following a uniform trajectory or retaining uniform effectiveness. The findings illuminate a recurrent tension between speed and coordination on the one hand and deliberation and contestability on the other in green transition-linked industrial development. The framework provides a basis for examining other fast-track or procedurally insulated development settings.
>
> **摘要：** 与绿色转型相关的大型工业项目可能通过加速且在程序上相互隔离的审批得以推进，而正式的参与或抗争机会依然存在。本文提出“制度性封闭”概念，用以指称这样一种配置：权限分配、审批设计和程序排序使得地方可及的干预途径与重大发展选择仍然开放的场域和阶段之间缺乏有效对接。通过对匈牙利五个市镇六个电池相关项目进行结构化定性比较，本文重建了审批安排，并追踪了地方声音在政治、参与、行政和法律等场域中的表达。比较分析识别出三种主要轨迹之间有规律但非决定性的差异：司法化的声音，即法律与行政场域的相对重要性上升；程序性耗竭，即反复参与逐渐失去对核心审批的及时影响力；以及受管理的参与，即参与仍集中于实施或缓解环节。在各案例中，地方声音持续存在，但既未遵循统一的轨迹，也未保持一致的有效性。研究结果揭示了在与绿色转型相关的工业发展中，速度与协调同审议与可争议性之间反复出现的张力。该框架为考察其他快速通道或程序隔离的发展情境提供了基础。

## Progress in Human Geography

`D1` **Geographies of production I: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence**  
**生产地理学I：生成式与智能体人工智能**  
*Martina Fuchs*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/03091325261501157](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261501157)

> **Abstract:** Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) has increasingly shaped the landscapes of production since the early 2020s. This report reviews the scholarly debate on geographies of production and includes further developments in the spatiality of digitalisation. It draws on literature on firms that develop and offer AI as a product innovation (geographies of production of AI) and firms that implement and use AI (geographies of production with AI). The report highlights current trends, reveals research gaps, and finally addresses some implications for public debate.
>
> **摘要：** 自2020年代初以来，生成式与智能体人工智能（AI）日益塑造着生产格局。本报告评述了关于生产地理学的学术讨论，并涵盖数字化空间性方面的最新进展。报告借鉴了关于开发并提供AI作为产品创新的企业（AI的生产地理学）以及实施和使用AI的企业（借助AI的生产地理学）的文献。报告重点阐述了当前趋势，揭示了研究空白，并最后探讨了其对公共讨论的若干启示。


`U1` **Towards a new age of renting: Reimagining housing geographies beyond generation rent**  
**迈向租房新时代：超越“租房一代”重新想象住房地理**  
*Santiago del Río*｜2026-10-27｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/03091325261500694](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261500694)

> **Abstract:** This paper critically reviews post-2008 housing geographies while reframing the field around the socio-spatial implications and possibilities of enduring housing insecurity. I introduce the new age of renting as a politico-economic conjuncture and demographic shift marked by an ageing and increasingly heterogeneous renter population. Against the logics of homeownership ideology, family-mediated asset-based welfare, and the digital governance of rental markets, I examine trajectories obscured by the generation rent framing as a window onto unfolding, though not inevitable, precarious futures. From this vantage point, I explore how changes in tenants’ social composition create possibilities to challenge housing structures and reimagine futures.
>
> **摘要：** 本文批判性地回顾2008年后的住房地理研究，并围绕持久性住房不安全的社会空间影响与可能性重新构建该领域。我提出“租房新时代”这一概念，将其视为一种以租户群体日益老龄化和异质化为标志的政治经济态势与人口结构转变。针对住房自有意识形态、以家庭为中介的资产型福利以及租赁市场数字化治理的逻辑，我考察了被“租房一代”框架所遮蔽的发展轨迹，以此作为观察正在展开但并非不可避免的不稳定未来的窗口。从这一视角出发，我探讨了租户社会构成的变化如何为挑战住房结构和重新想象未来创造可能。


## Dialogues in Human Geography

`P1` **Cartographies of sacrifice: Mnemonic geography, martyr commemoration and the making of national space in palestine**  
**牺牲的制图学：记忆地理、烈士纪念与巴勒斯坦民族空间的建构**  
*Mohammed Hamdan, Bayan AlAmmouri, Lindsey Moore*｜2026-11-10｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502888](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502888)

> **Abstract:** This article theorizes Nablus, a Palestinian city in the contemporary West Bank, as a cultural geographic site that exemplifies a rich cartography of sacrifice wherein urban spaces become inseparable from memory and identity. Drawing on Palestinian scholarship on memory, spatial practice and everyday life, we argue that the Old City of Nablus functions as a counter-geography in which martyr memory, survival and belonging are continually inscribed and lived. Its memorial walls and improvised shrines operate as mnemonic devices and spatial archives that reflect a long history of collective loss and resilience. Space is often weaponized in Palestine: checkpoints disrupt continuity, surveillance causes anxiety and settlements continue to radically change topography. However, in Nablus, ṣumūd or steadfastness allows people to reclaim their city and reconstruct its national value by turning geography into a narrative of commemoration. Drawing on interviews with residents and analysis of graffiti, heritage signs and commemorative sites in Nablus's Old City (al-Balda al-Qadīma), we suggest that memory is actively performed when mourning is intertwined with everyday life, thereby materializing a living geography of sacrifice and endurance. The article concludes that Nablus is an enduring testimony to how Palestinian urban spaces resist erasure by transforming martyrdom into a spatial language that embodies persistence.
>
> **摘要：** 本文将当代约旦河西岸的巴勒斯坦城市纳布卢斯理论化为一个文化地理场所，它体现了一种丰富的牺牲制图学，其中城市空间与记忆和身份密不可分。借鉴巴勒斯坦关于记忆、空间实践与日常生活的研究，我们认为纳布卢斯老城作为一种反地理而发挥作用，烈士记忆、生存与归属在其中被持续铭刻和体验。其纪念墙与临时祭坛作为记忆装置和空间档案，反映了漫长的集体丧失与坚韧抗争的历史。在巴勒斯坦，空间常常被武器化：检查站破坏了连续性，监控引发焦虑，定居点持续剧烈地改变着地貌。然而在纳布卢斯，“坚守”（ṣumūd）使人们得以夺回自己的城市，并通过将地理转化为纪念叙事来重建其民族价值。基于对居民的访谈以及对纳布卢斯老城（al-Balda al-Qadīma）涂鸦、遗产标识和纪念场所的分析，我们认为，当哀悼与日常生活交织在一起时，记忆被积极地展演出来，从而使一种关于牺牲与坚忍的活的地理得以物质化。文章最后指出，纳布卢斯是巴勒斯坦城市空间如何通过将殉难转化为体现坚持的空间语言来抵抗抹除的持久见证。


`T1` **Animals’ territories**  
**动物的领地**  
*Jamie Lorimer, Tom Fry, Oscar Hartman-Davies, Timothy Hodgetts et al.*｜2026-11-10｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261506619](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261506619)

> **Abstract:** This paper develops a conceptual framework for research on animals’ territories. It starts from a genealogy of the fraught traffic of territorial ideas between ethology and geography, in which the disavowal of biological determinism led to a stark humanist divide in territorial research in political geography. It argues that this division is founded on an anachronistic understanding of animal behaviour, which hinders the development of a more-than-human conception of territory in political geography, ultimately limiting critical analysis of the widespread and often devastating human impacts on animals’ territories. To redress these problems, the second and third sections draw on recent ethological research to introduce animals’ terrain – defined as the material properties of a territory sensed in relation to animals’ Umwelten – and animals’ territorialities or territorial practices. It shows how analyses of animals help further calls for plural and material accounts of territory. A fourth section outlines and evaluates a model of territory for a practical politics of multispecies coexistence premised on wild diplomacy through interspecies communication. The conclusions identify key points for dialogue with both animal and political geographers. The analysis is illustrated with examples of the territorial birds and mammals that predominate in research in ethology and animal geography.
>
> **摘要：** 本文为动物领地研究构建了一个概念框架。文章首先梳理了领地观念在动物行为学与地理学之间充满张力的交流谱系：对生物决定论的否弃导致政治地理学的领地研究中出现了鲜明的人本主义分野。本文认为，这一分野建立在对动物行为的过时理解之上，阻碍了政治地理学中超越人类的领地概念的发展，最终限制了对人类对动物领地所造成的广泛且常常是毁灭性影响的批判性分析。为纠正这些问题，第二和第三部分借鉴近期动物行为学研究，引入“动物的地形”——定义为相对于动物环境界（Umwelten）而被感知的领地物质属性——以及动物的领地性或领地实践。文章展示了对动物的分析如何有助于推进关于领地的多元化与物质性阐释的呼吁。第四部分概述并评估了一种面向多物种共存实践政治的领地模型，其前提是通过种间交流实现的“野性外交”。结论部分指出了与动物地理学家和政治地理学家展开对话的关键要点。文中以动物行为学和动物地理学研究中占主导地位的领地性鸟类和哺乳动物为例进行阐释。


`D1` **On hinterlands**  
**论腹地**  
*Jim Thatcher*｜2026-11-07｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502884](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502884)

> **Abstract:** Rosen and Alvarez León's typology of “variegated hinterlands” provides a powerful and timely analytic for examining digital capitalism. This commentary pushes the authors on two fronts: first, I question the fluidity between types of hinterlands and their related cores, warning against a typological collapse in which all spaces become hinterlands to some other; second, I push the authors to further define the differences in kind from previous iterations of capitalist expansion and exploitation. To do so, I highlight the growing computational inversion in which data is extracted from urban cores to be processed, analyzed, and decided upon by algorithmic agents located in distant, traditionally defined hinterlands. To better understand this inversion and its already-arriving impacts, I argue geographers must use the typology not simply to map the traces of these relations, but to build a topology of contestation.
>
> **摘要：** Rosen与Alvarez León提出的“多样化腹地”类型学为考察数字资本主义提供了一个有力且适时的分析工具。本评论从两个方面对作者提出推进：首先，我质疑各类腹地与其相关核心之间的流动性，警惕一种类型学坍塌，即所有空间都成为其他某处的腹地；其次，我敦促作者进一步界定其与以往资本主义扩张和剥削形态之间的本质差异。为此，我强调一种日益增长的计算倒置：数据从城市核心被提取出来，交由位于遥远的、传统意义上的腹地中的算法代理进行处理、分析和决策。为更好地理解这种倒置及其已经显现的影响，我认为地理学者不应仅用该类型学来描绘这些关系的痕迹，而应借此构建一种抗争的拓扑学。


`T1` **Volumetric commensuration and its remainders**  
**体积通约及其剩余**  
*Hung-Ying Chen*｜2026-11-03｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502903](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502903)

> **Abstract:** This commentary responds to Daniel Paiva's call for the right to atmosphere by advancing the framework of following air rights . I treat breathable air, buildable volume and emission allowances as claims held apart by three interrelated operations: differentiation, detachment and conversion. Comparing two instruments, I argue that the property/commons contradiction is produced by design and not just given in nature. Admissibility – whether claims are admitted for political consideration – is a prior condition of meaningful participation.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论回应丹尼尔·派瓦关于“大气权”的呼吁，提出“追踪空气权”的框架。我将可呼吸的空气、可建设的体积与排放配额视为通过三种相互关联的运作——区分、剥离与转换——而彼此分隔的权利主张。通过比较两种工具，我认为产权/公地之间的矛盾是被设计生产出来的，而非仅仅是自然给定的。可受理性——即权利主张是否被纳入政治考量——是有意义参与的先决条件。

`S1` **LGBTphobia as a multiscale assemblage: Rural LBT women’s everyday geographies**  
**作为多尺度集合体的LGBT恐惧：乡村LBT女性的日常地理**  
*Gilly Hartal, Yael Bar-Tzedek*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502892](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502892)

> **Abstract:** This article examines LGBTphobia as experienced by lesbian, bisexual, and transgender (LBT) women in Israeli rural space. Conceptualized here as a multiscale assemblage, five manifestations of LGBTphobia are presented: (1) Whispering, giggling and gossip; (2) insults related to sexual and gender identity; (3) discrimination and exclusion; (4) intrusion, sexualization, and eroticization; and (5) Condescension and boundary construction. Although neoliberal discourses often assume that LGBTQ + acceptance has become increasingly widespread, particularly in urban settings, this analysis demonstrates that inclusion does not eliminate LGBTphobia. Rather, inclusion and LGBTphobia coexist, with narratives of progress and acceptance frequently obscuring more subtle yet persistent forms of exclusion, marginalization, and prejudice. Relatedly, whereas recent scholarship suggests that rural areas may serve as spaces of tolerance and safety for LGBTQ + s thanks to the diffusion of urban discourses of acceptance into rural contexts, the findings show how LGBTphobia becomes internalized, embodied, and affectively reproduced in everyday life. Among LBT women interviewed, the anticipation of LGBTphobia prompted self-censorship even before any overt incidents occurred. These dynamics reveal LGBTphobia as an embodied and spatial structure rather than a solely interpersonal phenomenon, continuing to shape participants’ everyday rural lives despite apparent social progress.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察以色列乡村空间中女同性恋、双性恋和跨性别（LBT）女性所经历的LGBT恐惧。本文将其概念化为一种多尺度集合体，并呈现了LGBT恐惧的五种表现形式：（1）窃窃私语、嗤笑和流言蜚语；（2）与性取向和性别认同相关的侮辱；（3）歧视与排斥；（4）侵扰、性化与色情化；（5）居高临下与边界建构。尽管新自由主义话语往往假定对LGBTQ+的接纳已日益普遍，尤其是在城市环境中，但本分析表明，包容并未消除LGBT恐惧。相反，包容与LGBT恐惧并存，进步与接纳的叙事常常遮蔽了更为隐蔽却持续存在的排斥、边缘化与偏见形式。与此相关，尽管近期研究认为，由于城市接纳话语向乡村的扩散，乡村地区可能成为LGBTQ+群体的宽容与安全空间，但研究发现显示了LGBT恐惧如何在日常生活中被内化、具身化并在情感上被再生产。在受访的LBT女性中，对LGBT恐惧的预期甚至在任何公开事件发生之前就促使她们进行自我审查。这些动态揭示出LGBT恐惧是一种具身的、空间性的结构，而不仅仅是一种人际现象，尽管社会表面上取得了进步，它仍持续塑造着参与者的乡村日常生活。


`T1` **The everyday as ecological archive**  
**作为生态档案的日常**  
*Yogesh Mishra*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502904](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502904)

`T1` **Atmospheric fury, shadows of agonism, and the ethics of abolishing worlds**  
**大气之怒、竞争主义的阴影与废除世界的伦理**  
*Mikko Joronen*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502906](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502906)

> **Abstract:** This commentary engages Daniel Paiva’s (2026) proposal to think the ‘right to atmosphere’ in agonistic terms. First, drawing on Michel Serres, it elaborates the role of turbulent ‘fury’ in the elemental constitution of atmospheres. It argues the fury fundamentally troubles agonistic pluralism by showing how worlds are broken, ended and produced through materialities that do not easily lend themselves to what remains central to the ‘right to atmosphere’. After looking at the collective equivalence of fury, the commentary turns, second, to the three shadows of agonism – intrinsic atmospheric seductions, unruly movements irreducible to identifiable origins or parties to a conflict, and brutal colonial shadows shattering the promises of productive conflict – problematising the assumption that conflict necessarily functions as a creative, productive and capacitating force. Finally, drawing on abolitionist thought, it asks whether the solution to unequal access to atmospheres, one capable of acknowledging the anti-oppressive ethics of care underlined by Paiva, is inclusion and participation, or the abolition of oppressive worlds.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论回应Daniel Paiva（2026）以竞争主义（agonistic）视角思考“大气权”的主张。首先，本文借鉴米歇尔·塞尔的思想，阐述湍动的“狂怒”在大气的元素性构成中的作用。本文认为，这种狂怒从根本上扰乱了竞争性多元主义，因为它表明世界是如何通过那些难以纳入“大气权”核心要义的物质性而被打破、终结和生产的。在考察狂怒的集体对等物之后，本评论其次转向竞争主义的三重阴影——内在的大气诱惑、无法归结为可识别来源或冲突方的失序运动，以及粉碎建设性冲突承诺的残酷殖民阴影——从而质疑冲突必然作为一种创造性、生产性和赋能性力量的假设。最后，本文借鉴废除主义思想追问：对于大气获取的不平等，一种能够承认Paiva所强调的反压迫关怀伦理的解决方案，究竟是包容与参与，还是废除压迫性的世界？


`P1` **Planetary 'Singaporeanization': Geopolitics within planetary rural/urban geographies**  
**行星尺度的“新加坡化”：行星乡村/城市地理中的地缘政治**  
*James D. Sidaway, Dariusz Wójcik*｜2026-10-30｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261479812](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261479812)

> **Abstract:** This commentary engages the article by Chi-Mao Wang, Damien Maye, and Michael Woods (2025) on ‘Planetary rural geographies’ through the vantage point of the city-state of Singapore. We argue that viewing the themes developed by Wang et al. from and through Singapore and other city-states brings out (geo)political frames – notably sovereignty, empires, and their aftermaths – with implications for other cases, sites, and the wider conceptual debate.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论以城市国家新加坡为视角，回应王志弘（Chi-Mao Wang）、达米安·梅耶（Damien Maye）和迈克尔·伍兹（Michael Woods）（2025）关于“行星乡村地理”的文章。我们认为，从新加坡及其他城市国家出发并透过它们审视王等人所阐发的主题，能够凸显（地缘）政治框架——尤其是主权、帝国及其后续影响——这对其他案例、地点以及更广泛的概念讨论均具有启示意义。


`E2` **Before and beyond the rural rent gap: Brokered legibility and authorised asset value in China’s rural revitalisation**  
**在乡村租差之前与之外：中国乡村振兴中的中介化可读性与授权资产价值**  
*Ying Wang, Tin-Yuet Ting*｜2026-10-27｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502896](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502896)

> **Abstract:** This commentary extends Phillips and Smith's comparative agenda for rural gentrification by putting it in dialogue with Birch and Ward's account of assetization. We argue that rural gentrification and assetization require attention to a prior institutional passage: the conversion of ambiguous rural resources into authorised objects of operation. Drawing on China's rural revitalisation, we call this process brokered legibility. The concept identifies a legibility gap between potential value and value that can be contracted, funded, audited, governed and publicly defended. By foregrounding quasi-state intermediaries and delegated operation, the commentary shifts analysis from value capture alone to the contested authorisation of rural asset value, including the de-authorisation of alternative rural futures.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论通过将菲利普斯和史密斯关于乡村士绅化的比较研究议程与伯奇和沃德关于资产化的论述相对话，对前者进行了拓展。我们认为，乡村士绅化与资产化需要关注一个先在的制度通道：将模糊的乡村资源转化为获得授权的运营对象。基于中国乡村振兴的实践，我们将这一过程称为“中介化可读性”（brokered legibility）。该概念识别出潜在价值与可签约、可融资、可审计、可治理并可公开辩护的价值之间的可读性鸿沟。通过突出准国家中介机构和委托运营，本评论将分析从单纯的价值捕获转向乡村资产价值的争议性授权，包括对其他乡村未来的去授权。


`T1` **On seasonal influences, refrains, and weirdness: A response to Kaya Barry’s geographies of the seasons**  
**论季节性影响、叠歌与怪异：对卡雅·巴里季节地理学的回应**  
*Derek McCormack*｜2026-10-27｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502883](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502883)

> **Abstract:** Responding to the invitation offered by Kaya Barry to think more about seasonality, in this article I offer three supplements to her timely and important project. First, I revisit earlier geographical work on seasonality in order to think about the question of influence. Second, I consider how the shifting space–times of seasonality might be understood as refrains of a sort. Third, I reflect on what it might mean to think with/in the latitude-specific weirdness of un/seasonality.
>
> **摘要：** 响应卡雅·巴里关于进一步思考季节性的邀请，本文为她这一及时而重要的研究计划提供三点补充。第一，我重新回顾早期关于季节性的地理学研究，以思考影响的问题。第二，我探讨季节性不断变化的时空如何可以被理解为某种叠歌。第三，我反思与特定纬度的季节性/非季节性之怪异一同思考、并在其中思考可能意味着什么。

**作为伦理义务的呼吸权**  
*Marijn Nieuwenhuis*｜2026-10-27｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502898](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502898)
