`T1` **Layered proximity: Distant time and deferred temporality in circular bioeconomy**  
**分层的邻近性：循环生物经济中的遥远时间与延宕的时间性**  
*Goshu Wolde Tefera*｜2026-10-27｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1177/20438206261502879](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502879)

> **Abstract:** This commentary engages Datta's concept of distant time, which explains how colonial statecraft governs subaltern populations through temporal deferral, keeping them suspended in conditions of waiting while framing displacement and demolition as development. Building on Datta's work, I argue that her framework under-theorizes the complementary process of temporal compression. Using Harvey's concept of time-space compression as a conceptual lens and drawing on scholarship on the circular bioeconomy, I show how contemporary colonial-capitalist governance not only defers justice but also compresses marginalized communities into global circuits of value in real time. In the circular bioeconomy, forests, farmers, and waste pickers are rendered immediately governable and economically productive, even as the benefits they help generate are projected into an indefinitely deferred future. Rather than replacing Datta's framework, I propose a reorientation toward a theory of layered proximity, where immediate material incorporation coexists with the continuous postponement of justice.
>
> **摘要：** 本评论回应达塔（Datta）提出的“遥远时间”概念，该概念解释了殖民治国术如何通过时间上的延宕来治理底层人群，使其悬置于等待状态之中，同时将驱逐与拆迁包装为发展。在达塔研究的基础上，我认为她的框架对与之互补的时间压缩过程理论化不足。本文以哈维的时空压缩概念为理论视角，并借鉴有关循环生物经济的研究，表明当代殖民—资本主义治理不仅延宕正义，而且将边缘化社群实时地压缩进全球价值循环之中。在循环生物经济中，森林、农民和拾荒者被即时地变得可治理且具有经济生产性，而他们参与创造的收益却被投射到一个无限延宕的未来。本文并非要取代达塔的框架，而是主张转向一种“分层的邻近性”理论：即时的物质性纳入与正义的持续延宕并存。


## Antipode

`P1` **Karachi in/and Sindh: Articulations, Disarticulations and the Eternal Reciprocity of Tears**  
**卡拉奇在/与信德：接合、脱节与眼泪的永恒互惠**  
*Ayyaz Mallick*｜2026-11-16｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70235](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70235)

> **Abstract:** This paper develops a Gramscian sensitivity towards spatial claims and popular imaginaries to explore intertwined urban and national questions in Pakistan. The relationship between Karachi, Pakistan's largest city, and wider Sindh province animates intense contestation. Post‐1970s, this generated exclusivist ethnic politics, mediated through reified spatial divisions and claims over city, urbanity and nation. By analysing cultural and political productions, this paper considers shifting registers of urbanity, land and nation in the present conjuncture of crisis and dispossession. It delineates different articulations of urban and national questions, their slippages and contradictions, with an eye towards more inclusive, people‐centred resolutions of the Karachi–Sindh relationship. Drawing on Gramsci, the paper foregrounds the constitutive role of spatial imaginaries and practices in forging hegemonic projects. It thus calls attention to the situated spatial claims (including over city, urbanity and nation) produced by processes of urbanization and dispossession and the responsibility of intellectual‐cultural productions therein.
>
> **摘要：** 本文发展了一种对空间诉求与大众想象的葛兰西式敏感性，以探讨巴基斯坦相互交织的城市问题与民族问题。卡拉奇作为巴基斯坦最大的城市，与更广阔的信德省之间的关系引发了激烈争夺。1970年代以后，这种关系催生了排他性的族群政治，并通过固化的空间划分以及对城市、城市性和民族的诉求加以中介。通过分析文化与政治生产，本文考察了在当前危机与剥夺的历史关头中，城市性、土地与民族的话语域如何变迁。本文勾勒了城市问题与民族问题的不同接合方式及其滑移与矛盾，并着眼于以更具包容性、以人为本的方式解决卡拉奇—信德关系。借鉴葛兰西，本文突出空间想象与空间实践在锻造霸权计划中的构成性作用。由此，本文呼吁关注由城市化与剥夺过程所产生的情境化空间诉求（包括对城市、城市性和民族的诉求），以及知识—文化生产在其中所负的责任。


`E2` **Reproducing Water Coloniality: Water Switching and State‐Backed Accumulation by Dispossession in Mauritius**  
**再生产水的殖民性：毛里求斯的水资源转用与国家支持的剥夺性积累**  
*Aleksandra Peeroo*｜2026-11-16｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70246](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70246)

> **Abstract:** This article brings to light the coloniality of water in Mauritius—the ways in which colonially inherited water resource allocations continue to shape contemporary power relations underpinning elite accumulation by dispossession. It conceptualises ‘water switching’, the redirection of water to ever new profitable uses, as the process enabling continuous accumulation and reproducing coloniality. These dynamics rely on an active, enabling state. By generating an original typology of state support mechanisms—coercion, legitimisation, facilitation—the paper offers a framework for analysing the politics of dispossession. State‐capital symbiosis explains why these mechanisms persist, showing how Mauritius' economic development and the state's own legitimacy are deeply entwined with the protection of inherited elite privilege. The paper concludes by showing how the Mauritian case provides a basis for examining the role of resource endowments enabling accumulation across other sectors and geographies.
>
> **摘要：** 本文揭示了毛里求斯水资源的殖民性，即殖民时期遗留下来的水资源分配方式如何持续塑造支撑精英剥夺性积累的当代权力关系。本文将“水资源转用”（water switching）——即将水资源不断重新导向新的营利用途——概念化为使持续积累成为可能并再生产殖民性的过程。这些动态依赖于一个积极的、提供支持的国家。通过构建一个原创性的国家支持机制类型学——强制、合法化与便利化——本文为分析剥夺的政治提供了一个框架。国家—资本共生关系解释了这些机制为何得以延续，表明毛里求斯的经济发展与国家自身的合法性同对既有精英特权的保护深度交织。最后，本文指出，毛里求斯案例为考察资源禀赋在其他部门和地域中促成积累的作用提供了基础。


`S1` **Theorizing Racisms Across Italy and the United States: Lessons From Gramsci and Du Bois**  
**跨越意大利与美国理论化种族主义：来自葛兰西与杜波依斯的启示**  
*Camilla Hawthorne*｜2026-11-16｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70227](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70227)

> **Abstract:** The 2020 Italian Black Lives Matter mobilizations were a powerful incitement to consider what is at stake in understanding Italy as part of the global history of racial capitalism—and resistance against it. To do this, it is urgent for scholars to move from comparative to relational approaches in the study of global racisms. I argue that Antonio Gramsci and W.E.B. Du Bois provide a powerful set of analytical tools for breaking out of methodological nationalism in the study of racism and race in Italy—and beyond. When read together, Gramsci's writings on Italian national unification and Du Bois' writings on post‐Civil War Reconstruction in the United States offer both a historical grounding for understanding the connections between contestations over race and nation in Italy the United States, and a framework for how to think relationally about struggles against racism and fascism unfolding in both countries today.
>
> **摘要：** 2020年意大利“黑人的命也是命”（Black Lives Matter）运动强有力地促使人们思考：将意大利理解为种族资本主义全球历史及其抵抗的一部分，究竟意味着什么。为此，学者们亟需在全球种族主义研究中从比较方法转向关系性方法。我认为，安东尼奥·葛兰西和W.E.B.杜波依斯为突破意大利乃至其他地区种族与种族主义研究中的方法论民族主义提供了一套强有力的分析工具。将二者合读时，葛兰西关于意大利国家统一的著述与杜波依斯关于美国内战后重建的著述，既为理解意大利与美国围绕种族与民族之争的联系提供了历史基础，也为如何以关系性方式思考当今两国正在展开的反种族主义与反法西斯主义斗争提供了框架。


`S2` **The Limits of Cash: Guaranteed Income, Basic Income, and the Reproduction of the Precarious City**  
**现金的局限：保障收入、基本收入与不稳定城市的再生产**  
*James C. Fraser, Deirdre A. Oakley*｜2026-11-15｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70248](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70248)

> **Abstract:** Guaranteed income pilots and COVID‐era relief payments share a political form: they are time‐limited. This paper asks what that form does in a racialized housing market undergoing restructuring. Drawing on a five‐year participatory panel study of low‐income, predominantly Black women in Preservation of Affordable Housing properties in Cincinnati's Over‐the‐Rhine, we find that a bounded infusion of unconditional cash left the distributive register unmoved while the relational one eroded around it. Within‐person, neighborhood attachment declined significantly over the payment period and did not recover; social cohesion held through the infusion and then fell sharply after it ended; financial strain moved in neither direction. We read the pattern as normalization, a governing technology that holds precarity within a manageable range through statutory sunsets, tax‐credit covenants, and a resumed eviction docket. The argument is not against cash but against the assumption that a distributive instrument can reach a relational injury.
>
> **摘要：** 保障收入试点项目与新冠疫情时期的救济款共享一种政治形式：它们都是有时限的。本文探讨这一形式在一个正在经历重构的种族化住房市场中产生了何种作用。基于在辛辛那提莱茵河畔区“保护可负担住房”物业中开展的一项为期五年、针对以黑人女性为主的低收入群体的参与式面板研究，我们发现，有限度注入的无条件现金并未改变分配层面，而关系层面却在其周围逐渐受到侵蚀。在个体内部层面，邻里依恋在发放期间显著下降且未能恢复；社会凝聚力在现金注入期间得以维持，但在发放结束后急剧下降；财务压力则未朝任何方向变化。我们将这一模式解读为常态化——一种通过法定终止期限、税收抵免契约以及重启的驱逐案件审理，将不稳定性维持在可控范围内的治理技术。本文的论点并非反对现金发放，而是反对一种假设，即分配性工具能够触及关系性伤害。


`S1` **Black and Pan‐African Joy and the Specter of Fascism**  
**黑人与泛非之喜悦与法西斯主义的幽灵**  
*Horace G. Campbell*｜2026-11-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70233](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70233)

> **Abstract:** This paper situates contemporary neofascism within the history of racial capitalism, arguing that Black Joy and “Emancipation Circuits” constitute material practices of resistance to dehumanization. Grounding capitalist transformations in the systematic negation of Black humanity, it challenges abstract accounts of valorization and explores joy's ontological foundations in African traditions of optimism and collective struggle. It critiques Eurocentric interpretations of Nazism and fascist terror, examining how Cold War deployments of authoritarianism obscured the historical relations through which fascism emerged and consolidated. It identifies sonic landscapes, from jazz to R&B, as “rival geographies” of autonomous expression and resistance. Contrasting Wagnerian aesthetic closure with John Coltrane's improvisational freedom, it locates Black music within emancipation struggles. Finally, it advocates a “popular multiracial front from below” uniting anti‐racist, feminist, and Indigenous struggles against the normalization of financial warfare and the deployment of technological force that facilitate the maturation of neofascism into fully consolidated fascist rule.
>
> **摘要：** 本文将当代新法西斯主义置于种族资本主义的历史之中，认为“黑人喜悦”（Black Joy）与“解放回路”（Emancipation Circuits）构成了抵抗非人化的物质实践。本文将资本主义的转型建立在对黑人人性的系统性否定之上，挑战了关于价值增殖的抽象论述，并探讨喜悦在非洲乐观主义与集体斗争传统中的本体论基础。本文批判了对纳粹主义与法西斯恐怖的欧洲中心主义解读，考察冷战时期对威权主义概念的运用如何遮蔽了法西斯主义得以产生和巩固的历史关系。本文将从爵士乐到节奏布鲁斯（R&B）的声音景观识别为自主表达与抵抗的“对抗性地理”。通过对比瓦格纳式的美学封闭与约翰·柯川（John Coltrane）的即兴自由，本文将黑人音乐置于解放斗争之中。最后，本文倡导建立一个“自下而上的人民多种族阵线”，联合反种族主义、女性主义和原住民斗争，以对抗金融战争的常态化以及技术力量的部署——正是这些因素促使新法西斯主义发展成熟为全面巩固的法西斯统治。


`P1` **Hidden Fractures: Communal Capitalist Urban Transformation in Post‐Protest Delhi**  
**隐秘的裂痕：抗议后德里的社群资本主义城市转型**  
*Farah Farooqi*｜2026-11-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70238](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70238)

> **Abstract:** This paper examines how the 2019–2020 Shaheen Bagh protests in Delhi, a landmark movement against India's Citizenship Amendment Act, accelerated urban transformations that extend well beyond the protest site. Drawing on ten months of comparative ethnographic fieldwork (2022–2023) in Shaheen Bagh, Jaitpur and Jasola Vihar, the study shows how communal capitalism, a mode of rule in which Hindu nationalist domination directs capitalist development, reshaped urban space and social relations in the protest's aftermath. The paper reads the post‐protest period through a Foucauldian account of visibility and discipline joined to the Symposium's concept of communal capitalism: the knowledge produced about a ‘dangerous’ neighbourhood becomes the basis both for its containment and for the value extracted from it. First, it shows how an initial show of sovereign force was joined by pervasive forms of control, including heightened state surveillance and community self‐regulation. Second, it traces how the exclusion of Muslims was made productive, with paradoxical results: while offering new economic opportunities to some Muslims, these dynamics also deepen spatial segregation and internal stratification. Third, it follows these processes across three localities, whose different patterns are shaped by class composition, property relations and histories of communal interaction. The findings contribute to debates on authoritarian urbanism, on Muslim urban experiences in contemporary India and on communal capitalism. The paper argues that moments of political resistance may accelerate patterns of spatial segregation while spurring new forms of economic and social adaptation in marginalised communities.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察2019—2020年德里沙欣巴格（Shaheen Bagh）抗议——一场反对印度《公民身份修正法》的标志性运动——如何加速了远远超出抗议现场的城市转型。基于在沙欣巴格、杰特布尔（Jaitpur）和贾索拉维哈尔（Jasola Vihar）开展的为期十个月的比较民族志田野调查（2022—2023年），研究展示了社群资本主义——一种由印度教民族主义支配引导资本主义发展的统治模式——如何在抗议之后重塑城市空间与社会关系。本文以福柯式的可见性与规训理论，结合本专题研讨所提出的社群资本主义概念来解读抗议后时期：关于一个“危险”街区所生产的知识，既成为对其进行遏制的基础，也成为从中榨取价值的基础。首先，本文展示了最初的主权武力展示如何与无处不在的控制形式相结合，包括强化的国家监控和社区自我规制。其次，本文追溯了对穆斯林的排斥如何被转化为生产性的，并产生了悖论性的结果：这些动态在为部分穆斯林提供新经济机会的同时，也加深了空间隔离和内部分层。第三，本文追踪了这些过程在三个地方的展开，其不同模式受到阶级构成、产权关系和社群互动历史的影响。研究发现有助于推进关于威权城市主义、当代印度穆斯林城市经验以及社群资本主义的讨论。本文认为，政治抵抗的时刻可能会加速空间隔离的模式，同时也在边缘化社区中催生新的经济与社会适应形式。


`S1` **The Afterlives of Homeland: Black Veteran Homelessness and Racial Geographies of Unbelonging**  
**“祖国”的余生：黑人退伍军人无家可归与不归属的种族地理**  
*Rupak Shrestha*｜2026-11-10｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70236](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70236)

> **Abstract:** This article examines how Black veterans experiencing homelessness navigate the racialized geographies of “homeland” in the United States. Drawing on ethnographic fieldwork with Walter (pseudonym), a 65‐year‐old Black Vietnam veteran living unhoused in Boulder, Colorado, I argue that the US homeland emerges in his experience as a racialized field of continual displacement. Walter's everyday life reveals a dissonance between the homeland's promise of security and his growing loss of faith in the institutions charged with providing care and protection. Bringing Black Geographies into conversation with scholarship on homeland security and homelessness, the article traces how Walter's memories of anti‐Black violence in Mississippi and Vietnam, his refusal to engage with the Department of Veterans Affairs, and his reliance on a fragile power wheelchair illuminate what I conceptualize as racialized homeland insecurity. His humor and reflections on survival also reveal forms of Black spatial knowledge through which he insists on living otherwise.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察经历无家可归的黑人退伍军人如何在美国“祖国”（homeland）的种族化地理中周旋。基于对沃尔特（化名）——一位在科罗拉多州博尔德无家可归的65岁黑人越战退伍军人——的民族志田野调查，我认为在他的经验中，美国祖国呈现为一个持续流离失所的种族化场域。沃尔特的日常生活揭示了祖国所承诺的安全与他对负责提供照护与保护的机构日益丧失信任之间的不协调。本文将黑人地理学与关于国土安全和无家可归的研究相对话，追溯沃尔特对密西西比和越南反黑人暴力的记忆、他拒绝与退伍军人事务部打交道，以及他对一辆脆弱电动轮椅的依赖，如何揭示了我所概念化的“种族化的祖国不安全”。他的幽默以及对生存的反思也揭示了黑人空间知识的形式，借此他坚持以另一种方式生活。


`T1` **Epistemic Civic Death: Knowledge Infrastructures, Disciplinary Hegemony, and the Destruction of Critical Geographies**  
**认知性公民死亡：知识基础设施、学科霸权与批判地理学的毁灭**  
*İlhan Kaya*｜2026-11-10｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70234](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70234)

> **Abstract:** Accounts of authoritarian assault on universities center the scholar: dismissed, detained, exiled, reduced to civic death. This paper moves from the knower to the conditions of knowing. I call their destruction epistemic civic death : the dismantling of infrastructures that make critical knowledge collectively possible, such as platforms, pedagogies, and networks of trust and citation. It addresses a blind spot across academic freedom, geographies of knowledge, and comparative authoritarianism. Reading Foucault and Gramsci through Mbembe, I trace a disciplinary necropolitics where the state supplies the instrument of exception and a hegemonic discipline the target. My case is Turkish geography, built to serve a national imaginary and silent on class, ethnicity, and gender. The critical knowledge infrastructure built around the Turkish Association of Geographers was dissolved by emergency decree in 2016. I write from inside that closure. The association reopened in 2018; the community did not. An administrative decision can restore an institution, not the infrastructure of knowing it sustained.
>
> **摘要：** 关于威权主义冲击大学的论述往往以学者为中心：被解雇、被拘押、被流放，沦为公民死亡。本文将关注点从认知者转向认知的条件。我将这些条件的毁灭称为认知性公民死亡：即对使批判性知识得以集体生成的基础设施（如平台、教学法以及信任与引用网络）的拆解。本文针对学术自由、知识地理学和比较威权主义研究中的一个共同盲点。通过姆本贝的视角解读福柯与葛兰西，我追溯了一种学科性的死亡政治：国家提供例外状态的工具，而霸权性学科提供打击目标。我的案例是土耳其地理学——它被建构用以服务于民族想象，并对阶级、族裔和性别保持沉默。围绕土耳其地理学者协会构建起来的批判性知识基础设施于2016年被紧急状态法令解散。我从这一关闭事件的内部进行书写。该协会于2018年重新开放，但学术共同体并未随之恢复。行政决定可以恢复一个机构，却无法恢复该机构所维系的认知基础设施。


`D1` **Governing the Future: Artificial Intelligence as an Anticipatory Infrastructure of Control**  
**治理未来：作为预期性控制基础设施的人工智能**  
*Giuseppe T. Cirella*｜2026-11-10｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70237](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70237)

> **Abstract:** Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in systems of governance, yet its political implications remain insufficiently theorized. This paper argues that AI is best understood as an anticipatory infrastructure of control: a form of power that operates through the preemptive governance of possible futures. Drawing on cases of biometric border systems, predictive policing, and AI‐enabled surveillance in authoritarian contexts, it shows how algorithmic systems classify and intervene on the basis of predicted risk. In doing so, they shift governance from regulating past and present conduct to managing future possibilities. Extending theories of discipline, biopolitics, and control, the paper demonstrates how anticipatory control is unevenly distributed, disproportionately targeting migrants, racialized communities, and political dissidents within longer histories of colonial domination and social inequality. Addressing these dynamics requires moving beyond the regulation of technologies to contesting the infrastructures through which futures are governed.
>
> **摘要：** 人工智能（AI）日益嵌入治理体系，但其政治意涵仍缺乏充分的理论化。本文认为，人工智能最好被理解为一种预期性控制基础设施：一种通过对可能未来进行先发制人式治理而运作的权力形式。借助生物识别边境系统、预测性警务以及威权语境下人工智能驱动的监控等案例，本文展示了算法系统如何基于预测风险进行分类与干预。由此，治理从规制过去和当下的行为转向管理未来的可能性。本文拓展了规训、生命政治与控制理论，表明预期性控制的分布是不均衡的，在殖民统治与社会不平等的更长历史之中，不成比例地针对移民、种族化社群和政治异见者。应对这些动态需要超越对技术的规制，转而对治理未来的基础设施本身提出抗争。


`P1` **Mapping the Scales of Mobile and Carceral Logics of the
                    EU
                    –Turkey Deportation Machine**  
**描绘欧盟—土耳其遣返机器中流动逻辑与监禁逻辑的尺度**  
*Helen Mackreath*｜2026-11-08｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70224](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70224)

> **Abstract:** This paper situates Syrians in Istanbul at the intersection of overlapping practices of mobile and carceral logics. It traces the EU–Turkey deportation machine and the intertwined mechanisms, which work to produce certain individuals as ‘deportable’ at transnational scales. Secondly, the forms of carceral control in the unfolding realities of Turkish occupied North Syria, which are producing new relationships between people and place. And thirdly, the intrusions of these mechanisms into the everyday realities of Syrians living in Istanbul, which reproduce carceral logics at more granular levels. Reading these together may provide a more acute frame to register the multiple hierarchies of the border regimes active in, and on the edges of, Turkey. I ask what taking these logics as a starting point may offer us for rethinking questions of the overlapping borders connecting the geographies of Europe, Turkey and North Syria.
>
> **摘要：** 本文将伊斯坦布尔的叙利亚人置于流动逻辑与监禁逻辑相互重叠的实践交汇处。首先，本文追溯欧盟—土耳其遣返机器及其相互交织的机制，这些机制在跨国尺度上将特定个体生产为“可遣返者”。其次，本文考察在土耳其占领的叙利亚北部不断展开的现实中的监禁控制形式，这些形式正在生成人与地方之间的新关系。第三，本文探讨这些机制对生活在伊斯坦布尔的叙利亚人日常现实的侵入，这种侵入在更细微的层面上再生产着监禁逻辑。将三者结合起来解读，或可提供一个更敏锐的框架，以把握在土耳其境内及其边缘运作的边境体制的多重等级。本文追问，以这些逻辑为出发点，能为重新思考连接欧洲、土耳其与叙利亚北部地理的重叠边界问题带来什么。


`U2` **Ecological Statecraft for Hegemonic Visions: The Governance of Chengdu Eco‐Zone Over Two Decades**  
**服务于霸权愿景的生态治国术：成都生态区二十年治理**  
*Handuo Deng, Fulong Wu, Fangzhu Zhang*｜2026-11-05｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70229](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70229)

> **Abstract:** This study advances urban political ecology (UPE) by examining how ecological space production has become a key terrain for pursuing a hegemonic project of ecological civilisation in China. ‘Ecological statecraft’ provides a framework for examining how the ecological visions are materialised in the Chengdu Eco‐Zone. The area transformed from a landscape of mixed villages and construction in the 2000s to a hybrid of greenways and farmlands in the 2020s. These dramatic ‘shifts’ reveal a persistent logic of the ruling party, even as specific visions are continually reshaped in response to changing domestic and external conditions. Our analysis situates state politics within broader UPE debates, particularly historical materialist and Southern everyday‐state perspectives, and brings them into dialogue with Chinese UPE scholarship on state power, land politics and ecological territorialisation. In particular, combining statecraft and everyday‐state perspectives reveals how state‐dominated ecological governance is increasingly co‐shaped by grassroots interests and practices.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究通过考察生态空间生产如何成为中国推进生态文明霸权计划的关键领域，推进了城市政治生态学（UPE）研究。“生态治国术”为考察生态愿景如何在成都生态区中物质化提供了分析框架。该区域从2000年代村庄与建设用地混杂的景观，转变为2020年代绿道与农田交织的混合景观。这些剧烈的“转变”揭示了执政党一以贯之的逻辑，尽管具体愿景随国内外条件变化而不断重塑。我们的分析将国家政治置于更广泛的城市政治生态学讨论之中，尤其是历史唯物主义与南方日常国家视角，并使其与中国城市政治生态学关于国家权力、土地政治和生态领域化的研究展开对话。特别是，将治国术与日常国家视角相结合，揭示了国家主导的生态治理如何日益受到基层利益与实践的共同塑造。


`T1` **Subalternity in an Age of Planetary Crisis**  
**行星危机时代的底层性**  
*Alex Loftus*｜2026-11-03｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70226](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70226)

> **Abstract:** This paper argues that Gramsci's conceptualisation of subalternity offers crucial resources for theorising and intervening in the global climate crisis to popular democratic ends. While ‘the subaltern’ has proven highly generative in postcolonial writings—and in geographical thought more widely—Gramsci's own conceptualisation has been relatively neglected. Nevertheless, recent philological approaches have shed light on his highly distinctive interpretation, one that captures how subaltern social groups are integrally included in the relations of the modern state, limiting their autonomy and ability to enact political change. When the integral state is situated within its metabolic conditions of possibility, subalternity can be viewed as a form of domination specific to the age of fossil capital. Most importantly, Gramsci's methodological and strategic approach enables us to identify moments through which an age of planetary crisis might be transformed in popular democratic ways.
>
> **摘要：** 本文认为，葛兰西关于底层性（subalternity）的概念化，为以人民民主为目标来理论化并介入全球气候危机提供了关键资源。尽管“底层”概念在后殖民著作乃至更广泛的地理学思想中极具生产性，葛兰西本人的概念化却相对受到忽视。然而，近期的文献学研究揭示了他极具独特性的阐释，这一阐释捕捉到底层社会群体如何被整体地纳入现代国家的关系之中，从而限制了其自主性及推动政治变革的能力。当整体国家被置于其代谢性的可能性条件之中加以考察时，底层性便可被视为化石资本时代所特有的一种支配形式。最重要的是，葛兰西的方法论与策略路径使我们能够识别出那些可能以人民民主方式转变行星危机时代的关键时刻。


`U2` **Street Furniture as an Assetizing Apparatus: Advertising Concessions, Public Maintenance and the Production of Neoliberal Urban Space in Glasgow**  
**作为资产化装置的街道家具：格拉斯哥的广告特许经营、公共维护与新自由主义城市空间的生产**  
*Sinan Wang*｜2026-11-03｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70231](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70231)

> **Abstract:** Across British cities, everyday street infrastructure is increasingly sustained through revenue‐generating concessions that detach maintenance from direct municipal budgets. This article examines how street furniture in Glasgow has been incrementally reorganized as an assetizing apparatus under prolonged austerity. Drawing on micro political‐economic tracing of committee records, procurement documents and transport authority contracts, it shows how maintenance obligations are displaced from public budgets into advertising‐funded income streams, standardized through procurement and consolidated through concessionary portfolios. Rather than a sudden privatization of public space, assetization emerges as an accretive institutional process operating through commensuration, bundling and contractual duration. The article argues that low‐visibility infrastructures such as bus shelters are key sites where neoliberal urban governance is rendered durable, binding everyday public space to market performance while narrowing democratic contestation over public responsibility.
>
> **摘要：** 在英国各城市，日常街道基础设施越来越多地依靠创收型特许经营来维持，从而使维护工作脱离了市政直接预算。本文考察了在长期紧缩背景下，格拉斯哥的街道家具如何被逐步重组为一种资产化装置。基于对委员会记录、采购文件和交通管理机构合同的微观政治经济追踪，本文展示了维护义务如何从公共预算转移到由广告资助的收入流中，通过采购实现标准化，并通过特许经营组合得以巩固。资产化并非公共空间的突然私有化，而是一种通过可通约化、捆绑和合同期限而运作的累积性制度过程。本文认为，公交候车亭等低可见度基础设施是新自由主义城市治理得以持久化的关键场所，它们将日常公共空间与市场绩效绑定，同时收窄了围绕公共责任的民主争论空间。


`R2` **Moving Goods, Marginalizing Workers: Racialized Labor and Logistics‐Led Growth in Chicago's Freight Economy**  
**运送货物，边缘化工人：芝加哥货运经济中的种族化劳动与物流主导型增长**  
*José Miguel Acosta‐Córdova*｜2026-11-03｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70230](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70230)

> **Abstract:** Transportation, distribution, and logistics have become central to the contemporary organization of capitalism in the United States amid the long‐term decline of manufacturing employment. In freight‐intensive metropolitan regions, logistics has emerged as a major site of job growth, raising questions about how this expansion reshapes racial inequality in urban labor markets. This paper examines employment and wage patterns in the Chicago metropolitan area, North America's largest inland freight hub. Drawing on IPUMS microdata from the 1990 Census and 2008–2012 and 2018–2022 American Community Survey samples, the analysis traces changes in employment and median wages across railroads, trucking, and warehousing. The findings show that logistics growth has been accompanied by increasing Black and Latino employment, whereas persistent racial wage gaps remain across sub‐industries. Interpreted through racial capitalism, these patterns demonstrate how logistics restructuring has unfolded through an already segmented metropolitan labor market, with increased racialized incorporation occurring without corresponding wage convergence.
>
> **摘要：** 在制造业就业长期衰退的背景下，运输、配送与物流已成为美国当代资本主义组织方式的核心。在货运密集型大都市区，物流业已成为就业增长的主要领域，由此引发了这一扩张如何重塑城市劳动力市场中种族不平等的问题。本文考察了北美最大的内陆货运枢纽——芝加哥大都市区的就业与工资格局。基于1990年人口普查以及2008–2012年和2018–2022年美国社区调查样本的IPUMS微观数据，本文追踪了铁路、卡车运输和仓储业中就业与工资中位数的变化。研究发现，物流业增长伴随着黑人和拉丁裔就业的增加，但各细分行业中种族工资差距依然持续存在。从种族资本主义视角加以解读，这些格局表明物流重构是在一个早已分割的大都市劳动力市场中展开的，种族化吸纳的增加并未伴随相应的工资趋同。


`R2` **Under the Skin of the Factory: The Embodied Environmental Politics of Steelworkers in Dunkerque (France) and
                    IJmuiden
                    (the Netherlands)**  
**工厂的肌肤之下：敦刻尔克（法国）与艾默伊登（荷兰）钢铁工人的具身环境政治**  
*Anaëlle Bueno Patin*｜2026-10-27｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70225](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70225)

> **Abstract:** This paper takes the body as a focal point to deliver new insights into what drives or constrains labour environmentalism. More precisely, it looks at how labour–environment dynamics are inscribed in and negotiated through the bodies of steelworkers in IJmuiden (the Netherlands) and Hauts‐de‐France (France). While existing scholarship on Labour Environmentalism has explored relations between workers and environmental politics, the embodied experiences of working in polluting industries remain underexamined. This is problematic because it is precisely through ‘the body’ that industrial workers experience harm and can produce an ecological consciousness. Drawing on political ecology and critical geography, the paper conceptualises the body as a dialectic between the accumulation of environmental harm and emancipatory potential. The narrative interviews show how workers' bodies are sites of harm while carrying emancipatory potential. However, this emancipatory potential can be constrained. This underscores the persistence of the ‘jobs versus environment’ tension in practice.
>
> **摘要：** 本文以身体为焦点，为理解何种因素驱动或制约劳工环境主义提供新见解。更确切地说，本文考察劳动—环境动态如何铭刻于荷兰艾默伊登和法国上法兰西大区钢铁工人的身体之中，并通过身体得以协商。尽管已有劳工环境主义研究探讨了工人与环境政治之间的关系，但在污染性产业中工作的具身经验仍未得到充分考察。这一缺失颇成问题，因为恰恰是通过“身体”，产业工人经历伤害并可能生成生态意识。本文借鉴政治生态学与批判地理学，将身体概念化为环境伤害累积与解放潜能之间的辩证关系。叙事访谈表明，工人的身体既是伤害发生的场所，同时也承载着解放潜能。然而，这种解放潜能可能受到制约。这凸显出“就业与环境对立”的张力在实践中依然存在。


`U1` **Landlords and Tenants: A Class‐Theoretical Approach to the Relations of Social Reproduction**  
**房东与租户：社会再生产关系的阶级理论视角**  
*Danielle Kerrigan, David Wachsmuth*｜2026-10-27｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70228](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70228)

> **Abstract:** Current understandings of landlords and tenants are primarily based on individual attributes each group possesses, usually conceptualized as binary divisions or gradational distinctions. In this paper, we offer an alternative: a relational, class‐based concept of housing market actors. We argue much in the same way that a constitutive antagonism structures social relations with respect to control over the means of production, there is a constitutive antagonism that drives conflict over control over the means of social reproduction (specifically housing) and that by understanding this antagonism, we can better understand the political economy of housing and social reproduction. In the relational Marxian sense, landlords, tenants and homeowners are housing classes. After identifying the weaknesses in gradational approaches to landlords and tenants and laying out our relational alternative, we offer a set of examples which demonstrate the utility of employing a class‐based conceptualization of landlords and tenants.
>
> **摘要：** 当前对房东与租户的理解主要基于各群体所具有的个体属性，通常被概念化为二元划分或等级差异。本文提出一种替代方案：一种关系性的、基于阶级的住房市场行动者概念。我们认为，正如构成性对抗围绕生产资料的控制权构建社会关系一样，也存在一种构成性对抗，驱动着围绕社会再生产资料（具体而言即住房）控制权的冲突；通过理解这一对抗，我们能够更好地理解住房与社会再生产的政治经济学。在关系性的马克思主义意义上，房东、租户和自有住房者都是住房阶级。在指出房东与租户等级化研究路径的不足并阐述我们的关系性替代方案之后，本文提供了一组案例，展示采用基于阶级的房东与租户概念化方式的有效性。


`R2` **Labor, Life, and Leisure in the Global Factory: The Space–Times of Social Reproduction in Taiwan's Export‐Processing Zone and Mexico's Maquiladoras**  
**全球工厂中的劳动、生活与休闲：台湾出口加工区与墨西哥客户工厂（maquiladora）中社会再生产的时空**  
*Gabriel Antonio Solis, Nina Ebner*｜2026-10-21｜Vol. 58(6)｜[doi:10.1111/anti.70223](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70223)

> **Abstract:** The simultaneous creation of the first Economic Processing Zone in the Republic of China's Taiwan and the maquiladora in northern Mexico in the mid‐1960s were critical junctures in global capitalism's reorganization. Drawing on archival research and contemporary ethnographic fieldwork, this article traces the geographies and temporalities of social reproduction that powered these emergent export‐processing zones. Focused through the Kaohsiung Export Processing Zone and Ciudad Juárez, we look to state and factory‐mediated spaces and practices that managed workers' reproductive needs while simultaneously binding them ever tighter to the global factory's orbit. As we show, these “space‐times of reproduction” were key to Mexico and Taiwan's transformation into key nodes in global production networks in the 1960s and 70s. In turn, this South–South historical relational comparison demonstrates how social reproduction emerged as central to the global factory's restructuring more broadly, strengthening the labor control that is now emblematic of contemporary supply chain capitalism.
>
> **摘要：** 20世纪60年代中期，中华民国台湾首个出口加工区与墨西哥北部客户工厂（maquiladora）的同时设立，是全球资本主义重组过程中的关键节点。本文基于档案研究和当代民族志田野调查，追溯了驱动这些新兴出口加工区的社会再生产的地理与时间性。以高雄出口加工区和华雷斯城为焦点，我们考察了由国家和工厂所中介的空间与实践：它们在管理工人再生产需求的同时，也将工人越来越紧密地束缚于全球工厂的轨道之中。正如我们所展示的，这些“再生产的时空”是墨西哥和台湾在20世纪六七十年代转变为全球生产网络关键节点的核心所在。进而，这一南南历史关系性比较表明，社会再生产如何成为全球工厂更广泛重构的核心，强化了如今作为当代供应链资本主义标志的劳动控制。

`U1` **GENTRIFYING CAPITAL FROM WITHIN
                    : Rethinking Transnational Gentrification, Spatial Fix and Transnational Migration through Foreign, Urban and Rural Circuits in Kumasi, Ghana**  
**从内部士绅化的资本：通过加纳库马西的外国、城市与乡村资本循环重新思考跨国士绅化、空间修复与跨国移民**  
*Lewis Abedi Asante, Christian Narh Opata, Richmond Juvenile Ehwi, Zaid Abubakari et al.*｜2026-11-08｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70158](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70158)

> **Abstract:** In this article, we examine the rapid neighbourhood transformation of Ahodwo in Kumasi, Ghana, where Chinese entrepreneurial migrants have settled over two decades. We draw on qualitative fieldwork to advance arguments that extend the concepts of transnational gentrification, spatial fix, and transnational migration, and demonstrate that the capital driving neighbourhood change in Ahodwo operates through two intersecting circuits: a transnational one, involving the direct investment of Chinese surplus capital in urban businesses, and a domestic one, where profits from informal gold mining (galamsey) in rural and periurban areas are reinvested into urban businesses. This insight deviates from the assumption that transnational gentrification is primarily financed from overseas and brings into view rural–urban capital circuits within the global South. Gentrifying capital is therefore both exogenous and endogenous, interacting in complex ways. The process is also locally curated: local landlords and brokers facilitate the clustering of a foreign tenant class by tailoring commercial and residential spaces, financing neighbourhood security, and producing conditions for a transnational rent gap. These dynamics inflate rents, reprofile tenants, and generate varied displacement forms. Chinese migration in Ahodwo uniquely coproduces capital and urban transformation across foreign, urban, and rural settings, challenging conventional narratives of capital origins, circulation, and gentrification.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察加纳库马西阿霍德沃社区的快速转变，二十多年来中国创业移民陆续在此定居。基于质性田野调查，我们拓展了跨国士绅化、空间修复和跨国移民等概念，并表明推动阿霍德沃社区变迁的资本通过两条相互交织的循环运作：一条是跨国循环，涉及中国过剩资本对城市商业的直接投资；另一条是国内循环，即乡村和城市边缘地区非正规金矿开采（galamsey）的利润被再投资于城市商业。这一洞见偏离了“跨国士绅化主要由海外资本融资”的假设，并揭示了全球南方内部的城乡资本循环。因此，士绅化资本既是外生的，也是内生的，二者以复杂的方式相互作用。这一过程同样经过地方性的策划：本地房东和中介通过定制商业与居住空间、资助社区安保以及创造跨国租差的条件，促成外国租户阶层的集聚。这些动态推高了租金、重塑了租户构成，并产生了多种形式的驱逐。阿霍德沃的中国移民以独特方式在外国、城市与乡村情境中共同生产资本与城市转型，挑战了关于资本来源、流通和士绅化的传统叙事。


`R3` **NEGATIVE ASSETS
                    : Settler Colonialism, Speculative Urbanism, and the Moral Economy of Smallholding on Nairobi's Real Estate Frontier**  
**负资产：内罗毕房地产前沿上的定居者殖民主义、投机性城市主义与小农的道德经济**  
*Peter Lockwood, Smith Ouma, Tom Gillespie*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70154](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70154)

> **Abstract:** The expansion of Nairobi's real estate frontier into its peri‐urban hinterlands creates new opportunities and new dilemmas for smallholder families whose land is skyrocketing in value. Instead of taking the opportunity to become urban rentiers, many smallholders across Nairobi's peripheries are selling their inherited ‘ancestral’ plots in spite of strident taboos about the dangers of alienating patrimonial property. To explain why this is the case, we demonstrate how settler colonial histories of land reform created a demographic of smallholders that possessed land as a ‘negative asset’, never viable for long‐term commercial farming, and to which they became precariously rooted. While land's status as a commodity has been resisted despite titling, its rising value suggests that the commodification process is approaching thorough completion, with meagre plots sold rather than retained. Drawing on ethnographic cases of land sale, the article argues that the combination of settler colonial histories of land reform and the arrival of the real estate frontier works together to erode the moral economy of smallholding, forcing ‘ancestral’ land onto the market.
>
> **摘要：** 内罗毕房地产前沿向其城郊腹地的扩张，为土地价值飙升的小农家庭带来了新的机遇和新的困境。许多内罗毕周边地区的小农并未借机成为城市食利者，而是在出售其继承的“祖传”土地，尽管存在关于转让祖产之危险的严厉禁忌。为解释其原因，我们展示了定居者殖民时期的土地改革历史如何造就了这样一个小农群体：他们所拥有的土地是一种“负资产”，从未适合长期商业化耕作，而他们却不稳定地扎根于此。尽管土地确权后其商品地位一直受到抵制，但土地价值的上涨表明商品化进程正接近彻底完成，零星小块土地被出售而非保留。基于土地买卖的民族志案例，本文认为，定居者殖民主义的土地改革历史与房地产前沿的到来共同作用，侵蚀了小农的道德经济，迫使“祖传”土地进入市场。


`R3` **EMIGRE CITY
                    ‐
                    MAKING
                    : The Micropolitics of Diasporic Real Estate Investment and Urban Growth in Pakistan**  
**移民城市营造：巴基斯坦侨民房地产投资与城市增长的微观政治**  
*Fatima Tassadiq*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70156](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70156)

> **Abstract:** Contemporary cities in the global South are being reshaped through the intersecting logics of burgeoning real estate sectors and diasporic investment. This article examines the case of Pakistan to explore how emigration trajectories and remittance practices contribute to the transformation of urban landscapes in countries of origin through processes of emigre city‐making. Over the past few decades, the neoliberalizing real estate sector has aligned with shifts in the investment practices of elite emigrants, redirecting remittances from home‐building—once intended for eventual return—to speculative investments in urban real estate. These investments are part of broader, multi‐sited transnational strategies aimed at supporting permanent settlement in North Atlantic countries. Often made during presales, these speculative purchases and accompanying risk‐mitigation strategies help to create a niche market for vacant land, particularly in developments led by the military and its allies. These investments contribute to urbanization in major, second‐tier and emerging cities through low density suburban gated communities. By tracing the logics of such investments, this article sheds light on alternative trajectories of transnational urbanism and neoliberalization, illustrating how land and housing financialization unfolds in postcolonial cities through global flows of non‐corporate private capital, rather than institutional transnational capital and well‐developed financial markets.
>
> **摘要：** 当代全球南方城市正在房地产业蓬勃发展与侨民投资的交织逻辑下被重塑。本文以巴基斯坦为例，探讨移民轨迹与侨汇实践如何通过“移民城市营造”过程推动来源国城市景观的转变。过去几十年来，新自由主义化的房地产业与精英移民投资实践的转变相契合，使侨汇从曾以最终回国为目的的自建住房转向对城市房地产的投机性投资。这些投资是更广泛的多地点跨国策略的一部分，旨在支持其在北大西洋国家的永久定居。这些投机性购买常在预售阶段进行，连同相应的风险规避策略，共同催生了一个空置土地的利基市场，尤其集中于由军方及其盟友主导的开发项目中。这些投资通过低密度郊区封闭社区，推动了主要城市、二线城市和新兴城市的城市化。通过追溯此类投资的逻辑，本文揭示了跨国城市主义与新自由主义化的另类轨迹，阐明了在后殖民城市中，土地与住房金融化是如何通过非企业性私人资本的全球流动，而非通过机构性跨国资本和成熟的金融市场展开的。


`U2` **NAVIGATING UNCERTAINTY IN REGIONAL SOFT PLANNING
                    : The Interpretive Dynamics of Emerging Planning Practices**  
**驾驭区域软规划中的不确定性：新兴规划实践的诠释动态**  
*SABRINA FREDIN, THERESE ALDINGE*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70160](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70160)

> **Abstract:** This study investigates how uncertainty shapes the emergence and implementation of a new regional soft planning practice. While soft, non‐statutory planning has been widely discussed, less is known about how actors interpret these new regional planning practices and how uncertainty persists beyond formal adoption. The article addresses this gap by conceptualizing uncertainty as foundational, relational and temporal, and examining how these dimensions structure collaboration between a regional authority and its municipalities. Drawing on semi‐structured interviews with municipal and regional planners in Region Östergötland in Sweden, the analysis shows how unclear starting conditions and institutional ambiguity made it difficult for actors to understand the purpose and implications of the new regional planning practice. We demonstrate how uneven capacities between large and small municipalities translated interpretive openness into asymmetrical influence at first, how uncertainty was consciously used as a deliberate tactic to allow for more open discussions, and how uncertainty endured after adoption as actors struggled to relate the non‐binding strategy to statutory planning instruments and implementation responsibilities. We argue that uncertainty is not only a hindrance but a constitutive feature of regional soft planning, simultaneously enabling experimentation and risking reinforcing power asymmetries.
>
> **摘要：** 本研究探讨不确定性如何塑造一种新的区域软规划实践的出现与实施。尽管软性的、非法定的规划已被广泛讨论，但对于行动者如何诠释这些新的区域规划实践，以及不确定性如何在正式采纳之后持续存在，人们知之甚少。本文通过将不确定性概念化为基础性的、关系性的和时间性的，并考察这些维度如何构建区域当局与其所辖市镇之间的协作，来填补这一空白。基于对瑞典东约特兰省市镇和区域规划师的半结构化访谈，分析表明，不明确的初始条件和制度模糊性使行动者难以理解新区域规划实践的目的和影响。我们展示了大小市镇之间不均衡的能力如何在初期将诠释上的开放性转化为不对称的影响力，不确定性如何被有意识地用作一种允许更开放讨论的策略，以及在采纳之后，随着行动者难以将这一非约束性战略与法定规划工具和实施责任相衔接，不确定性如何持续存在。我们认为，不确定性不仅是一种障碍，更是区域软规划的构成性特征，它在促成实验的同时也有强化权力不对称的风险。


`U1` **HOUSING RETROFITS IN JUST TRANSITIONS
                    ? Emerging Contradictions in Catalonia, Spain**  
**公正转型中的住房改造？西班牙加泰罗尼亚正在显现的矛盾**  
*Melissa García‐Lamarca*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70159](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70159)

> **Abstract:** Efforts to decarbonize residential housing are growing globally. Following ambitious targets set by the European Commission's Renovation Wave and subsidized through Covid‐19 recovery funds, Spain's housing retrofit programme deployed EUR 3.4 billion in European grants to retrofit half a million homes between 2022 and 2026. This article uncovers emerging political, economic, environmental and social contradictions around expanding housing retrofits in Spain, with a focus on Catalonia, to explore how funds were disbursed and who stands to benefit. In considering the political economy of housing in just transitions, I draw from two dozen interviews in 2023 with a range of actors, meeting observations and grey literature to highlight contradictions emerging across five domains. These include the territorial distribution of funds, the universal nature of retrofit grants, the treatment of vulnerable populations, the scale of intervention and the nature of the housing retrofit sector. Together the contradictions largely illustrate how public retrofit funding has favoured increased capital circulation alongside reinforced social relations and ideologies of private property ownership and wealth accumulation. While the overall push is towards what I term a Spanish growth economy of housing repair, some contradictions in the five domains point to possibilities for a more transformative just housing transition.
>
> **摘要：** 住宅脱碳的努力正在全球范围内不断增长。在欧盟委员会“翻新浪潮”所设定的雄心勃勃目标的推动下，并借助新冠疫情复苏基金的资助，西班牙的住房改造计划在2022年至2026年间投入34亿欧元欧洲拨款，用于改造50万套住房。本文以加泰罗尼亚为重点，揭示西班牙扩大住房改造过程中正在显现的政治、经济、环境和社会矛盾，探讨资金如何拨付以及谁将从中受益。在考察公正转型中的住房政治经济学时，我基于2023年对各类行动者开展的二十余次访谈、会议观察以及灰色文献，揭示了在五个领域中显现的矛盾，包括：资金的地域分配、改造补助的普惠性质、对弱势群体的处置、干预的规模以及住房改造行业的性质。总体而言，这些矛盾在很大程度上说明了公共改造资金如何促进了资本循环的增加，同时强化了私有产权与财富积累的社会关系和意识形态。尽管总体趋势指向我所称的西班牙住房修缮增长经济，但五个领域中的某些矛盾也指向实现更具变革性的公正住房转型的可能性。


`S2` **TERRITORIAL VALORIZATION
                    : How Positive Stereotypes Advance Suburban Water Insecurity in Detroit**  
**地域增值：正面刻板印象如何加剧底特律郊区的用水不安全**  
*Dana Kornberg, Mackenzie Taradalsky*｜2026-11-06｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70161](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70161)

> **Abstract:** Suburbs historically enabled and protected white middle‐class wealth in the United States; however, their more recent diversification has included a rise in suburban poverty. This article examines these dynamics through the issue of household water access—a feature of rising suburban impoverishment that deserves greater attention. For two years, the first author participated in a Detroit‐area advocacy group, working together to conduct interviews with a hard‐to‐reach group of suburban residents struggling to afford their water bills. These 15 interviews reveal that water insecurity affects households even in the most affluent suburbs and that access to water assistance and affordability programs has been inadequate and variable. We explain these findings by developing the concept of territorial valorization —the relational counterpart of territorial stigma—to capture the phenomenon whereby certain areas may fail to acknowledge and address social problems in an effort to maintain their historically positive reputations. While territorial valorization has the power to attract investment and resources, we theorize that its positive stereotypes can have negative consequences for residents who fail to meet their demands.
>
> **摘要：** 在美国，郊区历来促成并保护了白人中产阶级的财富；然而，近来郊区的多元化也伴随着郊区贫困的上升。本文通过家庭用水获取问题考察这些动态——这是郊区日益贫困化中值得更多关注的一个特征。第一作者在两年间参与了底特律地区的一个倡导团体，共同对一个难以接触到的群体——难以负担水费的郊区居民——进行访谈。这15次访谈表明，即使在最富裕的郊区，用水不安全也影响着家庭，而获得用水援助和可负担性项目的途径既不充分又参差不齐。我们通过提出“地域增值”（territorial valorization）这一概念——地域污名的关系性对应物——来解释这些发现，用以描述某些地区为维持其历史上的正面声誉而未能承认和处理社会问题的现象。虽然地域增值具有吸引投资和资源的力量，但我们从理论上指出，其正面刻板印象可能会对无法满足这些期待的居民造成负面后果。


`S2` **SPACE AND POLITICAL PARTICIPATION IN POPULAR NEIGHBORHOODS
                    : Insights from the Chilean Urban Uprising**  
**平民街区中的空间与政治参与：来自智利城市起义的洞见**  
*Nicolás Angelcos, Valentina Abufhele*｜2026-11-02｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70152](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70152)

> **Abstract:** In this article we examine how space shapes the political dispositions of residents in marginalized neighborhoods and gave form to institutional and contentious participation during the 2019 urban uprising in Chile. Based on qualitative research in two neighborhoods on Santiago's outskirts, we reconstruct residents’ engagement with politics prior to and during the urban uprising. We use Lefebvre's relational concept of space in dialogue with Bourdieu's theory of habitus to understand how space (perceived, conceived, lived) interacts with individual resources, mainly cultural capital. This interaction shows that participation strategies during the revolt stemmed from political dispositions developed over time, having been shaped by the diverse and conflicting ways residents perceive, conceive, and live space. These strategies are also influenced by what Bourdieu terms residents’ ‘volume of capital’. Based on their political dispositions, residents of popular neighborhoods display a varied repertoire of political strategies. These strategies include institutional, collaborative, and oppositional participation, with the aim of resolving their daily material needs, affecting national politics, and challenging the neighborhood's reputation within the city's imagination.
>
> **摘要：** 本文考察空间如何塑造边缘化街区居民的政治倾向，并在2019年智利城市起义期间赋予制度性参与和抗争性参与以具体形态。基于在圣地亚哥郊区两个街区开展的质性研究，我们重构了居民在城市起义之前及期间的政治参与。我们将列斐伏尔关系性的空间概念与布迪厄的惯习理论相对话，以理解空间（感知的、构想的、生活的）如何与个体资源（主要是文化资本）相互作用。这种相互作用表明，起义期间的参与策略源于长期形成的政治倾向，而这些倾向是由居民感知、构想和生活空间的多样且相互冲突的方式所塑造的。这些策略还受到布迪厄所称的居民“资本总量”的影响。基于其政治倾向，平民街区的居民展现出多样化的政治策略组合，包括制度性参与、协作性参与和对抗性参与，其目的在于解决日常物质需求、影响国家政治，以及挑战街区在城市想象中的声誉。


`U3` **‘
                    PARTICIPATION IS WHAT WE DO EVERY DAY
                    ’: Unpacking the Meanings and Practices of Participation Addressing Mobility Justice in Rio de Janeiro and Porto Alegre, Brazil**  
**“参与就是我们每天在做的事”：解析巴西里约热内卢和阿雷格里港应对移动性正义的参与意涵与实践**  
*Aline Fernandes Barata, Sue Brownill*｜2026-10-29｜Online first 在线优先｜[doi:10.1111/1468-2427.70155](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70155)

> **Abstract:** Participation is a topic that has been widely debated in urban studies. Despite this, we argue in this paper that policies and academic work on mobility planning have paid little attention to the meanings attached to participation and its role in mobility justice. Building on the literature on spaces for participation, Southern theories and insurgent planning, this paper shows that participation has multiple and contested meanings, particularly when addressing mobility injustices in the global South. Based on case studies in Favela Santa Marta, Rio de Janeiro, and Vila Tronco, Porto Alegre, both in Brazil, we provide evidence of alternative meanings of participation and a range of participatory practices exercised by residents with, despite, and against the state. The everyday practices that flow from and interact with these meanings uphold the freedoms to move around, thrive, and dwell, challenging mobility planning and what we call ‘authorized participatory discourse’. Our findings show that while participation with the state is only one part of the story, particularly in contexts of informality and exclusion, the state's and residents’ responses are far from monolithic. We conclude by arguing that a focus on everyday practices can add new insights to the understanding of mobility justice and insurgent planning.
>
> **摘要：** 参与是城市研究中被广泛讨论的话题。尽管如此，本文认为，关于移动性规划的政策和学术研究很少关注参与所附带的意涵及其在移动性正义中的作用。本文借鉴关于参与空间、南方理论和叛逆规划的文献，表明参与具有多重且有争议的意涵，尤其是在全球南方应对移动性不公正时。基于巴西里约热内卢圣玛尔塔贫民窟和阿雷格里港通科村的案例研究，我们提供了关于参与的替代性意涵的证据，以及居民与国家合作、不顾国家和对抗国家而开展的一系列参与实践。由这些意涵产生并与之互动的日常实践，维护了出行、发展与栖居的自由，挑战了移动性规划以及我们所称的“授权参与话语”。我们的研究发现表明，与国家合作的参与只是故事的一部分，尤其是在非正规性与排斥的情境中，而国家和居民的回应远非铁板一块。最后我们认为，关注日常实践能够为理解移动性正义和叛逆规划带来新的洞见。
